pallāṇṭukaḷāka ulakiṉ uyarttuk kūṟum vaki, iv vulakiṉi yāṇṭa uyarntōṉākat tōṉṟiya kuṭiyiṟ piṟantavaṉē! malikaḷiyum kāṭukaḷiyum tamakku araṇākakkoṇṭa maṟaneṟi aracar palariyaḻippa, ataṉi taṉ valikaṇṭu añcit, tam nāṭukaḷi viṭṭakaṉṟaṉarāka; nāṭṭu nilaṅkaḷiyellām koṇṭa pērutaviyum, polantār viḷaṅkum mārpum uṭiya vaṭimpalam niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ vaḻittōṉṟalē! teṉṉavaṉāya irāvaṇaṉit tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉiṉravāṟu peyarttavar kiṭṭutaṟ kariya valimikka toṉ mutukaṭavuḷar. akattiyarāvar; avarkkup piṉṉar, cāṉṟōr tokiyuḷ oruvarāy vitteṇṇutaṟku mēviya cālpuṟṟa aṭippaṟṟōy! cīrcāṉṟa uyarnta nelliṉ peyarip peṟṟa pāliyūr eṉṉum cīriya ūri veṉṟukoṇṭa veṟṟimikkōy! nalamcāṉṟa pēraṇikalaṅkaḷi yellōrkkum koṭukkum vaḷḷaṉmi mikka palavākiya kuṭṭanāṭṭiṉar velkōvē. mutuveḷḷili yeṉṉum ūriluḷḷār aṉivarum toṉṟu moḻintu toḻil kēṭkum vaki, taliyālaṅkāṉa meṉṉumiṭattut taṉṉi yetirtta cēra cōḻarāya perunila maṉṉariruvar, titiyaṉ, eḻiṉi, erumiyūraṉ, iruṅkōvēṇmāṉ, porunaṉ eṉpārāya kuṟunila maṉṉarivar ākiya eḻuvariyum, leṉṉumāṟu kaṭitu parantu ceṉṟu, nāṭu muṟṟavum erivāykkup paṭumpaṭi veṉṟu, avar muracamuṅ kikkoṇṭu, veṟṟik kuṟiyākak kaḷavēḷviyum vēṭṭa kollum valimikka pukaḻ vēntē! koṟkiyūr vāḻnar virumpum poruna! teṉpuricikkaṇ vāḻum paratavarkkup pōrttoḻil ceyalvallavaṉē! pakivar nāṭṭit taṉṉāṭākkum poruṭṭu, ceyyappaṭṭa avaratu nāṭukaḷuṭ kōṭaṟpāṉmiyavāṉa pakutikaḷiṟ pala yāṇṭukaḷ taṅki, avaṟṟip paṇṭiyiṉ takuvaṉavaṟṟip purintu, vellum pōriṉum ciṟantu niṟkum kuricil!
kōvē! vaṭavēṅkaṭam, teṉkumari, kuṇakaṭal, kuṭakaṭal iv velliyiṭippaṭṭa nilattuḷḷōr tammuṭaṉ tiṟam pala kūṟit tam mēval kēṭkumāṟu ceytu, veṟṟiyōṭu ceṟintu naṭakkum koṟṟavarkkellām nī, kōṉākum taṉmi pūṇṭuḷḷāy. aṉṟiyum nī, oru poyyāl uyarnili yulakumuṟṟum āṇṭuḷ ḷōr nukarum amiḻtoṭu peṟa varuṅkālam amiyiṉum, ap poyyiṉi aṟavē kaṭintu meymmi cērnta naṭpuc cey taliyē yuṭiyāy. muḻaṅkuṅkaṭal elliyāka, iṭiyē parantu viḷaṅkum ulakattuḷḷārum, umparnāṭṭut tēvarum kūṭip pakivarāyt tiraṇṭu variṉum, ap pakivarkku añcip paṇintoḻukum pāṉmiyiṉ mēṟkoḷḷāy, teṉpula meṉappaṭum teṉṉāṭṭu malikaḷaṉittum niṟiyumvaki, vāṇaṉ eṉṉum cūraṉoruvaṉ vitta viḻuniti peṟal nēriṉum, nī, piṟarkūṟum paḻi namakku varuvatāka eṉṉum karuttuṭiyi yākāy. cīriya poruḷkaḷip piṟarkkuk koṭuttaliyē yuḷḷum niṉatu neñcōṭu pukaḻum virumpuvāyāka.
aṉṉāy! iṟṟippōtu niṉveṟṟiyāṟ peṟappaṭu  vatum, piṉṉar niṉvaḻit tōṉṟuvārkku uḷatāvatu māya koṟṟam, niṉ kulattiṟkuriyatum mēṟṟicik kaṭṭōṉṟuvatu māki, ellārāṉum toḻappaṭum paḻamicāṉṟa piṟittiṅkaḷ nāṭōṟum ciṟakkumāṟu pōla, avar tam vaḻivaḻiyāka mikuvatāka. niṉ pakivaratu ākkam, kīḻtticik kaṭṭōṉṟum niṟiyiruḷit tarum mati nāḷum tēyntoḻital pōl nāṭōṟum keṭuva tāka. niṉṉiṭattuṇṭākiya māyiyum iṉik keṭṭoḻivatāka. cēṭ pulameṅkum ceṉṟu viḷaṅkum niṉ cīriyapukaḻ orukālum keṭātu nilipeṟuvatākum.
iṉi, nī āḷum in naṉṉāṭu pulavar pāṭum pukaḻuṭiyatu itaṉ naṭuvaṇ, kāṇṭaṟkiṉiya ōviyattik kaṇṭāṟpōlum kāṭciyamintu viḷaṅkuvatu maturi yeṉṉum nalla ūr innakark kaṇ, nāḷaṅkāṭi, allaṅkāṭiyeṉum iru perum niyamaṅkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟuḷ nāḷaṅkāṭiyiṉ naṉantali yeḻum ōci,mēkaṅkaḷ paṭintu mukattalāṟ kuṟitalum yāṟukaṇ niṟintu pāytalāl mikutalumiṉṟikkariporutu oru taṉmiyatāy olikkum munnīriyoppa, palarum pōntu palakālum koḷḷutalāṟ kuṟitalum, palarum mēṉmēluṅ koṇṭu vara koṇṭuvara mikutalumiṉṟi oru taṉmittāy, kaṇam eṉappaṭuvatum, ēḻu nāḷkāṟum naṭattappaṭuvatumāya viḻāk kālattu vēṟōr iṭattillāta veṟṟi neruṅkum tirunāḷum, tīviṉi kaḻuvum tīviya nīrāṭṭum (tīrttamāṭal) taṉkaṭ koṇṭu ēḻā nāḷantiyil, kūṭuman nāṭṭumakkaḷ ceyyum pōrāra vāratti ottu icikkum. allaṅkāṭiyil mikuntu eḻutarum ōci, akaṉṟa ivvulakattup piṟanāṭṭu vaṇikar īṇṭuc ceyta pēraṇikalaṅkaḷik koṇṭupōtal kāraṇamākak kāṟṟāl untappaṭṭuvarum tam pāymarakkalam koṇarnta palavēṟuvakip paṭṭa paṇṭaṅkaḷip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷi liṟakkuṅkāl eḻuvatum iṟakkuvā riṭuvatumāṉa kalleṉṉum ōciyiyoppa niṟiya irimēyntu tam pārppukkaḷiyuḷḷik kūṭunōkki mālik kālattu mīḷum vēṟupal paṟaviyiṉ pēriciyi ottu imiḻum.
iṉi, avvūrkkaṇ, eḻunta ñāyiṟu, taṉ oḷḷiya cuṭart tiraḷiṉ ciṉamāya veppattip pakalellām parappip piṉṉart taṇintu, appoḻutiṉic cērakkoṇṭu kuṭamutaṟkuṉṟañ (attakiri) cērtalum, kalleṉṉum mālikkālam, nayanta kātalarkku eytum mammar nōykoṇṭu pukuntu nīṅkum. akkāli, mutal yāmamuṅ kaḻiyum, piṉṉar kaṅkul yāmam eṉappaṭum iraṇṭām yāmamum, kaṭavuḷ vaḻaṅkum kiyaṟu yāmamāya mūṉṟām yāmamum kaḻiya, iravukkālat tiṟutip pakutiyāya viṭiyal eytum.
avviṭiyalil, maṟiyōtal payiṉṟa antaṇar, ammaṟi yiṉuṭ kūṟappeṟum vaṇakkappakuti, vāḻttuppakutikaḷi āyntu ōtuvar; yāḻvallōr, tāḷavaṟutiyi iṉitu nuṉittu, narampiyum naṉku teṟittu marutappaṇṇicippar; kāḻōr (parikkārarkaḷ) kaṭuṅkaḷiṟukaṭkuk kavaḷa mūṭṭuvar; neṭuntērp puravikaḷ panti pantiyāy niṉṟu, pulluṇāt teviṭṭum; palvakip paṇṭaṅ kaḷum viṟkum kaṭikaḷil, viṟpōr meḻukutalic ceyvar; kaḷ viṟpōr, tam puḷitta kaḷḷiṉ viliyi vāṅkuvōrkku urippar; kaṟpuṭiya makaḷir iruḷ māyntu katirviriyum viṭiyal eytutalāl, atupōtu ceyaṟ pālāṉavaṟṟic ceytaṟku virumpi, tam kaṇkaḷi veṟiyōṭap paṇṇi, viḷaṅkum miṉṉoḻuṅki yokkum poṟcilampu, tam ceñcīṟaṭiyiṟ kiṭantu pulampap puṟam pōtuvar; avvamayam, tiṇ cuvarpaṭitta, avaruṟiyum nallillaṅkaḷiṉ kāḻoṉṟu katavaṅkaḷ, tiṟakkap paṭukiyil, pēroliceyyum; kaḷḷuṇṭu kaḷippōr, veṟiyāl, taṭṭamaḻalic coṟkaḷi (taṭipaṭṭu varum coṟkaḷ) vaḻaṅka, kaḷḷuṇṇum paḻañcerukkāḷar tam muḻakkattil accoṟkaḷ muṉṉiṉṟeḻum; niṉṟēttum iyalpiṉarāya cūtarkaḷ vāḻtta, iruntēttum mākatar tam maṉṉaṉ pukaḻi nuvaluvar; vitāḷikaḷ tattam tuṟikku uriyaṉavaṟṟi muṟiyē iṉitāka icippar; nāḻiki kūṟuvōr nāḻiki aḷantu colluvar; paḷḷiyeḻucci muracam orupuṭiyatira, ēṟukaḷ tammuḷ māṟupaṭṭu orupāl muḻaṅkum poṟimayirkkōḻikaḷ orucār vikaṟiyiyampa, orucār vaṉ ṭāḻṅkurukukaḷum, kāmuṟṟa aṉṉac cēvalum tattamakkuriya peṭikaḷi yaḻikkum; aṇimayilum tam ammeṉ peṭiyiṉi aḻippāṉ akavum; piṭiyoṭu kūṭiya peruṅkaḷiṟukaḷ muḻakkañceyya, karaṭi mutaliya valimikka vilaṅkukaḷ kūṭṭiluṟiyum vayappuliyōṭu kalantu muḻaṅkum. nīlavāṉattiṉ kaṇ miḷarum miṉṉiṉ nuṭakkamum māṇiḻiyu muṭiya māvuṇṭu kaḷippu mītūrnta makaḷir tam kaṇavaroṭu pulantaṉarāy aṟutteṟinta paruttavaṭamāya ārattiṟ corinta muttukaḷōṭu, poṉṉurukkum neruppuc cintiya nilam pōla māṇikkam, marakatam, poṉ, maṇi mutalāyiṉavum pacci pākkum, putumaṇal paranta muṟṟattil viḻuntu kiṭattalākiya avaṟṟiyum piṟa vāṭiya pūkkaḷiyum vaṇṭuntēṉum moyttu ārkkum.
aṉṟiyum, ivvūr, vaḷaṅkeḻumiya arumpaṇṭaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟōṭu, piṟartōḷ kuṟṟamām vakip perutta peruntōḷ maṟavar palarik keṭuppa avar puṟantantōṭuṅkāl iṭi pulamāya pōrkkaḷattu viṉiyōri yiḻantuniṟkum kōṭṭiyāṉi kaḷum, pakippulattuk kavarappaṭṭa pāymā puravikaḷum, kāy ciṉaṅkaṉalum taṟukaṇmi mikka kūḷi (vēṭṭuvar) pakivarūric cuṭaveḻum oḷiyil nirin tuṟiyum vīrarimāḷaveṭṭi ēnti yavēlē kōlākak koṇṭu ōṭṭikkoṇarnta āniriyum, akanāṭu cūḻaniṟkum muḻumutalaraṇaṅkaṭ kamittuḷḷa aṇaṅ kuṭiya katavukaḷum, nāṭōṟum tamakkuc celvamikumāṟu kiyāṟ ṟoḻutu vāyāl vāḻttip pakaṟkālattē piṟar tiṟiyākak koṇṭuvara vanta cīriyakalaṅkaḷum, attaṉmiya piṟavum kaṅkip pēriyāṟu āyiramukamākak kaṭaliṉkaṭ parantatupōlap parantu niṟṟalāl oru vakiyiṉum aḷantu muṭipukāṇṭal iyalāta takimi yuṭiyatāy, taṉ poṅkaḻakiṉik kāṇpāṉ poṉṉa karum vaṟitākap puttēḷir vanteytum pukaḻpaṭittatāy, nuṇṇiya aṟal paranta tuṟitōṟum palvakip pūkkaḷ ceṟinta taṇṭalikaḷ cūḻap peṟṟatāy neṭuṅkālam, aṭippaṭṭirunta perumpāṇar irukkiyum, maṇṇuḷḷa vaḷavu māḻnta nīlamaṇi pōlum nīr nirampiya kiṭaṅkum, viṇṇuṟa vōṅkiniṟkum palvakip paṭikaḷ amikkappeṟṟa puriciyum, mukiltavaḻum malipōl nivanta māṭaṅkaḷōṭu teyvamuṟiyum neṭunili kaḷum, neypalakālu miṭutalāṟ karukiṉa tiṇṇiya katavukoṇṭu viyiyāṟu iṭiviṭātu ōṭumāṟupōla māvum māntarum iṭiyaṟāta vāyilu muṭiyatu; nāṉmāṭattāl malinta pukaḻ kūṭum perumpatiyumākum.
innakarkkaṇ,
'ciṉitali maṇanta curumpupaṭu centī
 oṇpūm piṇṭi aviḻnta kāvila
 cuṭar poḻin tēṟiya viḷaṅkukatir ñāyiṟ(ṟu)
 ilaṅkukatir iḷaveyil tōṉṟi yaṉṉa
 tamaṉiyam vaḷiiya tāvil viḷaṅkiḻi
 nilamviḷak  kuṟuppa, mētakap polintu,
 mayilō raṉṉa cāyal, māviṉ
 taḷirē raṉṉa mēṉi, taḷirppuṟat (tu)
 īrkkiṉ arumpiya titaliyar, kūreyiṟ(ṟu)
 oṇkuḻi puṇariya vaṇṭāḻ kātil
 kaṭavuṭ kayat(tu) amaṉṟa cuṭaritaḻt tāmarit
 tātu paṭu perum pōtu puriyum vāṇmukat(tu)
 āytoṭi makaḷir'
poṟkalaṅkaḷil maṇanāṟum iṉiya pāṉatti yeṭuttu niṉakkuttaruvar. nī ataṉiyuṇṭu, tuyilkoṇṭu, piṉṉarc celvatti niṉintu iṉpuṟukiṉṟa paṟṟuḷḷam uṟakkatti yuṇarttutalāl, atu niṉittup piṉṉar iṉiteḻuntu aracark kuriya kaṭaṉkaḷik kaḻittuk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ceytalvēṇṭum. av vaḻipāṭṭi, vallōṉoruvaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu, eḻutik ki ceyyavumpaṭṭa oru pāviyiṉiṭattu teyvattaṉmi nikaḻntatupōlum vaṭivuṭiyaṉāy nī viḷaṅkuvāyāka.
piṉṉar, naṟumaṇaṅkamaḻum cantaṉam pūcik katirviṭum muttu vaṭam tavaḻntu miḷirum niṉ akaṉṟamārpil, tēṉiṉamum piṟamūcuvaṉavum moyppa, kaḻuttiṉiṉṟun tāḻnta pūkkaḷ pala viraviya māliyum uṭaṉ kiṭantu tavaḻa, valikeḻumiya niṉ taṭakkiyiṟ poṟṟoṭiyum, polañ ceyappolinta maṇikaḷ aḻuttiya mōtiraṅkaḷum cuṭarviṭṭu viḷaṅka, cōṟamivuṟṟa nīruṭik kaliṅkam oṉṟu (kañciyiṭṭup pāleṉa veḷukkac cey uṭi), mēlaṇiyap peṟum kalaṅkaḷāṟ polivumikum vaki yoḻuṅkupeṟa vuṭuttu, mikkuvarukiṉṟavōr yāṟṟu nīrp perukkik kallaṇi yoṉṟu niṉṟu tāṅkiṉāloppat tam paṭiyik keṭuttu mēlvarukiṉṟa pakivar paṭip peruk kiṉi naṭuniṉṟu takintu, avariyum aṟavē keṭutta pōrē virumpum paṭit talivarkaḷ vāḷveṟṟi poruntiya niṉtāḷ veṟṟiyi vāḻtta, oṉṟā vulakattil ōṅku pukaḻē oṉṟum vaṇṇam niṟuviya māṟaṉ *mutalākap pakivaṉ veṉṟu kollum tappāta vāḷēntiya iḷaṅkōcar īṟākap palarum cūḻvara, yāvarum pukaḻum pēraṇikalam pūṇṭa aimperum kēḷirumuṭpaṭa, maṟamikka kuṟunila maṉṉar palarum neṟi marapu vaḻāta niṉ vāymoḻi kēṭṭu ataṟkut takaniṟpa, poṟpuṭi pukaḻ viḷaṅkum aṟaṅ kūṟaviyat tār neruṅki, niṉ aṟaviyalpip pukaḻntētti, vāḻtta, "maṟavar, celvar, kuricilar, ūkkalar mutaliyōriyum, peruñ cey yāṭavariyum tammiṉ; ēṉōrum tammiṉ" eṉac cilari varintu kūṟiyum, vāyiliṉ kaṇ takiyāmal ivarpōlvārum paṭiyāḷar mutaliyōrum 'varuka' eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiyum, kāṭcikkeḷiyaṉāy nāḷōlakkamirukkum pērakattiṉkaṇ vīṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
atukāli, 'pulavar, pāṇar, pāṭṭiyar, vayiriyar mutalā yiṉār varuka' eṉa aṉpukaṉiya vaḻittu, avar tam cuṟṟattārāy, avarkaḷāṟ pōṟṟappaṭupavarāy varum pēriravalar aṉi vōrkkum nīr yāreṉṟum avarkaḷik kēḷātu avar kāṭṭiṉa aḷavikkoṇṭu, koṭiñci neṭuntērkaḷ yāṉi yoṭuṅ koṭuttu, vēṟṟup pulattiliruntu koṇarappaṭṭa arum poruḷkaḷi yāvarkkumaḷittu, ataṉūṭē, nāṭṭil vāḻum kuṭimakkaḷip perukkip purattal vēṇṭumātaliṉ, naṟ poruṇūāṟ kaḷik kacaṭaṟakkaṟṟut teḷivāyāka! kaṟṟavaṟiviṉāl, niṉpukaḻē evvulakattum niṉṟu nilavappaṇṇuvāyāka! maṇṭilamākkaḷ, taṇṭattalivar mutalāya cuṟṟattārkku naṭuvē, munnīr nāppaṇ tōṉṟum ñāyiṟu pōlavum, palakalikaḷik kaṟṟōriṭip paṉmīṉ naṭuvaṇ viḷaṅkum tiṅkaḷ pōlavum, nī polivukūr cuṟṟattōṭu polintu iṉitu vāḻvāyāka!
iṅṅaṉam, tampukaḻ eṅṅaṇum paravum vaki malartali yulakiṉit tammēvaliṉ naṭatti, makkaṭkuriya maṉaṉuṇarviṉmi pāl piṟappaṟa muyalātu payaṉiṉṟi māṇṭōr, kariyip porutu iraṅkik kaṉiyum irumunnīrttiriyiṭum nuṇmaṇa liṉum palarāvar. appayaṉiṉmiyālē nīyum avvāṟu kaḻiyalākātu eṉṟu ivvāḻviṟ peritāyiruppatōr poruḷi yāṉ kūṟuvēṉ; āyiṉum, aṇṇalē! aḵtu eṉṉāl eḷitiṟ kāṭṭalaritu. ataṉi, ellāvulakiyum taṉ cevvāyiṟṟantu kāṭṭiya māyōṉpōṉṟa, nalla yākaṅkaṭkuk kūṟiya tuṟi yaṉittum muṉṉarē muṭittuviṭṭa tollāṇi nallāciriya rām tattuvavuṇarvāṉ meypporuḷuṇarntu vīṭṭiṉpa nukarccikāṇum āciriyar palar piṉpu kūṭiṉa kantaḻiyāy koḷḷi yi  avariṭattē peṟṟu nukarnta pukaḻcāl ciṟappiṉaṉ, palyāka cāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti yoppa nīyum nallāciri yariṭattē kēṭpāyāka! kēṭṭu, nī, avvāciriyariṭattup peṟum kantaḻiyiṉ viyappum, piṉpu nī peṟṟamiyum amitiyum, pukaraṟu ciṟappiṉavāy eytum niṉ talimikaḷ yāvum cāṉṟōr palarāl tam maviyiṟ collappaṭṭu viḷaṅkum peruma! nallūḻiyuḷ "ittuṇikkālam irutti" eṉap pālvari teyvattāl variyāka aṟutiyiṭappeṟum nāḷ muḻuvatum nī iṉitu pēriṉpam nukarntiruppāyāka! ataṉi nukarātu aimpoṟikaṭkum muṉṉiṟkap paṭuvaṉavāya innukar poruḷkaḷōṭu niṉakku eṉṉavuṟa vuṇṭu! niṉṉiṭattuṇṭāya māyi iṉik keṭuvatāka!! aṇṇalē! nī neṭitu vāḻka!!!
 2. maturic ciṟappu
maturikkāñci yeṉṉum iccīriya pāṭṭiṉ perum pāṉmiyāṉa pakutikaḷ maturiyiṉ ciṟappiyum, ataṉic cūḻavuḷḷa nāṉilaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷaṉiyum, ataṉit talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭa maṉṉaratu āṭci nalaṉiyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ākaliṉ avaṟṟimaṭṭil ivvārāycciyiṉ ippakutiyil veḷiyiṭukiṉṟēṉ.
1.maturiyāṇṭa maṉṉaratu āṭci nalaṉ: ivvulakam mikka parappum uyariyamuṭikaḷ koṇṭa malikaḷum taṉpāṟ koṇṭatu. mali muṭikaḷil tēṉiṟāl tūṅkum māṇpum, yāṟukaḷ tōṉṟip paṭarum ciṟappum, kāṉañceṟintu niṟkum kaviṉum kāṇpār kaṭpulaṉik kavarum kāṭciyiṉavām. ikkāṭciyaminta ulakiṉi, ippāṭṭuṭittalivaṉāṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ āṭcipurintu purantu varunāḷ, kāṟṟu valamāka vicumpiṉkaṇ cuḻaṉṟu tiriya, ataṉāl  maḻi vēṇṭuṅkālatut tappātu peytu utava, nāṟṟiciyum viḷi nalaṉum piṟavum mikipaṭappeṟṟaṉa; toṭuppiṉ, vititta oṉṟu āyiramāka viḷiyum nilavaḷañciṟantatu; viḷinilaṉum maraṅkaḷum tampālām payaṉip palluyirkkuṅ koṭuttali mēṟkoṇṭu, taḻittaṉa; aṅkaṇ vicumpil, āṅkāṅ kumiḷirum nāṇmīṉ tattam nilitiriyātu koṭpuṟ ṟoḻukiṉa; ceññāyiṟu pakaṟpōtu ceytum, veṇṭiṅkaḷ iravu ceytum, mitīrntu kiḷarntu viḷaṅkiṉa; makkaṭku nōytaruvaṉavāya paciyum piṇiyum ilavāki nīṅka, nōkku viḷaṅkum vaciyum vaḷaṉum perukiṉa; eṇṭiciyiṉum iruntu ulakiṉpoṟi yēṟkum vayakkaḷiṟukaḷ aṟavum polivupeṟṟu, vaḷarcciyum valiyum oruṅkuvāyppa, ulakāḷ maṉṉaratu ceṅkōṉmi yāṉ, appoṟiyiṉ varuttam pēṇātu mēmpāṭupeṟṟaṉa.
innalaṅkaḷittarum āṭcikkuk kaṇṇeṉa vakutta māntar vāymoḻiyāl pukaḻ niṟintavar, poyyaṟiyāp polivum peṟṟavar; ivar vāḻunterukkaḷ palarōṭiruntu palanāḷuṇṇiṉum taṇṭāta mikuvaḷaṉum irumaruṅkum maḻiyeṉa maruḷum māṭampiṟaṅkutalāl, kāṇpavar kaṇṭu taṇṭāk kāṭciyum peṟṟaṉavām. ivarkaḷ amiccut toḻilpūṇṭu āṭcit tuṟiyil utaviceyya, pala nallūḻikaḷ tamakku aṭippaṭṭu naṭakka, ivvāṭcinalaṉip pala veḷḷam eṉum eṇṇiyaṟ peyarik koṇṭa kālamellām makkaḷ mīkkūṟa, ippāṭṭuṭit talivaṉatu muṉṉōr ulakiṉi ōṅkutiri yolikkum munnīrē varampāka niṟka āṇṭaṉar eṉpatu.
2. vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ: ivaṉ "nilantanta pērutavip polantārmārpiṉ neṭiyōṉ" eṉac ciṟappikkap peṟukiṉṟāṉ. peṟavē, ivaṉ, nāṭṭiluḷḷa ēṉiyaracar palarum taṉ tuṉṉarun tuppiṉikaṇṭu añcit tam nāṭaṉittum viṭṭuakaṉṟārāka, *avaṟṟik koṇṭu taṉ kīḻvāḻvārkkuk koṭukkum vaḷḷaṉmiyum, pōrvaṉmiyum taliciṟantavaṉ eṉpatu peṟalākum. eṉiṉum ivaṉ, viḻumiya vaḻiyum, viḷaṅkiya ōṭiyum, kaṭuñciṉamum, kaṭām kamaḻutalāl aḷaṟupaṭṭa ceṉṉiyum, varimaruḷum tōṟṟamumuṭiya viṉinaviṉṟa pōr yāṉikaḷ pala ciṉañciṟantu, pōrkkaḷattu vīrarikkoṉṟu tirintuḻakkavum, kutirittiraḷ pakippulanōkkip pāytara, āṇṭeḻum centukaḷ akalvāṉattu veyili maṟippavum, varavum parikaḷ pala piṇitta tiṇṇiya tērkaḷ kāṟṟeṉṉa virintu koṭpuṟavum, vāṭpōriṉ mikka maṟaṅkoṇṭu tiriyum mintar palar tōṇmuṟiyāl vīṟumuṟṟavum, iruperuvēntarāya cēracōḻaroṭu vēḷir palarum cāyntōṭap porutu, avaric ceruveṉṟum amiyāmal, uyarntilaṅkum aruvikaṉmikka mivariyulakattu maṉṉar palariyum veṉṟuyarntu, taṉṉoṭu māṟukoṇṭa paki maṉṉar tam nāṭukaḷip palavākac citaṟuṇṇap piḷanta ciṟappum mikkavaṉ eṉpatu pōtantatu.
3. maturiyiṉ vaḷam
nāṭṭuc ciṟappu:- vāṉam kuṇakaṭaliṉ nīri mukantu ceṉṟu kuṭamalivariyilmuṟṟi, avvariccāraliṉ vaḷarntōṅkum kaḻikaḷ nili taḷaravum, vāḻum kaḷiṟṟiṉamum añci alamaravum, urumēṟṟōṭu parantu, iravum pakalum oḻiviṉṟip perumaḻi peytatāka, āṅkāṅku, kallappaṭṭirukkum kavalikkiḻaṅkiṉ aḻakiya kuḻumpukaḷil (kuḻikaḷil) nīr niṟintu mikutippaṭṭu aruviyeṉa vaḻintōṭa, innīrmikutiyit tāṅkātu tiraṇṭa puṉal nīttam, cenniṟaṅkoṇṭu, kuṇakaṭal nōkkip parantu cellum, avvaḻi, iṭiyiluṟum ēri kuḷam mutaliyaṉa nīr nirampap peṟa, avaṟṟiṟkīḻ vayaṅkum kaḷiṟu niṟpiṉum maṟikkum uyarccicāṉṟa katirviḷiyum cenneṟkaḻaṉikaḷum, oḷirum ilañcikaḷum (maṭukkaḷ) nīr vaḷam mēmpaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum. aṭiyum, muṭṭāḷum ko ṇṭatāmari, kaṭkamaḻum neytal, vaḷḷitaḻ aviḻum nīlam, vaṇṭiṉam paṭiyum mellili koṇṭa āmpal mutaliyaṉavum, maṟṟum piṟa curumpiṉam iṟikoḷḷum pūkkaḷum niṟintu maṇaṅkamaḻntu, tēṉārnta curumpāṭat tikaḻkiṉṟa poykiyiṉkaṇṇum, iṭiyiṭiyē paṭarntu niṟkum vaḷḷik koṭikaḷit taḷḷi, vayamuṟṟa mīṉkaḷi valiñarkaḷ tam valikaḷāṟ piṭittuk koṉṟu kuvippar. atupōtu, avvaḷḷik koṭikaḷiṟṟaṅki yuṟaṅkiyirunta kampuṭ kōḻi paṟantōṭuṅkāl eḻum ōciyum, koṭumuṭikaḷ mikka mīṉvali vīcum valiñar, ammīṉkaṭku vilikūṟik kaḷikkum ōciyum orupakkam eḻuntu ciṟakkum. orupāl, karumpukaḷi āṭṭiyaṭum entiraccāliyiṉōciyum, vēṟorucār kaḷip paṟippōr ceyyum ōciyum, avvayalarukē, aḷḷaṟkaṇ valiyaṟṟu vīḻnta mutumimikka pakaṭukaḷi, eḻupputaṟkāka, kaḷḷuṇṭakaḷamar, avaṟṟikku uṟṟaviḻumam teriyātavāṟu peyarkkum āravāramum ōṅkum. taḻitta pakaṉṟi mutaliyaṉa mikkuṟum kaḻaṉikaḷil vittiya nel muṟṟiyatāka, ataṉi aṟuttaṟkaṇ, avvaṉkiviṉiñar ceyyum ōciyum, iṉkural toṭutta vācciyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ maḻivaḷaṉaṟāt taṇṇiya paraṅ kuṉṟattil naṭipeṟum viḻākkaḷil eḻum nallōciyum, putunīr viḻāvayartaṟ poruṭṭup pērāravārattuṭaṉ cellum makaḷir tam kōtiyi mintar puṉiyavum, ammintariṉ taṇṇiya tārit tām puṉiyavumākak kūṭiyāṭum nīrāṭṭōciyu mākaveḻum ivvōcikaḷōṭu, kurukukaḷ mēlē cūḻa, vīṭukaḷiṉaruku niṟkum maraṅkaṭōṟum; niriyē kōttu uṇakkap peṟum vaṟṟaṉ mīṉkaḷ aciyum pāṇarkuṭiyiruppuk kaḷil, avar pāṭalāl eḻum ōci muṟṟum kalantu, viṇpaṭarntu, āṇṭuvāḻvārkku iṉiya iciyiṉpam nalkum. itu maruta nilavaḷaṉ.
iṉi, mulli nilavaḷaṉ varumāṟu: itu kāṭum, kāṭu cārnta nilamum poruntiyatu. īṇṭu, kāṉavar orupakkal niṉṟu tiṉikoyvar. orucār eḷḷiḷaṅkāy muṟṟiniṟpa, iruntāḷ varaku taṉ muṟṟiya katirkāṭṭi aḻakuceyya, āḻnta viḻikaḷil maṇikaḷ kiṭantu miḷira, pēraḻaku vāynta navvimāṉkaḷ eḻun tōṅkiya akkāṭṭiṉkaṇ, maṭappam poruntum kaṇṇiṉi yuṭiya piṇimāṉōṭu, tam ciṟutaliyiyuyartti pāṭiyāṭiyukaḷum. cempoṟṟukaḷ, parantamaṇaliṉ mēṟpuṟattē yaṭintu nalloḷi parappum. parappivittāṟ pōlum pāṉmiyavāyp pāṟikaḷ, oḷḷiya koṉṟi paranta nīḻalil, iṉiya kāṭciyiṉi nalkum. nīlamaṇiyaṉṉa pacumpayirkaḷiṉ mēl murukkuṇṭa mucuṇṭiyiṉ oḷḷarumpukaḷōṭu, mulliyiṉ veḷḷiyotta voṇpūkka ḷutirntu parantu, aḻaku ceyya, avaṟṟiṉarukē, vallōṉ oruvaṉ tiiya (eḻutiya kūttayar veṟikkaḷamoppa, melliya avalkaḷil teḷinta nīr niṟintu, nīlamaṇiyeṉat tikaḻum neytal, iṭi maṇan toyyiṟ koṭiyoṭu māṟupaṭa malarntu vayaṅkum.
iṉic cēyōṉ mēya mivari yulakameṉappaṭum kuṟiñci yil, variccāralkaḷiṟ kiṭakkum perum pāṟikaḷ, veḷḷaruvikaḷ pala vīḻtalālum, nīrttārikaḷiṉ uṟippālum karuniṟaṅ koṇṭu añcuvaru kāṭcipayappa, iṭiyiṭi ceḻippuṟṟut taḻitta piṅkūḻum piṟavum āmā mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum pulluṇā vaḷikkum poṟpu viḷaṅkum. āṅkāṅkuḷḷa tiṉip puṉaṅkaḷiṟ paṭiyum kiḷimutaliya puṭkaḷik kāṉavar oppum ōciyum, tuṭavikaḷiṟ ceḻittu niṉṟu, palamaṇikaḷiyokkum aḻakiya pūkkaḷ niṟintu viḷaṅkum avariyiṉ naṟuntaḷiri mēyavarum avvāmā mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟavarkaḷ kaṭitalāleḻum ōciyum, malimiciyuṟiyum akkuṟicci makkaḷ poykkuḻitoṭṭuk kēḻal mutaliyavaṟṟip piṭittaṟ poruṭṭu, ataṉ vāyi mēlāka mūṭivippa, avvaḻivantakēḻal ataṉkaṇ vīḻtalum, ataṉik kollum veṟṟiyāka ceyyum āravāramum, karuṅkāl vēṅkiyiṉ naṟumpūk koṭiyum kuṟamakaḷir 'pulipuli' eṉṟu kūṟiyāṭum ōciyum, im maliccāralkaḷil māṟimāṟi yolik kum. aṉṟiyum iccāraṅkaḷ, kuṟuṅkatirtāṅkum tēriyum, neṭuṅkāliyuṭiya aiyaviyum, aivaṉameṉṉum veṇṇellu mākiya viḷinalam ciṟantu iñci, mañcaḷ, miḷaku mutaliyaṉavum piṟavumāya paṇṭaṅkaḷ pala mikuntu viḷaṅkum.
 iṉi, aruvikaḷum, avaṟṟāluḷatā maḻakumillāta pala malikaḷ āḻnta viṭākaṅkaḷ koṇṭuniṟpatu pāli. niṟin teḻum cūṟikkāṟṟu, ūkampul mutaliyaṉa ularntu kiṭakkum kāṭṭuḷ, vikkōlic cāṭuvatupōl avaṟṟic cāṭiyalittu avviṭākaṅkaḷiṉ mōticcērttu aṇittu aṭaṅkum kāṟṟu mōta, alipaṭuṅkaṭaliṉkaṇ eḻum pērōci yotta peru muḻakkam accūṟāvaḷiyiṉ payaṉāka yeḻum. innilattuk kāyum veyil, niḻalumtaṉ uruviḻakkun taṉmittāya vēṉil veppa muṭiyatu. ikkālattil, maliyaruku kiṭakkum curattiṉ kaṇ, vēṭṭuvaviḷiñar palar ciliyēnti āṟalikkum kaḷvarik kāttaṟ poruṭṭuk karantuṟivar. ivarkaḷuṟiyum maṉiyakaṅkaḷ taḻi vēyappaṭṭum, ivaratu paṭukkikaḷ māṉtōl parappap peṟṟum irukkum. potuvāka, ivarkaḷ taḻi viraviya kaṇṇi cūṭumiyalpum, kaṭuñcoṟ kūṟum kāyciṉamumuṭiyar.
iṉik kaṭaṟcārntatāya neytal vaḷaṉ varumāṟu: kaṭaṟṟuṟi kaḷil, perunīrōccum marakkalamīkāmar palaruṭaṉ kūṭi, iṭama kaṉṟa yavaṉam mutaliya piṟanāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu koṇarnta kutirikaḷuṭaṉ, muḻaṅkuṅ kaṭaltanta viḷaṅkukatir muttamum, aram pōḻntaṟutta ilaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷikaḷum, paratar (ceṭṭikaḷ) mutaliyōr koṇṭutanta palavēṟuvakik kūlaṅkaḷum orupuṭi maliyum kaṭalumeṉa mikkuttōṉṟa, vēṟōr pakkattil, iruṅkaḻikaḷil viḷinta veḷḷuppum, karuppukkaṭṭiyoṭu kūṭṭip porikkap paṭṭa puḷiyum, parantōṅkiya kāṉalil vaṉkittimilar (mīṉ piṭikkum paṭaku uṭiyavarkaḷ) tām koṇarnta koḻumīṉ kaḷit tuṭiyiṉ kaṇpōl uruṇṭa tuṇṭukaḷāka aṟutta uṇakkiya karuvāṭukaḷum ēṟṟappaṭṭa nāvāykaḷ pala niṉṟu aciyum polivu viḷaṅkum. iṅṅaṉam, ivi nāṭōṟum muṟiyē mikum peruvaḷam payiṉṟatu in neytal.
ivvaṇṇam aintiṇivaḷaṉum amintu aḻakupeṟṟu, muḻavu muḻaṅkun tirunāḷ naṭipeṟum neṭunterukkaḷum, tuṇaṅki, kuravi mutaliya kūttukkaḷ nikaḻum parattiyar cēriyum iṭiyaṟāp putuvaruvāyum koṇṭa pala ūrkaḷum, avaṟṟuṭ pala kuṭittiraḷum mikuntiruttalāl, ippāṇṭināṭu pāṭal cāṉṟa paṇpuṭiya nāṭāyiṟṟu.
nakaracciṟappu: itu perumpā ṇirukkiyum, maṇi nīrk kiṭaṅkum, palpaṭippuriciyum viyipōlum vaḻakkuṭiya vāyilumuṭiyatu. ivaṟṟuṭ perumpāṇirukkiyiṉ nalam.
perumpā ṇirukkiyāvatu perumpāṇar eṉṉum oru vakiccātiyār vāḻum iṭam. itu, viyittuṟitōṟum palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa pūntōṭṭaṅkaḷ cūḻappeṟṟu avaṟṟi ṉiṭiyil neṭuṅkālamāka iruntuvarum toṉmi ciṟantatu. aṉṟiyum itu, yāṟṟiṉ aṭikariyiliruttalāl kāvum poḻilum mikku, uyar cimayattu niṉṟu iyaṅkivarum nīr koḻippa varum nuṇṇiya veṇmaṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷ tammēṟ paṭinta pūkkaḷāl naṟumaṇam vīca, muḻaṅkukāl porutalāl māvicumpu taṭavum maraṅkaḷaciya, mayilkaḷ akavi āravārippa, mucukkalikaḷ tāvum komparil avaṟṟiṉ mantikaḷ viḷiyāṭṭayarum peruñ ciṟappuṭiyatu. kariyiṉiyalpu iṟṟāka, yāṟṟu nīroḻukku, tātu cūḻnta kōṅkam pūvum vēṟu piṟa malarkaḷum parantu, orukōti (pūmāli) yoppa oḻuki yaḻaku ceyyum.
maṇinīrk kiṭaṅku: innūṟporuḷ nikaḻnta kālattukku muṉṉar māṉaviṟal vēḷ eṉṉumoru kuṟunilamaṉṉaṉ aḻum pil eṉṉum ūruṭiyaṉāy viḷaṅkiṉaṉ. avvūr nila vaḷamum poruḷ vaḷamum mikuntatu. itaṉ vaḷattik kāṇappukum oruvaṉatu kāṭci vēṟoru nilattiyum viḷinalaṉiyum kāṇṭaṟkuc cellāteṉpar. ittakiya aḻumpili yotta nāṭiḻantavarkaḷum, celvaṅ koḻutta ceḻumpatikaḷiṭattavāya kuṭiyiḻantavarkaḷumāyt toṉṟutoṭṭuvarum ceṟṟaṅkalanta valimi tammit tūṇṭutalāl etirppāṉ vanta aṇṇal yāṉiyum aṭupōrt toḻilumuṭiya vēntarpalar uḻiñip pōrceytaṟku vantārāka, avar neñciṟ kiṭanta māṟupā ṭaṉittiyum pōkki, tām tam iṉṉicimuracu mutaliyavaṟṟi iṭippulattē viṭṭōṭac ceytu avar mutuku kāṇum vakiyiṟ kāttuniṟkum mikka āḻamuṭiyatu akkiṭaṅku.
vāyil: maḻitavaḻum maliyeṉa uyarntōṅkiya māṭaṅ kaḷāṟṟikaḻntu, mikka mutumiyum valimiyuṅ koṇṭu niṟkum innakarkkaṇvāyil, aṇaṅku vāḻtaralāl aiyaviyum neyyum appiya neṭuniliyum, tiṇṇiya katavaṅkaḷum uṭiyatāy, iṭiyaṟā nīrpperukkiṉik koṇṭu ōṭum viyiyāṟṟiṉip pōla māntarum māvum iṭiyaṟātu vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkiṉi yuṭiyatāy viḷaṅkum.
iṅṅaṉam vaḻakkiṭiyaṟātu viḷaṅkum vāyil kaṭantu uḷḷakam nōkkuvārkkuk kaṭpulaṉāvatu yāṟu kiṭantaṉṉa akaṉṟa neṭunteruvēyākum. ataṉ irumaruṅkum, maṇṭapam, kūṭam, tāykkaṭṭu, aṭukkaḷi mutaliyaṉavākap palavēṟu vakikaḷ viraviya māṭaṅkaḷ vāṉakam mūḻki niṉṟu uyarcci kāṭṭa, avaṟṟiṉ cāḷaraṅkaḷ, ammēṭiyilē vīcukiṉṟa melliyateṉṟali varavaḻikkum. teruviṉkaṇ, viḻāvayartal virumpi nakar mākkaḷ eṭutta pērōci orupuṭiyeḻuntu atira, avvōci kēṭṭu makiḻum vēṟoru cārār pala iṉṉiyam iyampuvar. avaṟṟiṉ ṟiraṇṭavōci nīrāṭṭiṭiyeḻum muḻakkatti yokkum. niṟka, immāṭaṅkaḷiṉ mēṉili yiṟṟikaḻum neṭuṅkoṭikaḷum, viḻā virumpiyeṭutta viyaṉ koṭikaḷum, vēṟṟu vēntari vēṟalāleṭutta nalam peṟu koṭikaḷum, nīr olittatupōla mikkolittuvanta vēṟ paṭiyip pulavuppaṭak koṉṟu, uṭaṉ mēlneruṅkiya yāṉip paṭiyiyum aṟavē cāyttup pukaḻceytu vīṟuṟṟu eṭutta viṟalcāṉṟa naṉkoṭikaḷum, peruvari maruṅkiṉ vīḻum aruvi pōl nuṭaṅkittōṉṟa, kaḷviṟpō reṭuttakoṭi yeḻuntu aciyum kāṭci, anniyamatti naṉku aṇiceyyum.
iṉi, anniyamaṅkaḷuḷ, kantu nīttu uḻitarum kaṭām poḻiyāṉi, kāleṉakkaṭukkum tēr, iṭumayirppuravi, kaḷḷār kaḷamar uṭpaṭa nālvakittāya paṭitiraṇṭu muṟiyē cella, naṟumaṇam kamaḻum pūkkaḷ pala pūntaṭṭukkaḷiṟ koṇṭu niṟpavarum, oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu māṟupaṭum vakiyiṟ palapaṭaviritta mālikaḷi ēnti niṟpavarum, navamaṇikaḷ, poṉ, cantaṉam, karuppūram mutaliyavaṟṟic cēra iṭittut tukaḷceytu, pūntātu oppat tūymippaṭuttiya cuṇṇatti uṭiyavarum, paciya karuṅkāli yic cīvik kāycci yeṭutta kaḷippoṭu, iṉiya nīrmikoṇṭa pākkukkaḷik kalantukoṇṭu, avaṟṟoṭu veṟṟiliyum cēravittu viṟpavarum, caṅkic cuṭuṅkāṟ peṟalākum cuṇṇāmpu uṭiyavarumākiya palavēṟu vakippaṭṭa makkaḷ, irumaruṅkum vantu kalanta pakippulam pōṉṟu viḷaṅkutalāl, tam iṉṉuyirkku añci, veytuyirttu, ēṅkiyiruntu, piṉṉar annāṟpaṭiyum nīṅka, māṭa nīḻalilē pala iṉṉiyaṅkaḷ kaṟaṅkac cemmāntiruppar.
niṟka, maṉiyakaṅkaḷil maṭappamum nōkkiṉāri varuttum ceyirppu muṟṟa kaṇkaḷum, aḻakuṟappaṭarntu tōṉṟum māmi yum, nirikeṭātu vārntu kūriyavāy ilaṅkum eyiṟuṭiya cevvāyum, mūṅkil pōlum tōḷum, viḷaṅkum oḷikoṇṭa kivantiyum *toyyil poṟitta cuṇaṅkupūtta iḷamuliyum, mipōlum kariya moytta kūntalum, mayilaṉṉa cāyalum, maṭappamum uṭiya makaḷir palarum, tammik kōlañceytu, melleṉak kiyeṟintu naṭantu, kāmanukarcciyaṉṟip piṟitoṉ ṟiṉiyuṅ kallāta iḷiyōruṭaṉ kūṭikkaḷikkumpaṭi caṅkoppa naritta kūntalik kōtip piṉṉē muṭiyiṭṭup poliyum toṉmutu peṇṭir palar, kāmaru paṇṇiyaṅkaḷ pala "puṭiyami polinta vakiyami ceppu" kkaḷil vittu, maṇam kamaḻum pūkkaḷiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu ceṉṟu maṉitōṟum ulāvi viṟpaṉi ceyvar.
iṉi, immaṉiyakam kaṭantu celliṉ, oruvaṉ kāṇalāvaṉa kaṭavuṭpaḷḷi, antaṇar paḷḷi mutaliyaṉavām. avaṟṟuṭ kaṭavuṭ paḷḷiyeṉpatu puttappaḷḷiyām. tiṇṇiya katirkaḷit teṟikkum pēraṇikalaṅkaḷ palapūṇṭu, kāmarupēriḷam peṇṭir tām virumpikkūṭip pōṟṟum tam kaṇavaruṭaṉ, tātaṇintu viḷaṅkum tāmarip pōtik kiyiṟ piṭittuc cellutaloppa oḷḷiya ciṟu piḷḷikaḷit tam kiyiṟ piṭittukkoṇṭu, ellōrumākat tiraṇṭu ippaḷḷikaḷi yaṭintu, pūvum pukiyuṅ koṇṭu, paḷḷik kaṭavuḷip paravit toḻutu vaṇaṅkuvar.
antaṇar paḷḷiyāvatu ekkālamum maṟimuṭiviyē pārttoḻukalāṟṟum pāṉmiyar uṟiyum viḻumiya viṭamām. ivarkaḷ maṟināṉkaṉuḷ, ciṟappillāta atarvamaṟi nīṅkalāka, ēṉicciṟappuṭi maṟimūṉṟiyum tamakkup poruḷ viḷaṅkum vakiyiṟ pāṭutaluṭiyavar. aṉṟiyum, ivar viḻumiya cīr payappatāya vēḷvi yoḻukkatti līṭupaṭṭu niṟpavar. ivaratu neñcam aṟaneṟi piḻiyāta aṉpu niṟintu, uyar nilimiya tāṉa umparulakiyum ivaṇ niṉṟē yeytum koḷkimēṟ koṇṭu, viḷaṅkum viḻuppamuṭiyatu. itaṉāl, ivar, innāl vaki nilaṅkaḷaminta ulakattil opparum mikkārumilarāki, orutāmāki uṟikiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam cīvaṉ muttarāyirukkum ivar virumpiyuṟiyum iṭamē ippaḷḷi. itu maliyoṉṟi uḷveḷiyāka vāṅki iruppiṭamākkiṉāṟ pōṉṟa tiṇmiyum perumiyum amintatākum.
ivaṟṟōṭu uṭaṉeṇṇappaṭuvaṉavāyp pala aṟiñar cēkkikaḷ (amaṇappaḷḷikaḷ) arukē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi, cempiṉāṟ ceytamittāṟpōlum cevviyacuvarkaḷ cūḻntavi; nōkkuvār kāṭciviciyum tavirkkum vakiyil mēlēpuyarntu, kuṉṟaṅkaḷ pala aṇukkattē niṟintu tōṉṟuṅ kāleḻunta kāṭciyiṉip peṟṟu, viḷaṅkuvaṉavām. iṅku, tātuṇṭa vaṇṭukaḷ cūḻvantu muralum tēṉārum pūkkaḷum naṟumaṇam kamaḻum pukiyum koṇarntu cāvakar mutaliyōr vaṇaṅkip paravuvar; antak 'kayam kuṭintaṉṉa vayaṅkuṭi'k kōyiliṉ kaṇṇirukkum periyōr, vāṉamum nilamum oruṅkē muḻutum uṇarum cāṉṟa koḷkiyiṉiyum, akkoḷkikkut taḷartal illiyāyc cāyāta tiṇṇiya yākkiyum amiyap peṟṟavar. mēlum, *ivar pulaṉkaṇmēṟ cellukiṉṟa avāvin taṉiyum avitta pērāṟṟal paṭittamintavar; ceṉṟa kālamum varūum amiyamum, iṉṟu ivaṇ tōṉṟiya (nikaḻkālam) oḻukkamoṭu naṉkuṇarum matinuṭpam nūlōṭuṭiyar. ivaratu cēkkikaḷil pala vaṭaṅkaḷāl aminta nūluṟikaḷ toṅkum. avaṟṟil ivaratu kuṇṭikikaḷ vikkap peṟṟirukkum; akkuṇṭikikaḷ mikak kuṟukiya vāyuṭiyavi; kallip poḷintāṉṟa pōṉṟa tōṟṟattaṉavumām(?) iccēkkikaḷic cuṟṟilum naṟumpūpputarkaḷ pala vayaṅkuṅ kāṭci, maṉattāṉōkku vārkkup periyatōr iṟumpūtu payakkiṉṟatu. itaṉkaṇ uṟiyum aṟiñarāya amaṇartām varuttamaṟap paṟittu vaḻipaṭutaṟkeṉavē ippūñceṭikaḷum piṟavum vittu vaḷarkkap peṟukiṉṟavām.
iṉi, mēlē kūṟappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟōṭu, kalvi, aṟivu, nalloḻukkam, aṟaṉaruḷuṭimi mutaliyavi maṇakkum ciṟappuṭimiyāl ciṟantu viḷaṅkum aracaviyiluṟiyum periyōrāya kāvitimākkaḷ mutaliyōratu nalaṉum, piṟavum kūṟukiṉṟār.
kāvitimākkaḷāvār kāvitiyeṉṉum paṭṭatti aracaṉāl tarappeṟṟavarāvar. avarkaḷuḷ īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭupavar, aracaṉiṭattu appaṭṭattippeṟṟu, amiccukkaṭam pūṇṭoḻukum amāt tiyarāvar. ivarkaḷ muṟi vēṇṭiṉārkkum, kuṟivēṇṭi ṉārkkum accam, avalam, ārvam mutaliya nikaḻāmaṟpōkki, ceṟṟamātal uvakiyātal oṉṟēṉum oru tiṟattēṉum ceyyātu, tammiyum kāttu, ñemaṉkōl (tulākkōl) pōlum cemmittāya naṭunili piṟaḻāta koḷki mēṟkoṇṭava rāvar. aṉṟiyum, ivarkaḷ aṟaṅ kūṟaviyattārōṭu uṭaṉiruntu, aracaṉmāṭṭu nikaḻum naṉṟum tītumkaṇṭu, attīṅkukaḷiyā rāyntu avaṟṟuḷ avaṉ mīṭṭum oḻukāvāṟu aṭakki, aṉpum aṟaṉum avaṉiviṭṭu oḻiyā vakiyiṟkāttu oḻukum pērāṇmi taliciṟantavar. ittoḻilkaḷil īṭupaṭuṅkāl, ivarkaḷ tammiyum paḻipāvaṅkaḷ vantaṇukāvāṟu nīkkikkōṭalum, paranta pukaḻi yipparantavulakil niṟuttalum ceytu koḷḷum tiṟalmikkavar. itupaṟṟiyē ivarkaḷi āṉṟavintaṭaṅkiya koḷki mēmpaṭṭa cāṉṟōrkaḷōṭu oppavittu eṇṇupa. ēṉeṉiṉ, iccāṉṟōrum ariya vēḷvikaḷ palaceytu māvicumpu (tēvarulaku) vaḻaṅkum vālaṟivum, vēḷvikkālattup paranta tammārpiṭip pūcappeṟum naṟiya cantaṉappūccu muṭiyavarē. ivarkaḷ tam aracaṉic cūḻavirukkuṅkāl, uyariya paṭṭāṭi yuṭuttu, muṭiyil mayirkkaṭṭaṇintu (talippākiyaṇintu) ciṟanta tōṟṟaṅ koṇṭu tikaḻvar.
vāṇipanalaṉ:-
itukāṟum, maturikkāñciyiṉ karupporuḷum, maturi yāṇṭa maṉṉaratu āṭci nalaṉum, maturiyiṉ vaḷattu oru pakutiyum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. iṉi maturiyiṉ vāṇipa nalaṉ, viḻavu nalaṉ ākiya ēṉinalaṉkaḷiyum, kūṟi ikkāñciyiṉ coṟ poruṇalaṅkaḷip piṟanūlkaḷōṭu oppu nōkkumiṭattut tōṉṟum cila nūṇ poruḷkaḷum, avvārāycci yiṭip pōtarum akkāla oḻukka nalaṉkaḷiyum kūṟi ikkaṭṭuriyi muṭikkiṉṟēṉ.
vaṭitta coṟkaḷāṟṟoṭukkap peṟṟu, āyuntoṟum puttiṉpam payakkum polivu kūrntu, kaṭavuḷiṉpattik kaṟpārk kūṭṭum iṉpa niliyamāya ippāṭṭiṉ kaṇvarum vaṇikar pala tiṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. oru cilar paṇṭam pakarunar āvar. ivarkaḷ mali, nilam, nīr mutaliya viṭaṅkaḷiṟ paṭuvaṉavum, piṟaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟupāṉmi kiṭikkap peṟuvaṉavumāya paṇṭaṅkaḷōṭu palavāy vēṟupaṭṭu aḻaku ciṟakkum maṇi, muttu, poṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkut tāmpiṟatēyaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu vantuḷḷa arum paṇṭaṅkaḷi māṟi vaḻaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷ koḷvatūum miki paṭātu; koṭuppatūum kuṟipaṭātu. mēlum, ivarkaḷ, tamakku ōtiya illaṟaneṟiyiṟ ṟavaṟātu niṉṟu, aṟattāṟu pōṟṟi āruyirppēṇum iyalpiṉarāvar. ivar tam illaṅkaḷ, tiraṇṭa kuṉṟaṅkaḷ pala mika aṇmiyavāy niṟpa avaṟṟik kāṇ pārkkup pulaṉākum cīriya tōṟṟattiyum, viṇṇiṭip paṟantu tiriyum paruntukaḷ iruntu iḷippāṟip piṉṉar uyarap paṟakkum uyarcciyiyum, palavēṟu ciṟpattoḻil viḷaṅkum māṇpiyum uṭiya nalla illaṅkaḷākum. ivaṟṟuḷ palvakip paṇṭamoṭu uṇavum mikkuṟṟup poliyum.
niṟka, kōṭupōḻ kaṭinar mutal kammiyar īṟākavuḷḷa vaṇikar palar uḷar. avarkaḷuṭkōṭupōḻkaṭinar caṅku kaḷiyaṟuttu vaḷi mutaliyaṉavākacceytu viṟpar; tirumaṇi kuyiṟṟuṉar eṉpavar aḻakiya maṇikaḷi vāṅkittuḷiyiṭṭu māli mutaliyaṉavākat toṭuttuviṟpar; iḻipuṉinar poṉṉik kāyccit tūymiceytu oḷḷiya pūṇāraṅkaḷ ceyvar; ivarkaḷ taṭṭār eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭuvar; poṉ vāṇikar varap peṟum poṟṟiraḷi uriyiṭṭu nōkki māṟṟuk kāṇpavarāvar; kaliṅkam pakarnar uyariya paṭṭāṭikaḷum puṭavikaḷum viṟpar; cempu niṟikoṇmār eṉpār cempiṉi niṟuttu vilikku vāṅkum vaṇikarāvār; vampu niṟi muṭinar kaccukkaḷi yaḻaku peṟa muṭiyiṭṭut tamtoḻil nayamtōṉṟap puṉintuviṟkum vaṇikar. ēṉippūvāṇikar naṟiyapūkkaḷum pukikamaḻum cāntum āyntu toṭuttu viṟṟal ceyvar; cittirakārikaḷ eṉpār makaḷirāvar; ivar kūriya matinuṭpa muṭiyavar; eḻutappeṟum vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ naṉku viḷaṅkum vakiyil, ivar tam kūriya vuṇarvumuṟṟum porunta eḻutiya ōviyaṅkaḷi, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu oppak kāṭṭa, avvaḷaviṟ kāṇpār kaṭpulaṉellāṅ kavarntu niṟkum toḻiṟpēṟu ciṟakkum ciṟappiṉar. ivarkaṭku ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻutiviṟṟalē toḻil. kammiyar eṉappaṭuvōr antikkaṭiyil puṭavi viṟpavar. periyarum ciṟiyarumākat tiraṇṭu, nālvakit teruk kaḷilum, makkaḷ, oruvar kāloṭu oruvar kāluṟa neruṅki niṟpa, teṇṇīrttiriyāl maṭippup peṟum yāṟṟaṟal oppa, periyavum ciṟiyavumāya pala maṭippuṭivikaḷi virittu vittup piṟarkku viṟpar.
cōṟutarunar: ivarkaḷ varuvārkkuc cōṟiṭutaṟkeṉat taṉippaṭac cōṟiṭucālikaḷamittu, aṅkup pāṟcōṟum pālum koṇarn taḷippar. varupavar tamittuvariṉum, kūṭṭamāy variṉum cōṟiṭutalil variviṉṟi yaḷippar. aṅku accōṟ ṟoṭu tēṉ, palaviṉcuḷi, māṅkaṉi, pākaṟkāy, vāḻikkāy, vaḻutuṇaṅkāy mutaliyaṉavum, vāḻippaḻam, muntiri kippaḻam mutaliya paḻavaki kaḷum, ilikkaṟikaḷ (kīrivaki), kaṟkaṇṭu, kiḻaṅku mutaliyaṉavum ceṟintu kiṭakkum. ivaṟṟoṭukalantu maṇakkum ūṉumpiṟavum urippār uriyiṉiyum pāṭuvār pāṭṭiṉiyum ikantu tikaḻum.
celvarkaḷ: ivarkaḷ cekkar vāṉatti yoppac civantu nuṇaṅkum oḷḷiya āṭiyuṭuttu, ariyiṟ poṉ puṉinta uṭivāḷip polivupeṟakkaṭṭi, tōḷil mēlāṭi kiṭantaciya, piṟakkiṭāp perumiyuṟṟa tam tiruntaṭiyiṟ kaccam tiṉṟa kaḻaliruntu olippa, vēppanteriyalum, ceṅkaḻunīr māliyum aṇikiḷarum mārpiṟṟikaḻum ārattōṭu kalantu viḷaṅka, kāṟṟiṉuṅ kaṭiya kataḻpari pūṭṭiya tiṇṇiya tērmī tivarntu, nāṟpuṟamum kālōr (kālāṭkaḷ) kāval ceyya virintu cellum mēmpāṭuṭiyavar. varuvārkku variyātu koṭukkum vāṉampōlum vaṇmiyiṉarākiya ivarkku, avvaḷḷaṉmikkēṟpa vaḷaṅkeḻumiya celvam ciṟantu pukaḻ vaḷarkkum.
iṅṅaṉam, vīramum, veṟṟiyum, vaṇmiyum, celvamu moruṅkē vāykkappeṟṟa ivarkku, uḷattē kaṭavuḷaṉpu perukkeṭuttuc cellutal nammaṉōrāl maṟakkaṟpāṟṟaṉṟu. ivarkaḷ anti viḻaveṭuttalum, ataṉi yeṭuttaṟ poruṭṭu, puṇṇiya nīrāṭi, mācaṟa viḷaṅkum yākkiyarāy, naṟiya maṇan taruvaṉavaṟṟip peritu aṇintu, avvaṇitalā leḻum maṇam kamaḻ nāṟṟam teruvuṭaṉ kamaḻac cellutalum niṉittoṟum pēriṉpam payappaṉa. niṟka, ivarkaḷ eṭukkum viḻāviṉik kāṇum makaḷir nirinili māṭaṅkaḷiṉ aramiyantōṟum niṉṟum, tāmaṇintuḷḷa poṟpūṇkaḷoṭu cīṟaṭic cilampu pulampa iḻintum viḻākkāṇṭalum, atukāli avar tantōṟṟam vāṉara makaḷiratoppa, āynta poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa iḻikaḷuntoṭikaḷum pēraḻaku ceytalum, akakkāṭciyāl  (Imagination) nōkkuvārkkut tōṉṟātu oḻiyā. piṉṉar, nāyaṉmārkaḷ eḻuntaruḷuṅkāl eṭukkappaṭum ‘tiṇkāḻuṟṟa viyaliruvilōtam” (periya akaṉṟa koṭikaḷ) kāṟṟaciviṉāl teṇkaṭaṟ ṟiripōlap puṭippa, ammakaḷiratu oṇkuḻi tikaḻum oḷikeḻu tirumukam, maḻikkaṇ maṟiyum matiya moppat tōṉṟupu maṟiyum. iṉi, ic celvarkaḷ, maḻuvāḷ neṭi yōṉi mutalvaṉākakkoṇṭu ēṉit teyvavoḷiyum, vāṭāta pūkkaḷum, imiyāta nāṭṭamum, nāṟṟavuṇavu (avi yuṇavu) ṅkoṇṭa vurukeḻu periyōrāya māyōṉ, murukaṉ mutaliya kaṭavuḷarkaṭku uyarpali koṭuttu avvanti viḻaviṉi muṭippar. akkāli, eḻum tūriyaṅkaḷiṉ oli ali kaṭaliyu maṭakkum.
koṇṭi makaḷir (parattiyar):-
ivar *vāṉara makaḷiriyotta vaṉappuṭiyavar. mēlum ivar nīṉiṟa vicumpiṉkaṇ amarntu viḷiyāṭṭayarum am makaḷiriyoppavē, tammik kāṇpavar maṉatti varutti, avaratu poruḷikkavarum parattiyarāvar. ivarkaḷ oṇcuṭar parappum viḷakkaṅkaḷiṉ nalloḷiyil, palarākac cēraneruṅki, āḻnta nīr niṟinta tuṟikaḷiṉmuṉ parantu kiṭakkum nuṇ maṇaliṟ kūṭiyāṭuvar. akkāli, mutalyāmattil vācittaṟkuriya yāḻici yoru maruṅku eḻa, avvicik kēṟpa, ivarkaḷ tāḻntum, arukē olikkum muḻavoliyoṭu ottum maṉamakiḻn tāṭuvar. iṅṅaṉam maṇal viḷiyāṭṭu muṭintatum, avviṭat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, kuvaḷimalar, nīrkkīḻc cevvarumpu mutali yavaṟṟāṟ ṟoṭukkappeṟṟa mālittoṭarpala, tam āṭiyiṉ vaṭimpil viḻumvaṇṇa muṭuttu, nīr tiraṇṭāṟpōṉṟa veṇpū mālikaḷik koṇṭiyiṟ cūṭic cēyarum aṇiyarumākap palarāyt tam nalaṉ nayantu varum pala iḷamiyuṅ celvamu mikkāri, vañcaṉiyum poyyuṅ kalanta coṟkaḷāl, mutaliṟ kūṭṭik koṇṭu, avaratu nuṇpūṇākam tam ākattil vaṭuk koḷḷum vakiyil muyaṅkit tam poyyiṉpatti yavarkku nalkuvar. aṅṅaṉam, aṉpuṭiyār pōl muyaṅkiya muyakkatti avar poruḷ tarumaḷavu maṟittalālum, karuttu muṟṟum nayantuvantavaruṭiya celvattik kavarum muṟiyilē yīṭu paṭṭiruttalālum ivarkaḷ tarum iṉpam poyyiṉpam eṉap paṭukiṉṟatu. avarkaḷ celvam muṟṟumkeṭat tāmvāṅki, nuṇtātu uṇṭu piṉṉar at tātu niṟintuḷḷa pūviṉit tuṟantu cellum meṉciṟi vaṇṭiṉam oppa, avar kūṭṭatti, (tammip puṇarntu, tam peruñ celvatti yiḻantōratu kūṭṭatti), avar neñcam ēmāntu kalaṅka, immakaḷir nērē kiviṭṭuppirintu celvar. celpavar mīṇṭum, tam polivu kulintukiṭakkum paṇikaḷiyum oppaṉikaḷiyum perumiyum aḻakum naṉkamiyap puṉintu, miku nala meytiṉa varāyc ceṅkaḻunīr malarkaḷil curumpupaṭuvaṉavum, ēṉippūkkaḷum koṇṭu, maḻikku malarnta malar neruṅkiya oru ciṟu tūṟoppat tam kuḻaliṟ cūṭi, pōtaviḻum apputu malarkaḷiṉ naṟumaṇam teruveṅkum kamaḻa, vaḷikuluṅkak kivīci, maṇaṅkamaḻ maṉi tōṟum paḻantēr vāḻkkip paṟavipōṟ ceṉṟu, poruḷ tarutaṟkuriya iḷiyōr palaruṭaṉ viḷiyāṭṭayarvar.
pāciḻimakaḷir: ivarkaḷ kuṭippeṇkaḷāvar. ivar kaḷuṭiya illaṅkaḷ aṇaṅkuṭiya nallillaṅkaḷākum. ivarkaḷ, koḻuṅkuṭic celvarum piṟarum mēya tam illat tiruntu, maṇam puṇarntōṅkiya paciya iḻiyum oṭṭaṟṟa poṉvaḷi yalum aṇintu, irākkālaṅkaḷil, oṇ cuṭar viḷakkaṅkaḷiṉ nalloḷiyilamarntu, iciyēḻum vāytta cīriya ciṟiya yāḻeṭuttu, paṇmuṟiyil māṟiyicittut tammi nayakkum āruyirk kātalarkkuc ceviccuviyamutam ciṟakkavūṭṭit taḻuvi, tam uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta nāṇiyun tammiṭattē taṭuttuk koḷḷun takuti mēmpaṭṭavarāvar.
vaḷamaṉimakaḷir: ivar peruṅkuṭip piṟantu perumiyum, celvañ ceḻikkum ciṟappuṅkoṇṭa maṉimakaḷirāvar. ivaruṭ cilar “irumi yiṉpamum payakkum aṟivaṟinta makkaḷip peṟṟēm” eṉat tam kaṇavaruppa, makkaḷippayantu, melivu mēvum puṉiṟum tīrntu, iṟivaṉaruḷāl ōr iṭukkaṇum aṟṟukkuḷattu nīrāṭṭayarvar. atukaṇṭu, taliccūl koṇṭa makaḷir, tāmum iṅṅaṉ oru iṭukkaṇumiṉṟi makkaḷip peṟutal vēṇṭumeṉak kaṭavuḷipparavik kuṟitīrntapiṉ, polinta cuṟṟattārōṭu, vaḻipāṭṭiṟku vēṇṭappaṭum poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟuṭaṉ, peruntōḷ koṇṭa cāliṉi eṉṉum tēva rāṭṭiyōṭu, tivaviṉi yāḻiṉ taṇṭil niṟuttic cevvaḻip paṇṇicittu, kural eṉappaṭum narampu kūṭum vaṉṉattāl nalamparappi, appaṇṇukkum vaṉṉattukkum oppa muḻavaṅkaḷ olippa, melliya ciṟupaṟi (ākuḷi) kaṟaṅka, makaḷirpalar viḷak kēnti muṉcella, naṉmāmayileṉa meṉ mela naṭantu ceṉṟu cūḻvantu, kiyāṟṟoḻutu vaṇaṅkiya piṟaku, tām paṭitta pāṟ cōṟu mutaliya maṭipporuḷkaḷi yuṇpar.
ūrkāppāḷar: irākkālaṅkaḷil mūṉṟām yāmattil ūriccuṟṟit tiritarum kāvalarāvar. piṭiyāṉiyiṉ tōl pōlum iyaṟkik karuniṟam vāynta iruṇiṟak karuñ caṭṭiyum, marantuṇippiṉum, kal uṭippiṉum vāymaṭiyāk kūrmi koṇṭa nilamakaḻum uḷiyum, tūkkiṭṭavāḷum, toṭu tōṟ kōtta ceruppumē ivarkaḷ kiyakattiruppaṉa. ivarkaḷ kaṇ māṟāṭavar (viḻittakaṇ imikku maḷavilē maṟikiṉṟa kaḷvar) oṭuṅkum otukkiṭaṅkaḷi nuṉittu, piṟar teriyātapaṭi āṇṭu maṟintu niṉṟu, vayakkaḷiṟṟiṉ varavu pārkkum vayappuli pōla, virinta vuḷḷamoṭu nōkkum tuñcāk kaṇṇum, pēy mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum añcākkoḷkiyum uṭiyar. aṉṟiyum, ivarkaḷ, kaḷavuttoḻil vallārāṟ ciṟappittukkūṟappaṭum toḻil nuṭpaṅkaḷi yeḷitil aṟintāḷum āṇmiyum, nuṇmiceṟinta ariya nūlkaḷ palavum ārāyum nuṇṇaṟivum paṭittavar. ivar kaṭpulaṉāki, piṉṉart tappak karuti muyal vārkku orukālum tapputal iyalātu. ivarkaḷ ūkkaruṅkaṇi yiṉarātaliṉ, atu kūṭātu pōkiṉṟatu. niṟka, ivarkaḷuṭiya toḻil nayappō (Love towards the discharge of duties) yāvarum mikap pōṟṟaṟpālatām. tēr vaḻaṅku teruvil nīr tiraṇ ṭoḻukumāṟu, perumaḻi peyyum naṭunāḷ yāmattum, ivarkaḷ, oru ciṟitum acivu ilarāki niṉṟu, tam toḻili nayam vantu ceytoḻukukiṉṟavar. iṉi, ippeṟṟiyarāya kāvalar irunta nāḷil kaḷavut toḻil mēṟkoṇṭirunta kaṇmāṟāṭavar, iyalpu oruciṟitu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivvāṭavar karuniṟamum nuṇmiyuṅ koṇṭa cēli yoṉṟi yariyiṟ kaṭṭi, ataṉkaṇ kūriya coṭṭi (uṭivāḷ) kaḷic ceruki, iru tuṭikaṭkum iṭiyiṟceluttip puṟattē teriyāmal maṟittuk koḷvar. piṉṉar pala niṟaṅkaḷamittup polivu ceyyappeṟṟa nīlakkacciṉiyaṇintu, ataṉ kīḻmelliya nūlēṇi yoṉṟic cuṟṟik koṇṭumiruppar. ivarkaḷiṉ naci muṟṟum celvarkaḷ vittuḷḷa pēraṇikalaṅkaḷiṉ mēlatākavē niṟkum. (ivaratu toḻiṟṟiṟam mutaliyavaṟṟip poruḷārāyc ciyiṟ kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.)
aracaṉāl iṉpanukarap peṟutaṟkuriya mātar: aracaṉ taṉ aracirukki nakarākiya maturiyiṉ kaṇṇiruntu ivarkaḷ poṟ kalaṅkaḷil tarum iṉpakkuṭiyiṉi yuṇṭu tuyilkoḷḷap pukuvatu marapu. kāmapāṉameṉappaṭuvatu
“paḻumiḷa kukka pāṟi neṭuñcuṉi
 muḻumutaṟ kokkiṉ tīṅkaṉi yutirnteṉa
 puḷḷeṟi piracamoṭu īṇṭip palaviṉ
 nekiḻntuku naṟumpaḻam viḷinta tēṟal”
pōlum oruvakit tēṟal. itu naṟumpūkkaḷiliruntu peṟap paṭutaliṉ naṟumaṇamum iṉcuviyum mikkuḷatu. itaṉi yuṇṭa piṉṉarē maṉṉaṉ paḷḷikkuc cēṟalum tuyil kōṭalum nikaḻum. avarkkup pōkamakaḷirākum ivarkaḷatu vaṉappu īṇṭuk kūṟap paṭukiṟatu.
nāṟṟiciyum tam ciṉikaḷ paṭarntu centīppōlum pūkkaṇ malarntu curumpu murala, aḻakumikku viḷaṅkum acōkam poḻiloṉṟil, pakal muṟṟum cuṭar poḻintu kuṭavariyēṟiya viḷaṅkukatir ñāyiṟu taṉ iḷaveyil parappuṅkāṟṟōṉṟum vaṇṇamum vaṉappum poruntiya mēṉiyuṭiyarāy, naṭuvē maṇikaḷ vittiḻitta tāvil poṟkalaṉkaḷ aṇintu viḷaṅkum immakaḷir, tampeṟalaruṅ kaṟpiṉāl, ulakamuṟṟum viḷakka muṟumpaṭi mēmpāṭu peṟṟavarāvar. ivarkaḷ mayil pōlum cāyalum māviṉ taḷirpōlum mēṉiyum, attaḷiriṉ puṟattirukkum īrkkuppōlum titaliyumuṭiyavar. ivar tam paṟkaḷ oṇmiyum kūrmiyum peṟṟavi. vaḷaviya kātukaḷ oḷḷiyamakākkuḻi yaṇiyappaṭṭut tāḻntu viḷaṅkum. mēlum, ivarkaḷ kuḷaṅkaḷil neruṅkiyiruppatum, neruppup pōlum itaḻkaḷi yuṭiyatāyt teyvaṅkaṭku uriyatumākavuḷḷa tāmariyiṉ tātupaṭum periya pūppōlum vāṇmukattiṉarāyk kikaḷil āytoṭi pūṇṭu viḷaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷōṭu kōti yāṟ polinta cēkkik kaṇ maṉṉaṉ iṉpanukarntu iṉṟuyil koḷvaṉ.
mālikālacciṟappu:
neṭunakarāya maturiyiṉ elliyellām, pakal muṟṟum oḷiyum veppamum oruṅkukoṉṟu, taṉ ciṉamāṟiyateṉum vaki, ñāyiṟu taṉ oḷiyic cērakkoṇṭukuṭamutaṟ kuṉṟam cēra, kuṇamutal variyil muḻumatiyantōṉṟit taṉ nilavu virittup pakaleṉumpaṭi iravuc ceyyum. peṇmic ciṟappum māṉpiṇipōlum nōkkamumuṭiya makaḷir, neṭuñcuṭar parappum viḷakkukkaḷiyēṟṟi, palavēṟu vakippaṭṭa aṇi kaḷip puṉintu, taṇṇaṟuṅ kaḻunīr malari māliyākat toṭut taṇintu, tām tam kūntaliṟ pūciya naṟuviya mayirc cantaṉatti alittu nīkkik kattūri, cantaṉa mutaliyavaṟṟi yarittu oṉṟākak kalantu pūci, meṉṉūl āṭikku naṟumpuki yūṭṭiyuṭuttu viḷaṅkuvar. nayanta kātalarip pirinta makaḷirō varuntutal ceyvar.
iravucciṟappu: mutalyāmam:
ivyāmattiṉkaṇ, mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa koṇṭi makaḷir poruḷ tarutaṟkuriya iḷiñarōṭu viḷiyāṭi, avar maṉattik karittut tamvayappaṭutta muyaluvar. pāciḻimakaḷir tam kaṇavarkku yāḻiciyum muyakka viṉpattiyuntantu, tam uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta nāṇik kāttukkoḷḷuvar. māṟupāṭumikkup porum perum pōrkaḷil, niṉṟa aṭi māṟāmiyāṟpaṭum vaṭuppaṭṭa neṟṟiyum, pōrē virumpum uḷḷamum, curumpu muralum tumpippūc cūṭutalumuṭiya maṟavar, (vīrarkaḷ) tōṭṭiyāl vaṭuppaṭṭa nutalum, pōrtāṅkum vaṉmiyum, periya kiyiṉiyuṅ koṇṭa peruṅ kaḷiṟukaḷikkoṇarntu, kolipaḻakku mukattāl, palakālum eṭuttu ōṭṭa, akkāli, ak kaḷiṟukaḷ peruvicikoṇṭu eḻutalum, avaṟṟiṉ viciyik kāṇṭaṟ poruṭṭu, immaṟavarkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ muṉpu ōṭippārppār. avi tammip piṭittukkoṇṭu tammēl vārāmal añci mīḷutaṟkeṉa, avaṟṟiṉ kālkaḷiṟ potukkumpaṭi, cila pala  kappaṇaṅkaḷi, *avi varumvaḻiyil nilattē citaṟuvar. ip payiṟci muṭintatum, ivarkaḷ kaṭṭeḷiviyuṇṭu makiḻ ciṟantu terukkaḷiṟ ṟiritaruvar. vaḷamaṉimakaḷir putalvarp payantu puṉiṟṟup melivuntīrntu kuḷanīrāṭalum, avarik kāṇum talic cūl makaḷir kaṭavuḷip paravutalum ceyvar. orutti nāṟṟam, tōṟṟam mutaliyavikaḷāl vēṟupaṭṭāḷāka, avaṭku av vēṟupāṭṭukkaṇ, murukaṉāl vantatu eṉa veṟiyāṭṭayarum vēlaṉ avaṭaṉ peṟṟōrkku nayampaṭakkūṟi, avarit taṉ collāl vaḷittukkoṇṭu, calli, karaṭi mutaliya iṉṉiyaṅkaḷ kaṟaṅka, kārkālattu malarum iyalpiṉiyuṭiya kuṟiñci malaric cūṭi, kaṭampucūṭi aḻaku mikum murukaṉit taṉ meyk kaṇṇiṟutti, vaḻipāṭu ceyvaṉ. atupōtu mātarmakaḷirum matavali mintarum tātupōlum erupparanta maṉṟaṅkaḷiṟ kuraviyayara, ēṉiyōr uriyum pāṭṭum iṭiyiṭiyē viravappāṭiyum āṭiyum makiḻvar. iṅṅaṉam, pala nikaḻccikaḷ nikaḻvataṉāl pala vēṟuvakippaṭṭa kampali mikuntu, pērici koṇṭa naṉṉaṉ eṉṉum vaḷḷal koṇṭāṭum piṟanta nāṭkoṇṭāṭṭattaṉṟu eḻum ōciyi ottu imiḻum.
pāṉāṭkoṇṭakaṅ kulyāmam: (iraṇṭām yāmam)
itu patiṉintām nāḻikiyi muṭivākakkoṇṭa pakuti yākum. itaṉ kaṇ, caṅkukaḷi muḻakkum muḻakkam oru puṭiyaṭaṅ kiyaviya, paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vilikūṟi viṟpaṉi ceyyum vaṇikar tam kaṭikaḷiṉ caṭṭakkāl eṉappaṭum kāḻic cāyttut tam neṭiya kaṭikaḷi yaṭittukkoṇṭu illam ēkuvar. maṭappam matarppu mutaliya uyariya kuṇaṅkaḷum oḷḷiya iḻikaḷum koṇṭamakaḷir paḷḷiyiṟ kaṇṭuñcal ceyvar. variporuntiya tēṉiṟāloppa, aṭi, mōtakam, karittamāvappam mutaliya vaṟṟi viṟkum  appavāṇikar avaṟṟōṭē tūṅkuvaṉarāy uṟaṅka, kūttāṭal payiṉṟa vayiriyarum piṟarum kūttāṭ ṭoḻintu uṟaṅka, pāṭu avintu aṭaṅkiya paṉikkaṭalpōl pāyil kaṇvaḷarvōr iṉitu kaṇṇayarvar.

24. naṉṉaṉum paraṇarum
naṉṉaṉ eṉpavaṉ cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattukkuc ciṟitu piṟkālattil cēraṉāṭṭu oru pakutiyil iruntu aracu purinta kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ. ivaṉ vēḷir kulattil tōṉṟiyavaṉ; ivaṉuṭiya peṟṟōr varalāṟu oṉṟum terintilatu.
ivaṉuṭiya nāṭu koṇkāṉa nāṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ituvē piṉṉar, koṅkaṇanāṭeṉṟum, koṅku nāṭeṉṟum maruvi vaḻaṅkivaralāyiṟṟeṉṟu kūṟupa. itaṉuṭiya parappu, ikkālamuṟippaṭi, micūr nāṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyum, cēlam kōyamputtūr ākiya nāṭukaḷum, nīlakiri mēlakiri mutaliya kiḻakkumalit toṭariṉ pakutiyum koṇṭiruntatu. innāṭṭiṉ kaṇ pāram, pirampu, viyalūr, pāḻi eṉṉum ciṟanta nakaraṅkaḷum, pāḻi, ēḻil mutaliya malikaḷum iruntaṉa. in nāṭṭiṉkaṇ koṇkāṉam eṉṉum ciṟanta mali yiruntamiyiṉ, ataṉic cūḻnta innāṭum koṇkāṉa nāṭu eṉap paṭalāyiṟṟu. in nāṭṭiṉkaṇ poṉmikutiyākak kiṭikkum.
mēṟkūṟiya nakaraṅkaḷuḷ pāram eṉpatu ivaṉukkē urit tāyiṉum nāṭṭiṉ parappuṭimiyum, āṭciyarumi yuṭimiyum kuṟittu, miñili eṉṉum vīraṉ oruvaṉ āṭciyiṉ kīḻ, iruntatu. pāḻi eṉṉum nakaram, pāḻi yeṉṉum maliyaṭiyilirunta pērūr; itaṉkaṇ, naṉṉaṉ tāṉ īṭṭiya poruḷi vēḷir palar kāvalceyya vittiruntāṉ. itu tēṉvaḷam ciṟantatu.
naṉṉaṉ, palarip puṟaṅ kaṇṭu avar aruṅkalam koṇarntu pāḻikkaṇ tokuttu vittamiyē yaṉṟi, "iṭaṉiṉṟi iran tōrkku illeṉṟal iḻi" veṉak karuti variyātu vīcum vaḷḷaṉmiyum, pulavar pāṭum pukaḻum paṭittavaṉ. ivaṉ kālattiṉ "nāṇmaṅkalam" eṉṉum piṟantanāṭ koṇṭāṭṭam nikaḻntatu.
ivaṉ orukāl koṇṭa vañciṉam, toḻilāl muṟṟup peṟuṅkāṟum, atu taṇiyāṉ eṉap peṟappaṭuvatu avaṉ pōrc ceyalkaḷāl aṟiyalām. ivaṉ kālattil piṇṭaṉ, atikaṉ eṉpār mika meyvali paṭittiruntaṉar. ivaruḷ  atikaṉ eṉpavaṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ āṇikkīḻ niṟkum kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ. ākavē, avaṉ atikaṉpacumpūṇpāṇṭiyaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. ivaṉ pōrttoḻilil mikka vaṉmi yuṭiyavaṉ; nallici tarum iravalarkkup perum poruḷ aḷittu aḻiyāp pukaḻ peṟṟavaṉ; villēntiya paṭivīrar palar koṇṭavaṉ. naṉṉaṉ iv viruvariyum veṉṟu perum poruḷ koṇṭatōṭamiyātu, apporuḷkaḷi avarnāṭṭu yāṉi mītēṟṟi, avartam makaḷir kūntalāl kayiṟu tirittup piṇittuk koṇarntu taṉatu pāḻinakariṉ kaṇ vēḷir kāppa vittaṉaṉ.
naṉṉaṉ ceṉṟa viṭaṅkaḷilellām taṉ veṉṟiyē viḷaṅkac ceytaṟkut tuṇiyāka ēṟṟi, atti, kaṅkaṉ, kaṭṭi, puṉṟuṟi, kaṇiyaṉ eṉap pala vīrar iruntaṉar. ivaruḷ kaṭṭi yeṉpāṉ ciṟanta vīraṉallaṉ; ivaṉ tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ eṉpā ṉōṭu pōrceyyat toṭaṅkic ceṉṟavaṉ, avaṉuṭiya avikkaḷattu eḻum kiṇiyōci kēṭṭu añci yōṭiṉāṉ. orukāl, naṉṉaṉ, ivartam tuṇiyuṭaṉ, perumpūṇ ceṉṉi yōṭu pōrceyyac ceṉṟu, avaṉuṭiya paṭivīraṉākiya paḻiyaṉik kolla, ataṉāṟ ciṉam ciṟantu perum pōruṭaṟṟiya ceṉṉikkup puṟantantu ōṭiṉaṉ.
naṉṉaṉ paṭivīraruḷ miñili yeṉpavaṉ ciṟanta vīraṉ; naṉṉaṉ pukaḻkku mutaṟkāraṇa mākiyōṉ im miñiliyē eṉak kōṭal vēṇṭum. ivaṉ aṉṉi yeṉṉum vīraṉukku makaṉ. inta aṉṉi, akkālattil perum valic ciṟappuṭiyaṉā yirunta titiyaṉ eṉpavaṉuṭiya kāvalmarattit taṭintu, vēḷevvi eṉpavaṉ kūṟiya nalluriyiyum kēḷātu, avaṉ ciṉattiṟ kāḷākip piṉ avaṉōṭu pōr ceytu, kuṟukki yeṉṉu miṭattup puṟantantu vīḻntaṉaṉ. piṉṉar avvaṉṉi, kōcar kaḷōṭu porutu tōlviyuṟa avar avaṉ kaṇkaḷikkaḷintu aḻittaṉar. icceytiyāl āṟāc ciṉamuṟ ṟirunta miñiliyi naṉṉaṉ taṉ paṭittalivaṉākak koṇṭu, akkōcarkaḷi veṉṟaḻittu, vākicūṭiṉaṉ, titiyaṉ iṟantapiṉ, miñili avaṉuṭiya aḻuntūriṉ kaṇ ciṟappuṭaṉ ulāviṉaṉ.
akkālattil, kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi nārmuṭiccēral eṉṉum cēravēntaṉukku āyeyiṉaṉ eṉṉum paṭit talivaṉ oru vaṉiruntaṉaṉ. iccēraṉukkum, naṉṉaṉukkum ekkāraṇattālō pakimiyuṇṭāka, eyiṉaṉ miñiliyōṭu pōr ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. eyiṉaṉukkup palar miñiliyiṉ valiyuṭimi viḷaṅkak kūṟiyum eyiṉaṉ koḷḷātu pōr ceytu oḻintāṉ. itaṉā luṇṭāṉa ciṉam nār muṭic cēralukku virivil aṭaṅkiṟṟaṉṟu; cēraṉukkum naṉṉaṉukkum kaṭampiṉ peru vāyil, vākip peruntuṟi eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷil pōr nikaḻntaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ. vākip perun tuṟip pōrkkaṇṇē, naṉṉaṉ perumpōr ceytu taṉṉāṭiḻantu āviyu miḻantāṉ.
itu nikaḻnta ciṟitu kālattukku muṉṉar, peṇkoli oṉṟu purintu tīrāp paḻiyum peṟṟāṉ. kuṇam kuṟṟam iraṇṭiṉuḷ oṉṟē yuṭiyār ulakat tilaraṉṟō! peṇṇorutti nīrāṭac ceṉṟavaḷ, nīrkkaṇ mitantu pōnta ivaṉukkuriya muṟṟāta paḻam oṉṟit tiṉṟoḻin tāḷāka. atu poṟāta ivaṉ, avaḷ tanti eṇpattoru kaḷiṟukaḷiyum, avaḷ niṟi poṉcey pāvi koṭuppavum koḷḷātu, avaḷik koliyē purintaḻintāṉ. ivaṉ orukāl oḷaviyār taṉṉip pāṭi vantami yaṟintu avar variciyaṟintu paricil nalkā toḻiya, avar ivaṉi veṟuttup pāṭiṉar.
iṉi, ivaṉatu varalāṟu aṟitaṟkup pērutaviyā yirunta āciriyar paraṇar pāṭalkaḷi ovvoṉṟāka ārāyvām. ivaṟṟuḷ akanāṉūṟṟil uḷḷaṉavaṟṟuḷ palavaṟṟiṟku uri yiṉmiyiṉ, uriyum īṇṭuk kūṟukiṉṟōm. piḻi paṭuvaṉa vaṟṟit terikkumāṟu periyōrkaḷi vēṇṭukiṉṟēm.
āciriyar paraṇar akanāṉūṟṟil tām pāṭiya muppattu nāṉkupāṭṭukkaḷuḷ naṉṉaṉ varalāṟṟuk kiyiyap patiṉāṉku pāṭṭukkaḷ pāṭiyuḷḷār. avaṟṟuḷ, ivaṉ tittaṉ, piṇṭaṉ mutaliyōri veṉṟa ceytiyum, āay pirampu eṉṉum pērūr ivaṉukkē uritteṉpatum piṟavumākiya ceytikaḷōṭu perum pālaṉavaṟṟuḷ miñiliyiṉ pōrcceyaliṉ nalaṅkaḷiyum viḷakkic celkiṉṟār. ēṉik kuṟuntoki, naṟṟiṇi mutaliya toki nūlkaḷuḷ, naṉṉaṉ kōcariveṉṟamiyum, peṇkoli purintamiyum, piṇṭaṉ mutaliyōri veṉṟu avartam poruḷ kaḷi makaḷir kūntalāṟ ṟiritta kayiṟṟāṟ kaṭṭik koṇarntamiyum piṟavum kūṟukiṉṟār.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ, tittaṉ, piṇṭaṉ mutaliyōri veṉṟa ceyti kūṟum pāṭṭu,
"neñcunaṭuṅ karumpaṭar tīra vantu
  kuṉṟuḻi naṇṇiya cīṟūr āṅkaṇ
  celīiya peyarvōḷ vaṇarcuri yimpāl
  nuṇkōl akavunarp puranta pēricic
  ciṉaṅkeḻu tāṉit tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ
  iraṅkunīrp parappiṟ kāṉalam peruntuṟit
  taṉantaru naṉkalam citiyat tākkum
  ciṟuveḷ ḷiṟaviṉ kuppi yaṉṉa
 vuṟupaki tarūum moymmūcu piṇṭaṉ
 uṇmura ṇuṭiyak kaṭanta veṉvēl
 icinal līkik kaḷiṟuvīcu vaṇmakiḻp
 pārattut taliva ṉāra naṉṉaṉ
 ēḻil neṭuvarip pāḻic cilampiṟ
 kaḷimayiṟ kalāvat taṉṉa tōḷē
 valvil iḷiyar perumakaṉ naḷḷi
 cōli yaṭukkattuc curumpuṇa virinta
 kaṭavuṭ kānta ḷuḷḷum palavuṭaṉ
 iṟumpūtu kañaliya vāymalar nāṟi
 valliṉum vallā rāyiṉum ceṉṟōrkkuc
 cālaviḻ neṭuṅkuḻi niṟiya vīcum
 māal yāṉi yāay kāṉattut
taliyāṟṟu niliiya cēyuyar piṟaṅkal
vēyamik kaṇṇiṭi puriic
cēya vāyiṉum naṭuṅkutuyar tarumē"	- eṉpatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: (kūṭṭam peṟāmiyāl) neñcam naṭuṅkat takka poṟuttaṟkariya varuttam nīṅka vantu, kūṭṭattāl an nōyip pōkkik, kuṉṟiṉuk kaṇittāṉa ciṟṟūriṉkaṭ celvāṉ peyarntu pōkiṉṟavaḷuṭiya, neṟittuc curinta kūntaliṉiyum, nuṇṇiya kōliyu muṭiya pāṇmakaḷirip pātukātta mikka pukaḻiṉiyum, ciṉam poruntiya tāṉi yiṉiyu muṭiya tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ eṉpāṉuṭiya olikkiṉṟa nīrp parappiṉiyuṭiya kāṉalam peruntuṟikkaṇ poruḷ mikutiyākak koṇarum nalla marakkalaṅkaḷ citiyumpaṭi mōtu kiṉṟa ciṟiya veḷḷiya iṟāmīṉkaḷiṉ tokutiyi yoppa, mikka pakiyiṉic ceppum valimi niṟinta piṇṭaṉ eṉpāṉit taṉ maṉamāṟupāṭu keṭumāṟu porutu veṉṟaveṟṟi poruntiya vēliṉiyum, icipayakkum variyā īkiyum, kaḷiṟukaḷip paricilarkkuk koṭukkum vaḷaviya makiḻcciyiiṉayumuṭiya, "pāram" eṉṉum ūrukkut talivaṉāṉa āram aṇinta, - naṉṉaṉ eṉpavaṉuṭiya "ēḻil" eṉṉum neṭiya maliyic cārnta "pāḻi" yeṉṉum maliyiṉkaṭ kaḷittāṭum mayiliṉ kalāpatti yotta -tōḷ, valiya villiṉiyuṭiya vīrarkaḷuk kup periya talivaṉāṉa naḷḷi yeṉpavaṉuṭiya malip pakkattut taḻitta cōlikkaṇ vaṇṭukaḷ matuvuṇṇa malarnta kaṭavuḷ virumpum kāntaḷ malarkaḷuḷḷum palavākap perumitam niṟinta melliya malarkaḷākiya kiyiṉittōṟṟuvittu, taṉṉi yuḷḷivantavar oṉṟu kūṟa vallarākavātal alla rākavātal iruppiṉum avarkku mikka cōṟṟiṉi avar tam neṭiya pāṉi niṟiyumpaṭi aḷikkum periya yāṉikaḷi yuṭiya āay eṉpāṉatu kāṉattiṉ arukuḷḷa taliyāṟu eṉṉumiṭattu nilipeṟṟa mika vuyarnta maliyiṉkaṇ uṇṭākiya mūṅkiliṉkaṇuviṉ iṭip pakutiyi ottavāy, nammip pirintu cēymikkaṇṇē akaṉṟu nīṅkiṉa eṉiṉum, kūṭṭam peṟāmuṉ tanta naṭuṅkat takka tuyarattiyē iṉiyum, tārā niṉṟaṉa vākalāṉ nām kūṭṭam peṟṟum pēṟēmāyiṉēm eṉpatu.
iḵtu, iravuk kuṟikkaṇ talimakaḷik kūṭi nīṅkum talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu. arumpaṭar tīra vantu cīṟūr āṅkaṇ, celīiya peyarvōḷ, kaḷimayiṟ kalāvat taṉṉa tōḷ āymalarnāṟi, vēyamik kaṇṇiṭi puri iccēya vāyiṉum naṭuṅku tuyar tarum eṉa viyiyum.
iravuk kuṟikkaṇ talimakaḷit talippeyvāṉ varupavaṉ kūṭṭam iṭiyīṭu paṭuṅkolō eṉṉum eṇṇattaṉāy naṭuṅki varukiṉṟāṉākaliṉ, "neñcu naṭuṅku paṭar" eṉṟum appaṭartāṉum, maṟippiṉum aṭaṅkātu mikutaliṉ, "arum paṭar" eṉṟum, kūṟiṉāṉ. āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārum, ataṉi "iṟippārkku, ūṟṟunīr pōla miku" meṉṟu kūṟiṉār. (kuṟaḷ 1161) uḻi, ēḻām vēṟṟumipporuḷ paṭa vanta iṭic col. kūṭṭam peṟṟavaḻi mayiṟkalāvam pōl meṉmiyum uyarvu muṭiyavāy iṉpam payanta ciṟappunōkkik "kaḷimayiṟ kalāvat taṉṉa tōḷ" eṉṟum, avviṉpam tantu peyarntavaḻit tārāmiyāy "aṟitōṟaṟiyāmi kāṇpatu pōl maṟintu tuyartarutaliṉ  "naṭuṅkutuyartarum" eṉṟum, neruṅkiyavaḻit taṉ piriviṉāluḷatākiya melivu kāṭṭiyum, nīṅkiyavaḻit taṉmaṉattē tōṉṟi niṉippittum varuttutaliṉ "cēya vāyiṉum naṭuṅkutuyar tarumē" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. ummi, eccavummi.
pāṇmakaḷ kiyil mūṅkiṟkōl uṭiyaḷātal, taṉpiṟap puṇarttaṟkeṉpa. iṅṅaṉam pāṇmakaḷ orutti kātampari eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi nūliṉ kaṇṇum kūṟap paṭukiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ kiyilum mūṅkiṟ kōloṉ ṟirunta teṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. veḷiyaṉ eṉpatu veḷiyam eṉṉum ūriṉaṉ eṉṉum poruḷa teṉpar. ivaṉ uṟantikkaṇ iruntu aracāṇṭavaṉ eṉṟum, tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ eṉṟē āciriyar paraṇarē piṟitōriṭattum, "malitārt, tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ  uṟanti nāḷavi" (akam. 226) eṉak kūṟi yuḷḷār. ivaṉ "vīri veḷiyaṉ tittaṉ" eṉṟum "vīri vēṇmāṉ veḷiyaṉ tittaṉ" (naṟ. 58) eṉṟum "vīri veḷiyaṉ tittaṉār" eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum cōḻamaṉṉaṉ. akanāṉūṟṟil "peruṅkaṭal mukanta iruṅkiḷik koṇmū"eṉṟu toṭaṅkum pāṭṭu ivaṉ pāṭiyatu. veḷiyaṉallur eṉṉum ūr oṉṟu tiruvattipurattukkuk kiḻakkē mūṉṟumil tūrattil uḷatu.
kāṉaṟ peruntuṟikkaṇ taṉam tarum valiya marakkalaṅkaḷ citiyumāṟu ciṟiyavum veṇmiyavu māya iṟāmīṉkaḷ kūṭit tākkum eṉṟa vuvamiyāl villēntiya valiya vīrarkaḷuṭaṉ varum taṉ paṭiyic citaṟumāṟu piṇṭaṉatu cēṉi pōntu tākkiṉami peṟṟām. acceyal viḷaṅka āciriyar "uṟupaki tarūum moymmūcu piṇṭaṉ" eṉṟār. eṉavē naṉkalam citiyumāṟu iṟaviṉ kuppi tākkiṉum, akkalaṅkaḷiṉ viciyāl etiraḻintu avi irintōṭal pōlap piṇṭaṉ cēṉiyu moḻinta teṉpār, "uṇmura ṇuṭiyak kaṭanta" veṉṟār. murukakkaṭavuḷ virumpum pūvākaliṉ "kaṭavuṭ kāntaḷ" eṉṟār. tirumāl uyartta koṭiyik kaṭavuṭ koṭi eṉpatu pōla(pari. 4.43uri)
malar, uvamiyākupeyarāyk kiyiṉi yuṇarttiṟṟu "koṭiyiyalār kipōṟ kuvintaṉa muki" eṉṟār paripāṭali lum, pari. 20.98) 'valliṉum vallārāyiṉum ceṉṟārkkuc cālaviḻ neṭuṅkuḻi niṟiya vīcum, māal yāṉi āay eṉṟatu āay aṇṭiraṉatu koṭimaṭamkūṟiyavāṟu cālaviḻ neṭuṅ kuḻi eṉpataṟkuc cāl eṉṉum maṭkalattu āḻnta kuḻi niṟiya eṉiṉumām. taliyāṟṟu niliiya eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇattic cellum neṟiyiṉ mutaṟkaṇṇē nili peṟṟa eṉiṉumām. atupōtu atu piṉmuṉṉākat tokka āṟāvataṉ, toki, cēyavāyiṉum eṉpuḻic cemmiyuṭiya vāyiṉum eṉṉurip piṉumām.
itaṉkaṇ piṇṭaṉatu cēṉi naṉṉaṉ cēṉiyi eti rūṉṟit tākkutalum, atu nōkki naṉṉaṉ cēṉi accēṉiyi yaḻittup pūcal māṟṟutaliṉ, itu kuṟiñciyiṉ puṟamākiya veṭci yāyiṟṟu. tuṟi pūcaṉmāṟṟu, āy aviḻ vīcutal veṭciyil uṇṭāṭṭu.
iravuk kuṟikkaṇ kūṭṭam peṟṟavaṉ peṟātāṉ pōla neñcoṭu nontu kūṟutaliṉ, meyppāṭu; taṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya varuttam paṟṟiya aḻuki. payaṉ; neñcoṭu taṉ ārāmi kūṟutal.
kuṟippu: iravukkuṟikkaṇ kūṭṭam peṟṟuc  cellum talimakaṉ taṉ neñcoṭu kūṟuvatu poruḷākap pāṭat toṭaṅkiya āciriyar mutaṟkaṇ taliviyiṉ iyalkūṟip piṉ avaḷ tōḷnili kūṟuvār māṭṭuṟuppākat tittaṉatu kāṉalam peruntuṟikkaṇ kalaṅkaḷ varum ciṟappuk kūṟi ataṉiyē uvamiyāka iyittup piṇṭaṉi naṉṉaṉ veṉṟamiyum, avaṉuṭiya pāram eṉṉum nakaram, ēḻil neṭuvari, pāḻimali mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ nalamum viḷakkip piṉṉar avaḷ kicciṟappiṉi naḷḷiyiṉ cōli yaṭukkattip pukaḻu mukattāṟ kūṟip paṭip pōr maṉattit tamvayam koṇṭu ic ceytikaḷuḷ naṭunāyakamāy naṉṉaṉ pukaḻ avartam maṉatiṟ patiya vittuḷḷami nōkki yiṉ puṟaṟpāṟṟu. iṅṅaṉ ovvorupāṭṭiyum viritteḻutiṉ, itu mika viriyumākaliṉ, iṉi ovvoru pāṭṭiyum potup poruḷ kūṟumukattāl viḷakkic cellukiṉṟēṉ.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ nakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa āay pirampu eṉpatu uṇarttum pāṭṭu.
"mēṟṟuṟik koḷīiya kaḻāliṟ kīḻttuṟi
  yukuvā raruntap pakuvā yāmi
  kampu ḷiyava ṉāka vicipiṇit
  teṇkaṭ kiṇiyiṟ piṟaḻu mūraṉ
  iṭineṭun teruviṟ katumeṉak kaṇṭeṉ
  poṟṟoṭi muṉki paṟṟiṉa ṉāka
  vaṉṉā yeṉṟaṉe ṉavaṉkiviṭ ṭaṉaṉē
  toṉṉaci cālāmi naṉṉaṉ paṟampiṟ
  ciṟukā rōṭaṉ payiṉoṭu cērttiya
  kaṟpō ṉāviṉē ṉāki maṟṟatu
  ceppaleṉ maṉṉāl yāykkē naṟṟērk
  kaṭumpakaṭ ṭiyāṉic cōḻar marukaṉ
  neṭuṅkatir nelliṉ vallaṅ kiḻavōṉ
  nallaṭi yuḷḷā ṉākavum oṉṉār
  katava muyaṟalu muyalpa atāaṉṟu
  olipal kūntal namvayiṉ aruḷātu
  koṉṟaṉa ṉāyiṉum kolipa ḻutaṉṟē
  aruvi yāmpal kalitta muṉṟuṟi
  naṉṉaṉ āay piram paṉṉa
  miṉṉī rōti yeṉṉiniṉ kuṟippē" (akam .356)
eṉpatu,
iḵtu irantu piṉṉiṉṟa talimakaṟkut tōḻi tali makaḷik kuṟinayappak kūṟiyatu. aḵtāvatu tōḻiyi matiyuṭam paṭukku mukattāl talimakaṉ talimakaḷ kaṇṇāṟ kāṭṭiya tōḻiyi yaṇukit taṉ kuṟiyirantu niṟpa, avaḷ avaṟkuk kuṟinērntu talimakaḷiṭam pōntu, "yāṉ talimakaṉit teruviṭik kaṇṭēṉāka, avaṉ eṉ muṉki paṟṟa, yāṉ uṭaṉē aṉṉāy eṉṟu kūṟa, avaṉ eṉ kiviṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉaṉ; yāṉum ataṉi yāykku uṇarttiṉē ṉilli; avaṉ poruṭṭu niṉkuṟippu yātu? kūṟuka." eṉa avaḷ kuṟinayantu uṭampaṭumāṟu colliyatu.
itaṉporuḷ: miṉṉalpōla oṭuṅkiya kuḷirnta kūntali yuṭiyāy! mēlittuṟikkaṭ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟik kaḻuvutalāl, kīḻittuṟikkaṇ utirntu tappi, varuvaṉavaṟṟi yuṇṭaṟkut tiṟanta vāyi yuṭiya yāmi, kampuṭkōḻi pāṭumakaṉāka viḷaṅka, vārkkaṭ ṭaminta teḷinta kaṇṇi yuṭiya kiṇiyeṉṉum icikkaruvi pōlap piṟaḻum ūraṉ, neṭiya teruviṉiṭiyil, eṉṉik katumeṉak kaṇṭu eṉ poṉvaḷi yaṇinta muṉkiyip paṟṟiṉāṉāka, yāṉ añci "aṉṉāy" eṉak kūviṉēṉ; avaṉ uṭaṉē eṉkiyi viṭṭaṉaṉ; paḻiya viruppa mamiyāmiyiṉāl, naṉṉaṉatu paṟampiṉkaṇ ciṟiya paṇiyiṉāl arakkoṭu kūṭṭappaṭṭu aciyātu niṟkum kallippōl aciyāta nāviṉi yuṭiyēṉāy, yāykkum ceppā toḻintēṉ; ākavē, nalla tēriṉiyum virinta celaviṉiyuṭiya yāṉikaḷiyu muṭiya cōḻar kuṭiyō ṉākiya nīṇṭa katirkaḷiyuṭiya nelvayal mikka "vallam" eṉṉum ūrukkuriyōṉ piṟar taṉ nalla aṭiyi aṭitali niṉiyā ṉāyiṉum, pakivar, avaṉatu an neñcat tiṭpattik koḷḷātu avaṉ kōpikkattakuvaṉavaṟṟi muyalutalum  ceyvar; ataṉāl avaṉ avarkaḷiyē yaṉṟi, taḻitta palavākiya kūntaliyuṭiya nam pōlum makaḷirmāṭṭu maruḷceyyātu appakivarōṭu kolluvā ṉāyiṉum atu kuṟṟamākā taṉṟē. aruviyum āmpalum taḻitta muṉtuṟiyiṉiyuṭaya naṉṉaṉatu "āay pirampu" eṉṉum nakaratti yotta niṉ karuttu yātu? kūṟuka, eṉpatu.
kal, cāṇi, arakkum kallum kūṭṭicceyta kalli vēṟu pirikkavum acikkavum oṇṇāteṉṟu, "kaṟpōl nāviṉēṉ" eṉṟār. taṉṉi yaṭintārkku ātaravu ceyyum iyalpiṟṟā kaliṉ, "nallaṭi" eṉṟār.
oṉṉār avaṉ veṟuppaṉavaṟṟic ceytu vekuḷuvit talāl avaṉ avaric cekuttal kuṟṟamaṉ ṟākaliṉ, avar makaḷirik kōṟalum kuṟṟamaṉṟā meṉpāḷ "koṉṟaṉaṉāyiṉum koli paḻutaṉṟē" eṉṟāḷ. ummi, koṉṟatu, kuṟṟamē yeṉpatupaṭa niṉṟatu. oṉṉāratu nallaṭi yuḷḷāṉākavum, avar uḷḷiyatākak karuti avaṉ veṟuppaṉavaṟṟic ceyya muyalpa, ataṉāl avaṉ ciṉantu nampōl vāriṭattum aruḷceyyātu kōṟal kuṟṟamaṉṟu eṉṟum; nallaṭiyuḷḷiya kālattut tāṉ aruḷ ceytal vēṇṭumeṉpati niṉiyāṉākavum, pakivar avaṉ aruḷ ceyyat takuvaṉavaṟṟic ceytaliṉṟi vekuḷvaṉavaṟṟic  ceytaliṉ avaṉ ceyyum peṇkoli paḻutākāteṉṟumām.
"cōḻar marukaṉ" eṉṟataṉāl kuṭipperumiyiyum "nallaṭi yuḷḷāṉ" eṉṟataṉāl avaṉatu maṟamuṭimiyum nōkkātu, oṉṉār takātaṉavaṟṟic ceyya muyalvar; avaṉ avaṟṟik kāttaṟporuṭṭu mikka ceyalic ceytal kuṟṟamākā teṉpa tām; eṉavē, āṭavar tam kuṭipperumiyum āṇmiyum kāttaṟporuṭṭu makaḷirmāṭṭum aruḷ ceyyātu mikka ceyal ceyvārataliṉ, ūraṉum, tāṉ uḷḷiyatu peṟaṟ poruṭṭu maṭalēṟutal, varipāytal mutaliya mikka ceyalkaḷiyum ceyvaṉ; aḵtu avaṟkukkuṟṟamākā tākaliṉ, nām avaṉ vēṇṭum kuṟinērntu taṭuttal nam kaṭaṉ eṉpāḷ. "eṉṉi niṉ kuṟippu" eṉṟāḷ.
"iṭineṭun teruviṟ katumeṉak kaṇṭu, poṟṟoṭi muṉki paṟṟiṉaṉ" eṉṟataṉāl talivaṉ irantupiṉṉiṉṟatum, "kaṟpōl nāviṉēṉāki maṟṟatu, ceppaleṉ maṉṉālyāykkē" eṉṟataṉāl tāṉ avaṟkuk kuṟinērntamiyum, "eṉṉi niṉ kuṟippē" eṉṟataṉāl kuṟinayakkumāṟum, kūṟiṉāḷām. meyppāṭu: taṉkaṇ tōṉṟiya icimi poruḷākap piṟanta perumitam. payaṉ: talimakaḷ kēṭṭuk kuṟi nayappāḷāvatu.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ taṉ pāḻinakarattiṉkaṭ poruḷ vittu vēḷir palar kāppac ceyta ceyti kūṟum pāṭṭu,
naṉṉaṉ utiyaṉ aruṅkaṭip pāḻit
toṉmutir vēḷir ōmpiṉar vitta
poṉṉiṉum aruminaṟ kaṟintu maṉṉōṭ
ṭuṉṉala mātō veṉiṉumaḵ tollāy
taṇmaḻi tavaḻun tāḻnīr naṉantalik
kaṭuṅkāṟ ṟeṭukku neṭumperuṅ kuṉṟattu
māya viruḷaḷi māykaṟ pōla
māykatil vāḻiya neñcē nāḷum
melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ nallaka nacii
yaraviri tēru mañcuvaru ciṟuneṟi
yiravi ṉeytiyum peṟāa yaruḷvarap
pulleṉ kaṇṇi pulampukoṇ ṭulakat
tuḷḷōrk kellām perunaki yākak
kāmam kimmika vuṟutara
vāṉā varumpaṭar talittan tōyē," 	   (akam. 258)
eṉpatu 
iḵtu allakuṟippaṭṭup patippeyarnta talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.
itaṉporuḷ:- neñcē! naṉṉaṉākiya utiyaṉ kiṭṭutaṟkariya kāvalaminta pāḻi yeṉṉum nakariṉkaṇ mikavum tollō rākiya vēḷir palar kāval ceyya vitta poṉṉiṉum ivaḷ (talimakaḷ) peṟutaṟ kariyaḷātalit teḷiya aṟintu vittum, avaḷi neruṅkā toḻikuvam eṉṟālum, amiyātu, varuttukiṉṟaṉi; mēlum, nāṭōṟum meṉmiyāṉa iyal paminta ciṟiyavaḷuṭiya nalla maṉiyi virumpi, pāmpukaḷ tamakkuriya iriyit tēṭukiṉṟa accam poruntiya iraviṟ ceṉṟum, avaḷatu kūṭṭattip peṟṟāyilli; kaṇṭōr neñcattu irakkam eytumāṟu pulleṉṟa kaṇṇiyuṭiyi yāyt taṉimiyuṟṟu varunti, ulakatti luḷḷavarkaḷellārum kaṇṭu nakiceyyum vakiyiṟ kāmam peruki varuttutalāl amiyāta taṭuttaṟkariya nōyi mikat tantaṉiyātalāl, kuḷirnta mēkaṅkaḷ tavaḻap peyta maḻinīr tāḻntōṭa, ataṉiṭiyil, peruṅkāṟ ṟeḻuntu cavaṭṭum neṭiya maliyiṉ mikak karutta iruḷ niṟinta muḻiñciṉuḷ viḻuntu maṟikiṉṟa kaṟpōla nīyum viḻuntu māyvāyāka, eṉpatu.
neñcē, utiyaṉ pāḻiyiṉkaṇ vitta poṉṉiṉum arumi naṉkaṟintu vittum, eṉiṉum, ollāyāy, nallakam nacii, neṟiyi iraviṉ eytiyum, peṟātoḻintaṉi; mēlum, pulleṉ kaṇṇiyāyp pulampukoṇṭu nakiyāka, kāmam kimmika vuṟutara, talittantōy ātalāl māykal pōla māyvāyāka eṉa viyiyum.
'naṉṉaṉ utiyaṉ aruṅkaṭip pāḻit, toṉmutir vēḷir ōmpiṉar vitta poṉṉiṉum arumi" eṉṟataṉāl tali makaḷic ceṉṟu talippeyaṟkuk kūṭāvaki amintuḷḷa kāvalum, ataṉik kaṭattaṟ karumiyum viḷakkiya vāṟām. kūṭṭam iṭiyīṭupaṭṭu eytap peṟāmiyiṉ, "aṉṉōḷ tuṉṉalam" eṉṟum, piṉṉart talimakaḷāl avviṭiyīṭey tāmi yuṇarntaviṭattu, av voḻukkatti ṉiṭattē uḷḷam cēṟaliṉ, "aḵtollāy, nāḷum............ iraviṉ eytiyum" eṉṟum, viṉimēṟ koṇṭavaṉ, iṭiyi luḷavām iṭiyīṭu kaḷāl maṉamuṭintu pulampukoṇṭu maṭaṅkuvāṉāyiṉ, aṟivum māṉamu milaṉeṉa ulakattār ikaḻutaliṉ "ulakat tuḷḷōrk kellām perunakiyāka" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ.
"araviri tēru mañcuvaru ciṟuneṟi, iraviṉ eytiyum" eṉṟataṉāl, muṟpōntu avaḷatu tuṉṉaṟ karumiyuṇarntatu pakaṟkuṟi yiṭiyīṭupaṭṭa teṉpatām. eṉavē, talimakaṉ iraviṉum pakaliṉum kuṟivaḻi yoḻuki iṭiyīṭupaṭṭuk kūṭṭam peṟāmiyiṉ, kalakkamuṟṟup pulampukoṇṭu neñciṉi neruṅkik kūṟuvā ṉāyiṉaṉ eṉavaṟika.
"iṉpatti veṟuttal" (tol. po. 270) eṉṟa meyp pāṭṭiyaṟ cūttirattu "nalattaka nāṭiṟ kalakkamu matuvē" eṉṟatu koṇṭu, "ivvāṟu kūṟavē iccūttirattu ḷōtiya irupatu meyppāṭun talimakaṟkum ēṟṟavakiyāṉē koḷḷappaṭum" eṉpavākaliṉ, itaṉkaṇ  "araviritērum añcuvaru ciṟuneṟi" yeṉṟatu ētamāytal, "pulleṉ kaṇṇi pulampukoṇṭu" eṉṟatu āṅku neñcaḻital. "ulakattu, uḷḷōrk kellām perunakiyākak, kāmam kimmika vuṟutara, āṉā varumpaṭar talit tantōy" eṉṟatu uṭampu naṉi curuṅkalum kalakkamumām. ēṉi meyppāṭu; aḻukiyic cārnta iḷivaral payaṉ neñcoṭu nērtal.
piṉvarum pāṭṭum ipporuṭṭē yākum.
arunteṟal marapiṉ kaṭavuḷ kāppap
peruntēṉ ṟūṅku nāṭukā ṇaṉantali
yaṇaṅkuṭi varippiṟ pāḻi yāṅkaṇ
vēṇmutu mākkaḷ viyaṉakark karanta
varuṅkala veṟukkiyi ṉariyōḷ paṇpuniṉintu
varuntiṉa mātō veṉiṉumaḵ tollāy
irumpaṇit toṭutta palarā ṭūca
lūrntiḻi kayiṟṟiṟ celavara varunti
neṭuneṟik kutirik kūrvē lañci
kaṭumuṉi yalitta koṭuvi lāṭavar
ākoḷ pūcaliṟ pāṭuciṟan teṟiyum
peruntuṭi vaḷpiṉ vīṅkupu nekiḻā
mēymaṇi yiḻanta pāmpiṉ nīnaṉi
tēmpiṉi vāḻiyeṉ ṉeñcē vēntar
koṇṭaṇi yeyiliṟ kāppuc ciṟan
tīṇṭaruṅ kuriyaḷnam maṇaṅki yōḷē.     
ituvumatu.   			(akam. 372)
uri: eṉ neñcē! taṭuttaṟkariya teṟutaṟ ṟoḻilic ceyyum muṟimiyiṉi yuṭiya teyvam niṉṟu kāttalic ceyya, periya tēṉaṭikaḷ tūṅkum koṇkāṉanāṭṭiṉ iṭiyilirukkum teyva makaḷiriyuṭiya maliyic cārnta pāḻiyeṉṉum nakariṉkaṇ vēḷirākiya mutukuṭi. makkaḷ akaṉṟa pērakatti ṉiṭattē maṟittuvit tōmpiya ariyavākiya poṉṉum maṇiyum pōlap peṟutaṟ kariya talimakaḷatu arumip paṇpiṉiniṉittu, "iṉi ivaḷippeṟēm" eṉa veṇṇi varuttamuṟuvē māyiṉēm eṉṟu kūṟiyum. nī atu kēṭ ṭamiyā yāyiṉi; periya mūṅkiliṟ piṇitta palarāṭum ūcaliṉatu ēṟiyum iṟaṅkiyum varum kayiṟṟip pōla vaṟitē cellutal varutal eṉṟa toḻilkaṭ kañci, nīṇṭa vaḻiyiṉi yuṭiya kutirimalikku uriyavaṉāṉa kūriya vēlēntiya añci yeṉpavaṉatu accam poruntiya pōrmuṉikkaṇ niṉṟu pakivari varuttiya vaḷinta villēntiya vīrar pakippulattu āviṉik koḷḷum pōriṉkaṇ olimikku eṟiyap paṭum periya tuṭiyiṉ vārippōla varuttattāl vimmi nekiḻntu, mēytal kāraṇamāka vumiḻnta maṇiyi yiḻantu taṭumāṟum pāmpip pōla nī mikavum kalakka muṟṟu varuntiṉi nammi varuttiya avaḷ, vēntar pakippulattē veṉṟu kavarntu valiyākiya aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭa matilip pōlak kāval mikkuṟṟu neruṅkutaṟku ariyaḷāyiṉāḷ, kāṇ eṉpatu.
eṉ neñcē! pāḻiyiṉkaṭ karanta veṟukkiyiṉ ari yōḷiṉ paṇpu niṉintu nām varuntiṉa meṉiṉum, aḵtol lāyāy, ūcaṟkayiṟṟip pōlac celavuvaravukaṭku añci varunti, vīṅki nekiḻntu, mēymaṇi yiḻanta nākam pōla nī tēmpiṉi; nam aṇaṅkiyōḷ, kāppuc ciṟattalāl īṇṭutaṟ kariyaḷāyiṉāḷ eṉa viyiyum.
pakivariṭam kavarntu koḷḷappaṭṭa eyil, cīrkēṭuṟṟa pakuti muṟṟum cīrceyyappaṭṭu ap pakivar mīṭṭum neruṅkik kippaṟṟāvakik kāvalamikkappaṭutal pōlat taliviyoṭu nām oḻukum oḻukkam, peṟṟōrāl uṇarappaṭṭu avaḷ veḷippōtā vaki iṟceṟikkap paṭṭaḷātaliṉ iṉi nām aṇukum pāṉmi yilaḷāyiṉāḷ eṉṟum, mēlē "aruṅkala veṟukkiyi ṉariyōḷ" eṉṟataṟ kēṟpa, "mēymaṇi yiḻanta" eṉṟum, mēymaṇi yiḻanta nākam piṉṉar uy vaḻiyiṉṟi iṟattal pōlat talimakaḷik kūṭutali yiḻanta nī tēmpip piṟitupaṭuvā yeṉa iraṅkuvāṉ, "vāḻi" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. tuṭiyiṉ vaḷpu aṭikkappaṭuṅkāl vimmutalum aḵtoḻinta viṭattu nekiḻ talum pōla neñcam kūṭṭam eytiyavaḻik kaḷippāl vimmiyum, eytāvaḻik kalaṅki nekiḻntum niṟṟaliṉ, peruntuṭi vaḷpiṉ "vīṅkupu nekiḻā" eṉṟār.
kuṟiñcit tiṇikkēṟpa ataṉ puṟaṉāya veṭci kūṟuvār, "koṭuvil āṭavar, ākoḷpūcal" kūṟiṉār.
palarāṭum ūcal irumpaṇikkaṇ toṭukkappaṭṭa teṉṟa miyāṉ, talimakaṉ neñcam talimakaḷ tōḷiṟ piṇikkap paṭṭa teṉa oru nayantōṉṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam tali makaṉip piṇittaṟkut tōḷ iṭamātal, "piṭipayiṉṟu tarūum peruṅkaḷiṟu pōlat, tōḷkan tākak kūntaliṟ piṇittu" (akam. 276) eṉa varutal kāṇka. meyppāṭum payaṉu mavi.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ piṇṭaṉ mutaliya vēntar palari veṉṟu avar makaḷirtam kūntalāṟ kayiṟutirittu, avartam aruṅkalam koṇarnta ceytiyik kūṟum pāṭṭu,
"taṭantāḷ tāḻik kuṭampi nōṉāt
  taṇṭali kamaḻum vaṇṭupaṭu nāṟṟat
  tiruḷpuri kūntaṟ poṅkutuka ḷāṭi
  yuruḷpoli pōla vemmuṉi varunta
  vaṇittaki yallatu piṇittal tēṟṟāp
  peruntōṭ celvat tivaḷiṉu mellā
  veṟperi taḷittaṉi nīyē poṟpuṭi
  viriyuḷip polinta pariyuṭi naṉmāṉ
  vēnta rōṭṭiya vēntuvēl naṉṉaṉ
  kūntal muraṟciyiṟ koṭitē
  maṟappaṉmā tōniṉ viṟaṟṟaki miyē," 	       (naṟ. 270)
eṉpatu. itu tōḻi vāyil nērkiṉṟāḷ talimakaṉi neruṅkic colli vāyil etirkoṇṭatu, uṭaṉilik kiḷavi vakiyāl.
uri: *ēṭā! nī pirintakkāl periya tūṟṟiṉiyuṭiya tāḻip putaliṉālē kaṭṭappaṭṭa ematu ciṟiya vīṭṭiṉ kaṇṇē poṟukka muṭiyātapaṭi, cōliyiluḷḷa puṉṉi mutalākiya vaṟṟiṉ malari muṭittalāl vaṇṭukaḷ moykkiṉṟa naṟunāṟṟattiyuṭiya iruḷotta kūntalil mikka tukaḷ paṭiya nilattilē puraṇṭu cāyntāṟpōlāka emmuṉṉēvaruntu tali yuṭiyaḷāki aḻakaḻinta taṉmi yoṉṟallātu niṉṉi vacamākkutalit teḷiyāta periyatōḷiyuṭiya cel vamakaḷākiya ivaḷiṉuṅ kāṭṭil eṉṉip peritum nī aṉpucey toḻukā niṉṟaṉi: aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum nī pirintu ceṉṟatāṉatu aḻaku poruntiya virinta piṭarimayir polivuṟṟa virinta celaviṉiyuṭiya nalla kutirip paṭikaḷiyuṭiya pakiyaraca rākiya piṇṭaṉ mutalāṉōrip pōrilē tōṟṟōṭac ceyta ēntiya vēṟpaṭiyiyuṭiya naṉṉaṉ, tāṉ appakiyaracariṉ urimi makaḷirip paṟṟivantu avar taliyi maḻittu ak kūntalik kayiṟākat tirittu, akkayiṟṟālē appakivariṉ yāṉiyip piṇitta koṭumiyiṉum, koṭiyatāy irāniṉṟatu. ātalāl niṉatu valiya takutip pāṭṭiṉi yāṉmaṟantē viṭu kiṉṟēṉ, kāṇ, eṉpatu.
taliviyuṭaṉ etirkoṇṭamiyāl uṭaṉilik kiḷavi yāyiṟṟu. ivaḷeṉac cuṭṭiniṉ ṟamiyiṉ, uṭaṉiṉṟami teḷika.
pulavitīrkkum vāyilāka yāṉ amiyātavaḻi eṉṉuṭa ṉum pēcātu veṟuttal ceyvāy, amintamiyāl eṉṉuṭaṉ pēcuvāyāyiṉi eṉṟu koṇṭu, "eṟperi taḷittaṉi" eṉṟāḷ. meyppāṭu: uvakiyic cārnta perumitam. payaṉ: vāyil nērtal.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ peṇkoli purinta ceyti kūṟum pāṭṭu.
"maṇṇiya ceṉṟa voṇṇuta larivi
  puṉaltaru pacuṅkāy tiṉṟataṉ ṟappaṟ
  koṉpatiṟ ṟoṉpatu kaḷiṟṟo ṭavaṇiṟi
  poṉcey pāvi koṭuppavuṅ koḷḷāṉ
  peṇkoli purinta naṉṉaṉ pōla
  variyā nirayattuc celīiyarō aṉṉi
  orunāḷ, nakimuka viruntiṉaṉ vanteṉap
  pakimuka vūriṟ ṟuñcalō vilaḷē"		 (kuṟu. 292)
eṉpatu.
itu, tōḻi iravukkuṟikkaṇ ciṟippuṟamākak kāppu mikuti kūṟiyatu.
uri: *kuḷittaṟkuc ceṉṟa arivi, puṉalkoṇarnta piḻikkāka avaḷ tamar eṇpattorukaḷiṟṟōṭu avaḷ niṟiyuḷḷa poṟpāviyik koṭuppavum koḷḷāṉāy, appeṇṇik kolipurintiṭṭa naṉṉaṉip pōla nīkkāta nirayattup pōvāḷāka aṉṉi; orunāḷ viruntiṉaṉāka vantataṉāl pakiyi  mukattiṟkāṭṭum tuyilutal ilaḷāyiṉāḷ ākalāṉ. e-ṟu
paṟittilaḷ; paḻamumaṉṟu; avaṉatēyeṉṉum tuṇivum illi; ivaḷ eṭātoḻiyiṉ avaṟkē kiṭittalum aiya muṭiyatu eṉumivi pulappaṭa "puṉaltaru pacuṅkāy" eṉap paṭṭatu. meyppāṭu: accattic cārnta perumitam. payaṉ: iṭi yūṟuṇarttal.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ taṉ paṭittalivaṉ miñili yeṉpāṉatu tanti aṉṉiyeṉpavaṉip pōril veṉṟu avaṉ kaṇkaḷik kaḷintami kāraṇamāka, kōcarkaḷiveṉṟu avar tam nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ turattum ceyti kūṟumpāṭṭu,
"makiḻnaṉ mārpē veyyi yālnī
 aḻiyal vāḻi tōḻi naṉṉaṉ
 naṟumā koṉṟu nāṭṭiṟ pōkkiya
 oṉṟu moḻik kōcar pōla
 vaṉkaṭ cūḻcciyum vēṇṭumāṟ ciṟitē"
eṉpatu.   					           (kuṟun. 73)
itu pakaṟkuṟi maṟuttu, iravukkuṟi nērntu ataṉiyum maṟut tamipaṭat talimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.
uri: tōḻi! nī makiḻ naṉatu mārpiyē virumpuvāyāyiṉi yēyaṉṟi, kuṟivaḻi yoḻukutalāl ivvoḻukkam piṟarāl uṇarappaṭiṉ varum ētattiṉi yārāyāmiyiṉ, tāṉ avaṉi iruvakik kuṟiyum maṟuttaliṉ, iṉik kūṭuṅkolōveṉa maṉam aḻuṅkātoḻika; naṉṉaṉ eṉpāṉ, vaṉmi poruntiya kōcar kaḷiṉ kāval maramākiya naṟiya marattiṉittaṭintu avar tām muṉṉar iruntu pōntara nāṭṭuṭ puṟan tantupōkap poṟātavaḻi, akkōcarkku tēṭiyirunta vaṉkaṇmi mikka cūḻcciyiṟ ciṟitēṉum tām vēṇṭumākālāṉ. eṉpatu.
veyyi, vemmiyeṉṉum uriccollaṭiyākap piṟanta kuṟippu muṟṟu. "vemmi vēṇṭal" eṉpa (tol. col. uri. 26) oṉṟumoḻi -maṉattoṭu poruntiya māṟupaṭāta col. naṉṉaṉ, pōkkiya, kōcar eṉa viyiyum. naṉṉaṉāṟṟurattap paṭṭa kōcar eṉpatām. kōcar oṉṟu moḻiyuṭiyareṉpatu, "oṉṟu moḻik kōcarkkoṉṟu muraṇ pōkiya, kaṭuntērt titiyaṉ (akam. 196) eṉpataṉālumuṇarka. "iḷampal kōcar viḷaṅkupaṭikaṉmār, ikaliṉareṟinta vakalili murukkiṉ, perumarak kampam pōlap, porunarkkuliyā niṉvalaṉ" (puṟam. 169) eṉpataṉāl ivar paṭip payiṟci mikutiyu muṭiyar eṉpatu peṟutum.
ikkōcar tāmum tam naṉmoḻiyālum, paṭip payiṟci yālum tammi viyantu, vaṉkaṇmiyum cūḻcciyum ilarā yoḻiya, naṉṉaṉ avi yiraṇṭu muṭiyaṉāy ivariveṉṟā ṉeṉavē, nīyummakiḻnaṉ mārpē virumpi, avaṉ pirivāṟṟātu kalaṅki, ivvoḻukkattā ṉuḷatākum ētatti niṉiyā tiruntaṉiyeṉṟum, akkōcar tāmum vaṉkaṭcūṭciyuṭiya rāyiṉ naṉṉaṉ veṉṟiyeytutalākātavāṟupōla, nāmum vaṉkaṇmiyum cūḻcciyum koḷḷiṉ, oḻukkattāluḷavākum ētaṅkaḷi vārāmaṟ kākkalām eṉṟum, ivvāṟu namnalaṅ karutiyē ceyya vēṇṭutaliṉ, nī aḻuṅkutal kūṭātu eṉpāḷ, "aḻiyal" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
pakaṟkuṟi varuvāṉi, atumaṟuttu iravukkuṟi nērntum, avvaḻi varuvāṉi maṟuttum alittaṟku vēṇṭappaṭutaliṉ "vaṉkaṇmiyum," kāppu mikuti kūṟiyum, paṭittumoḻi yāṉum piṟavāṟṟāṉum talimakaṉit teruṭṭi varivukaṭā taliṉ "cūḻcciyum",  ivi tāmum mikka vaḻit talimakaṉ āṟṟāṉāy piṟitu paṭuvaṉākaliṉ, "vēṇṭumāl ciṟitē" eṉṟum, ivaṟṟik kiyāḷumiṭattu avaṉāṟṟāmikaṇṭu talimakaḷu māṟṟāḷāvaḷkol eṉṉumeṇṇattiṉaḷākaliṉ, "aḻiyal" eṉṟataṉōṭoḻiyātu "vāḻi" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
"makiḻnaṉ mārpē veyyi" eṉṟatu kimmikalum niṉi talumām. "aḻiyal" eṉṟatu naṭukkam. immeyppāṭukaḷ, "iviyumuḷavē aviyalaṅkaṭiyē" (tol. po. 260) eṉa vantaṉavām. ēṉi meyppāṭu iḷivaralic cārnta perumitam. payaṉ: talimakaḷi yāṟṟuvittal.
iṉi, itaṟku "naṉṉaṉatu ciṟanta yāṉiyik koṉṟu avaṉatu nāṭṭiṟ pōkaviṭṭa vāymiporuntiya colliṉi yuṭiya kōcar" *eṉṟu itaṟku itaṉ urikārar kūṟukiṉṟār. naṟumā eṉṟaṟku yāṉiyeṉap poruḷ kōṭalākāmiyālum, koṉṟa yāṉiyi mīṭṭum nāṭṭiṉ kaṭpōkkutal ākāmi yālum, kōcarkaḷ iṉṉōraṉṉa ceyal ceytuḷareṉaṟku vēṟu cāṉṟiṉmiyālum, yām kūṟiyaporuḷi yēṟṟalāṟ kavip poruṭ ciṟattalālum aḵtu uriyaṉmiyaṟika.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, naṉṉaṉatu varalāṟum, atu paṟṟi āciriyar paraṇar pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ nalaṉum peṟappaṭṭaṉavām.
25. cintāmaṇiyiṉ centamiḻ nalam
centamiḻ nalam paravivarum ikkālattil, poḻilaṉpar kaḷukkuc cintāmaṇiyip paṟṟi muṉṉuri yātēṉum kūṟal, vēṇṭā kūṟalākum. āyiṉum kaṟpaṉik kaḷañciyamākiya tuṟimaṅkalam civappirakācar mutaliya civa naṉmakkaḷum, pulavar perumakkaḷum, itaṟkuc "cīvaka cintāmaṇi" yeṉṟa peyar irukkavum, cintāmaṇi yeṉṟu ciṟappak kūṟum ciṟappuṭiya tākaliṉ, ikkaṭṭurik kēṟpavēṉum ciṟitu kūṟuvatu vēṇṭu vatoṉṟēyām.
cīvakacintāmaṇi eṉpatu nam centamiḻ moḻiyiṉ nantāc celvamāy viḷaṅkum kāviyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. itaṉ talimakaṉ cīvakaṉ eṉpavaṉ. ivaṉ palamakaḷiri maṇantu muṭivil tuṟavu pūṇṭa aracakumaraṉ. iccentamiḻ nūl tiruttakkatēvar eṉṟa camaṇa muṉivar oruvarāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatu. itaṉi accēṟṟit tamiḻulakiṟku utaviya perumi ṭākṭar. uyartiru. u.vē. cuvāmi nātayyar eṉṉum centamiḻc ciṟappuṭi antamil varalāṟu, palavum nilavum niliyamāy, nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulat tamiḻmāṇ pulamiyoṭu iṉmoḻi vaḻaṅkum cemmalāy vaḻaṅkum perumakaṉārāvar avarkaṭku, ivvutavipaṟṟit taṇṭamiḻ ulaku eṉṟum naṉṟi celuttum kaṭappāṭṭiṉāl ivar neṟikkaṇ aṭittoṇṭu puriyum aṟam uṭittu.
innūṟkaṇ, kaṟpārmaṉattil centamiḻiṉ tīviya nalat tit tērntu nukartaṟkuriya pakutikaḷ mikappalavākum. avaṟṟit tērntu nukarum neṟikaḷum palavākum. āciriyar uyartiru. a.varatanañciyappiḷḷi mutaliya aruntamiḻc cāṉṟōr tam pāl ārac ciṟantu niṟkum matinuṭpattāl ovvoru kaviyiṉiyum ārāyntu kāṭṭum urimiyuṭiyōrāyuḷar. uccimēṟ pulavarkoḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar utaviya nalluriyiṉṟēl, itaṉ centamiḻ nalam iṉitu viḷaṅkiyirātu eṉṟu palarum karutuvar.
itaṉi ārāytaṟku virumpiya māṇavar cilarkku itaṉi urittuvarukiyil, avar cīvakaṉāl maṇakkappeṟum makaḷiruḷ, kuṇamāli, curamañcari eṉṟa iruvaruṭiya kuṇanalam paṟṟit tammuṭ collāṭip piṇaṅkuvārāyiṉar. avar poruṭṭu ivvārāycci ceyyap peṟṟamiyiṉ, ikkaṭṭurikkaṇ ivviruvaruṭiya varalāṟṟiṉi ārāyuṅkāl tōṉṟum centamiḻt tīñcuvi nalatti nam tamiḻppoḻil aṉparākiya "tēṉūṟa niṉṟu teruṇṭār" tirumuṉ ceppaluṟukiṉṟēṉ.
cīvaka cintāmaṇi yeṉṉum ikkāviyattuk kati nikaḻccikku nilikkaḷam ēmāṅkatam eṉṉum iciyāl ticipōkiya nāṭākum. itaṟku irācamāpuram eṉpatu talinakaram. inna karattē kantukkaṭaṉ eṉṉum cīriyōṉ celvamaṉiyil cīvakaṉ, kalvi kēḷvikaḷil oṉmiyum, paṭiyāṇmiyil vaṉmiyum peṟṟuc ciṟantu vāḻntuvarukiṉṟāṉ. aṅkē kupēramittiraṉ eṉṉum celvaṉ taṉ maṉivi viyimāli yoṭukūṭi ilvāḻ pavaṉ kuṇamāliyeṉṉum naṅkiyip payantu vaḷarttu varukiṉṟāṉ. avaḷum tirumaṇattukkuriya paruva muṭiyaḷāy, naṟkuṇa naṟceykiyiṟ ciṟanta naṅkiyāy vāḻntu varukiṉṟāḷ. annakarattē kupēratattaṉ eṉṉum tirumikkōṉ irukkiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ maṉivipeyar cumati eṉpatu. avviru varum kūṭippeṟṟa kātaliṉpap payaṉē curamañcari yeṉṉum peṇṇaṇaṅku. kuṇamāliyum curamañcariyum otta uruvum, tiruvum, uyarnilikkalviyum vāykkap peṟṟa oṇṭoṭiyar.
paṇṭikkālattil nam nāṭaṭuccelvar vēṉiṟkālattu nakariṉ nīṅki, yāṟum, kuḷamum, kāvum pukku nīrāṭiyum, poḻil viḷiyāṭiyum pōkanukartal marapaṉṟō? "yāṟum kuḷaṉum kāvum āṭippatiyikantu nukartalum uriya eṉpa" eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum kūṟukiṉṟāraṉṟō? ammuṟiyē, ēmāṅkata nāṭṭilum nikaḻvatu marapu. annakara māntarum, vēṉiṟ kālattup poḻilāṭalum nīrāṭalum purintu pōkanukartali mēvukiṉṟaṉar. akkālattil iḷamakaḷir tām peṟṟa kalvip payaṉāl, cuṇṇap poṭi ceytal iyalpu. ammuṟiyē vēṉiṟkālattil, poḻil viḷiyāṭalum nīr viḷiyāṭalum nayanta nakaramāntaruṭaṉ, kuṇamāliyum curamañcariyum tām ceyta cuṇṇattōṭu poḻiliṉkaṇ ceṉṟu taṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
iruvarum iṉpamāka uriyāṭiyirunta kālattil, kuṇa māliyiṉ tōḻi, māli yeṉpavaḷ, kuṇamāli ceyta cuṇṇatti, avaḷ kuṟippiṉpaṭi, curamañcarikkuk kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉaḷ. avaḷ taṉ cuṇṇattiṉum ciṟantataṉṟu eṉṟu kuṟittu nakiyāṭukiṉṟaṉaḷ. atu kēṭṭa kuṇamāli, "taṉ cuṇṇattiṉ mikka cuṇṇam peṟalaritu; oppaṉavuḷavāyiṉ avi cilavēyām; accilavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟāka vēṇṭumāyiṉ, nī kūṟik koḷka" eṉ kiṉṟāḷ. itaṉāl ivviruvarkkum maṉa vēṟupāṭu uṇṭākiṟatu. muṭivil, iruvar cuṇṇattiyum aṟintārpāl kāṭṭi, mikkatu tuṇital naṉṟeṉṟu tuṇintu, iruvarum tattam cuṇṇaṅkaḷit tōḻiyarpāl tantu aṉuppukiṉṟaṉar. tōḻiyar iruvarum, takkōr cilariyaṇukit tam karutti aṟivikkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ, "ittuṟiyilum piṟavaṟṟilum vallunaṉ cīvakaṉē; avaṉpāṟ kāṭṭit teḷintu koṇmiṉ," eṉkiṉṟaṉar. eṉṟalum, avarkaḷ, appoḻilakattē orupāl taṉ tōḻar cūḻavīṟṟirunta cīvakaṉi yaṭintu tam karuttit terivikkiṉṟaṉar. cīvakaṉ, iruvar cuṇṇaṅkaḷiyum pārttu, "kuṇamāli ceyta cuṇṇamē cīritu" eṉakkūṟi, ataṉit tuṇivataṟkāka vaṇṭukaḷi yaḻittu, avaṟṟiṉ muṉ iruvar cuṇṇaṅkaḷiyum aḷḷit tūvukiṟāṉ. avi, kuṇamāli cuṇṇatti uṇṭoḻikiṉṟaṉa; curamañcariyiṉ cuṇṇam tariyil viḻukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl kuṇamāli veṟṟiyuṟu kiṉṟāḷ; curamañcari tōlviyuṟukiṉṟāḷ.
cuṇṇattāl tōlviyuṟṟu āṟṟāḷākiya curamañcari, tāṉ ceyta cūḷuriyiṉpaṭi nīrāṭalākiya viḷiyāṭṭiṉi virum pāmal, nīṅkic ceṉṟu, taṉ maṉiyakam pukuntu taṉ cuṇṇattit tīteṉṟu kūṟit taṉṉi avalam uṟuvitta antac cīvakaṉ tāṉākavē valiyavantu taṉ cilampaṭi kitoḻumāṟu nōṟpēṉ eṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu kaṉṉimāṭam camippittu, āṭavar kuṟukā aruṅkaṭi amittu, āṇṭēvatitali mēṟ koḷḷukiṉṟāḷ.
ippāl, curamañcariyiṉ pirivāl varunti aritutēṟic ceyvaṉaceytu teḷinta kuṇamāli, nakaramāntarōṭu kūṭit tāṉum nīrviḷiyāṭṭiṉi iṉitu ayarkiṉṟāḷ. cīvakaṉum tōḻarum nakaramāntar viḷiyāṭum kāṭciyiṉip pārttuk koṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. piṉpu, viḷiyāṭṭu muṭintatum, makkaḷ aṉivarum nakarkkut tirumpukiṉṟaṉar. atupōtu, acaṉivēkam eṉṉum paṭṭattu yāṉi mataṅkoṇṭu piḷiṟi varukiṟatu. yāvarum talittali mayaṅkic citaṟi ōṭukiṉṟaṉar. kuṇamāli matimaruṇṭu niṉṟu tavikkiṉṟāḷ. acaṉivēkamum avaḷik kolvatu kuṟittu aṇukukiṉṟatu. atukaṇṭu pēraccaṅkoṇṭa avaḷ tōḻi, māli yeṉpavaḷ, avaṭku muṉṉē niṉṟu, "āṭavar illiyō?" eṉṟu alaṟi araṟṟukiṉṟāḷ. aḵtu aṅku vantukoṇṭirunta cīvakaṉ ceviyuṟ pukutalum, avaṉ virintu pāyntu, avyāṉiyit takintu piṟitorupakkam pukuvippāṉ, avaḷi uyirtappuvikkiṉṟāṉ.
tappittēṟiya kuṇamāli, taṉṉi uyvitta vaḷḷaliṉ mēṉinalamum kuṇanalamum kaṇkaḷāṟ kaṇṭu, avaṉiyē maṇakka vēṇṭum eṉṉum kuṟippiṉaḷāy, maṉiyakam ceṉṟu, avaṉ poruṭṭu melikiṉṟāḷ. tāṉ pēṇivaḷartta kiḷiyiṉ pāl taṉkātali orumuṭaṅkalil varintutantu, cīvakaṉiṭam pōkku kiṉṟāḷ. ivaḷip pōlavē, ivaḷpāl eḻunta kātalāl veytuṟṟu varuntum cīvakaṉ, ammuṭaṅkalip peṟṟut, taṉ uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉiyum terivikkiṉṟāṉ. itaṟkiṭiyē, kuṇa māliyi avaḷ mittuṉaṉ oruvaṟku maṇam pēcukiṉṟaṉar. avaḷ ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭukiṉṟilaḷ. kuṇamāliyiṉ maṉakkuṟippuc cīvakaṉpāl uḷateṉat terintu, peṟṟōr, piṉpu maṇampēci, muṟippaṭic cīvakaṉukkut tirumaṇam purivikkiṉṟaṉar. maṇam puṇarnta iruvarum perukiya iṉpam tuyttu iṉitirukkiṉṟaṉar.
iḵtu ivvāṟāka, cīvakaṉāl takippuṇṭa acaṉivēkam eṉṉum aracuvā, kavaḷamutaliyaṉa koḷḷātu melintu varukiṟatu. atukaṇṭa kaṭṭiyaṅkāraṉ eṉṉum annakara vēntaṉ, kāraṇam viṉava, pākarkaḷ poḻil viḷiyāṭṭil nikaḻntatu kūṟu kiṉṟaṉar. avaṉ, ciṉam mikuntu, taṉ vīrari yēvi, "accīvakaṉik koṇarka" eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭukiṟāṉ. vīrarkaḷ kuṇamāliyōṭu irunta illattic cūḻntu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. cīvakaṉ pōrk kōlaṅ koṇṭu veḷi varukiṟāṉ. ak kāli avaṉ peṟṟōr vantu, "aracaṉukku aṭaṅkiyoḻukutal aṟam eṉṟu kūṟi avaṉi aṭaṅkic cellu māṟu vēṇṭukiṉṟaṉar. icceyti kāntaruvatatti eṉṉum cīvakaṉuṭiya mutal maṉivikku eṭṭukiṟatu. avaḷ taṉ kaṇa vaṉik kāttaṟ poruṭṭut teyvaṅkaḷi vēṇṭukiṉṟāḷ. avitāmum vantu avaḷic cūḻntu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉic cīvakaṉ aṟintu cutañcaṇaṉ eṉṉum taṉ naṇpaṉ oruvaṉi niṉikkiṉṟāṉ avaṉ  virintu maḻiyōṭum miṉṉalōṭum pōntu cīvakaṉi vāṉāṟākat tāṉvāḻum patikkuk koṇṭu celkiṉṟāṉ. vīrarkaḷ cīvakaṉik kāṇāmal, vēṟoruvaṉik koṉṟu viṭṭatākap poykūṟi oḻikiṉṟaṉar. kaṭṭiyaṅkāraṉum atukēṭṭu makiḻntu pōkiṟāṉ.
ikkūṟiya ceyti muṟṟum cīvakacintāmaṇiyil, kuṇamāli yār ilampakam eṉṉum pakutiyil viriyak kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu. cīvakaṉ curamañcariyi maṇantakati curamañcariyār ilampakam eṉṉum pakutikkaṇ virikkap peṟṟuḷatu. ataṉ curukkam varumāṟu:-
cutañcaṇaṉ eṉṉum tēvaṉāl koṇṭucellappaṭṭa cīvakaṉ, avaṉpāl cilanāḷ taṅkip piṉ viṭipeṟṟu, pallavatēyam  mutaliya palatēyaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟu, āṅkāṅku, muṟiyē, patumi mutaliya makaḷiri maṇantu, avarkaḷi avaravar nakarattē tamittiruppaviṭuttu, maṟupaṭiyum irācamāpuram vantu cērukiṉṟāṉ. appōtu, avaṉ makaḷir virumputaṟkuriya vēṟṟuruvam koṇṭē varukiṉṟāṉ. avvūril cākaratattaṉ eṉṉum vaṇikaṉ makaḷ vimali yeṉpavaḷi maṇampurikiṉṟāṉ. avaḷpāl iraṇṭunāḷ taṅkiyiruntu, piṉṉart taṉtōḻari aṭikiṉṟāṉ.
avaṉ maṇakkōlattōṭu varuvatu kaṇṭa tōḻar, avaṉ maṇanta tiyaliṉ peyar kēṭṭu viyantu, "ivaṉēkāmaṉ" eṉṟu pārāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. avaruḷputticēṉaṉ eṉpavaṉ, cīvakaṉpāl curamañcariyiṉ virataneṟiyikkūṟi, "avaḷi mayakki maṇantuvariṉ, niṉṉik kāmatilakaṉ eṉpēṉ," eṉkiṉṟāṉ. atukēṭkum cīvakaṉ, "yāṉ ataṉic ceytu varukiṟēṉ; nī nāḷiyē kāmaṉ kōṭṭam pukuntu maṟin tiru," eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭu iṟappamutirnta vētiyaṉ pōla vaṭivukoṇṭu, curamañcariyirukkum kaṉṉi māṭattukkuc cel kiṉṟāṉ. vāyilil kāvalirukkum makaḷir avaṉik kaṇṭu, avaṉ paciyiyum uṇarntu curamañcarikkut terivikkiṉṟaṉar.
curamañcari avaṉik kāṇṭalāl taṉ viratam keṭāteṉa niṉintu, avaṉ muṉvantu, avaṉōṭu collum āṭukiṉṟāḷ. avaṉ kavarporuḷ eytap pēcukiṉṟāṉ. ataṉi uṇarāta appēti, avaṉip pittaṉ eṉṟeṇṇiyum, avaṉatu paciyiṉip pōkkutal pēraṟam eṉṟu karutiyum, taṉ māṭattē aḻittuc ceṉṟu, amutūṭṭi, iṉiya pañcaṇiyiṟkaṇ paṭuvikkiṉṟāḷ. avaṉum ataṉkaṇ, ciṟitu nēram uṟaṅkiṉavaṉpōṟ kiṭantuviṭṭu, eḻuntu, iṉiya pāṭṭup pāṭat toṭaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. ataṉik kēṭṭa makaḷir paravacamākiṉṟaṉar; curamañcariyum makiḻvuṟukiṉṟāḷ.
piṉpu, ammakaḷir, "ippāṭṭuc cīvakaṉ pāṭṭi ottuḷatu," eṉṟu kūṟic curamañcarikkuc cīvakaṉip paṟṟi niṉippūṭṭalum, avaḷ, maṟunāḷē, kāmaṉ kōṭṭam nōkki āṭavar evarum kāṇātapaṭi, vētiyaṉum tōḻiyarum uṭaṉvara cakaṭamūrntu ceṉṟu cērkiṉṟāḷ. aṅkē, cīvaka vētiyaṉi ōr ariyuḷ viṭuttu, kāmampaṭivattiṉ muṉniṉṟu, cīvakaṉi viriyat tarumāṟu vēṇṭukiṉṟāḷ. aṅkē, maṟintirukkum putticēṉaṉ,"nī cīvakaṉip peṟṟāy" eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟāṉ. aḵtu atteyvattiṉ tirumoḻi eṉap piṟaḻak koṇṭa curamañcari, makiḻntu, vētiyaṉ irunta aṟikku varukiṉṟāḷ. aṅkē cīvakaṉ taṉ uṇmi vaṭivōṭu niṟkiṉṟāṉ. atu kaṇṭu, avaḷ, kātal kimmikku meymmaṟantu niṉṟoḻikiṉṟāḷ. cīvakaṉ, avaḷit taḻuvi, "niṉṉi nāḷivantu maṇappēṉ" eṉṟu tēṟṟi aṉuppukiṉṟāṉ.
