māṟuvatic cīritākak koṇṭu makkaḷ iyaṅkat talippaṭṭatu iṉṟunēṟṟaṉṟu; maṇivācakar kālattēyē tōṉṟi nilaviyatu.  kālamāṟṟaṅkaṭku ulaku iṟiyāvatu kaṇṭum iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷ māttiram māṟātu nilipeṟuvatu uṇarntum, "māṟilāta māk karuṇiveḷḷamē" eṉa maṉamārap pārāṭṭik kūṟiṉar.  ak karuṇi veḷḷattiṉ viḷivāyt tōṉṟiyataṉāl makkaḷvāḻviṉ aṭippaṭi māṟātē irukkiṟatu.
makkaḷiṉ niṉivilum collilum ceyalilum ūṭuruvi niṟpatu oṉṟu uṇṭu.  ataṉi iṉpa nāṭṭam eṉpar.  iṉpatti vēṇṭāta uyirē illi.  makkaḷuyirē yaṉṟi ellāvuyirumē ataṉi virumpukiṉṟaṉa.  ataṉik kaṇṭa tolkāppiyar, "ellā vuyirkkum iṉpa meṉpatu, tāṉamarntu varūum mēvaṟ ṟākum" eṉṟār.  iṉṟuvari anta iṉpa nāṭṭam māṟātiruppatu viññāṉa vaḷarcciyālum naṉku teḷiyappaṭṭatu.  ataṉip perukap peṟutaṟkum, eḷitil virivil eytutaṟkum makkaḷiṉam taṉ aṟivu āṇmi poruḷ mutaliyavaṟṟit toḻiṟpaṭutti muyaṉṟavaṇṇam irukkiṟatu.  immuyaṟciyāl putiya putiya karuttukkaḷum karuvikaḷum piṟantu perumāṟṟaṅkaḷi viḷivittu iṉpa nāṭṭatti ēṟṟam ceykiṉṟaṉa.
itaṉāl makkaḷiṉam iṉpap pēṟṟukkup poruḷiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi uṇarntu apporuḷip peṟutaṟkum perukkutaṟkum perumuyaṟci ceykiṟatu.  poruṭpēṟu toḻil vāṇikaṅkaḷiṉ tuṇiyiṉṟi iyalātu eṉpatu teḷivāṉapiṉ avaṟṟil makkaḷiṉ karuttu ūkkamuṭaṉ iyaṅkukiṟatu.  ip poruḷum ataṟku uriya āḷviṉiyum oruṅku iyaluṅkāl, iṉpa nāṭṭam maṉakkaṇṇil tōṉṟi ulaka vāḻvi iṉpaniliyamākkutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa uyariya koḷkiyi uṇartti ūkkukiṉṟatu.
iṉpaniliyi uṇarntu ataṉi eytutaṟkuriya neṟiyil ēṉi uyirkaḷiviṭa makkaḷuyir māṇpumikkatu. naṉṉeṟi tīneṟi eṉṟa iraṇṭālum iṉpam peṟappaṭiṉum, tīneṟiyil varuvatu ciṟitupōtu niṉṟu piṉpaḻiviḷittut tuṉpamāy muṭitalāl ataṉi viṭuttu naṉṉeṟiyāl varum iṉpam pukaḻviḷittu nilipeṟṟup perukuvati makkaḷuyir uṇarkiṟatu.  itutāṉ makkaḷuyirkkuḷḷa māṇpu.
am māṇpiṉi aṟintu muyalvatil makkaḷiṉam muḻutum īṭupaṭavilli. kuṇamēyuṭiyavarum kuṟṟamēyuṭiyavarum makkaḷil illi.  iraṇṭum viraviyē makkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.  "kuṇanāṭik kuṟṟamum nāṭi avaṟṟuḷ, mikināṭi mikka koḷal" eṉavum, "ariya kaṟṟu ācaṟṟār kaṇṇum teriyuṅkāl iṉmi aritē veḷiṟu" eṉavum tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiṉar.  itaṉāl, makkaḷ naṉṉeṟiyum tīneṟiyum mēṟkoṇṭu iṉpamum tuṉpamum eytukiṉṟaṉar.  eṉiṉum, "iṉpamē ennāḷum tuṉpamilli" eṉṟa nili eytuvatē kuṟikkōḷ eṉpatu paṇṭum iṉṟum eṉṟum māṟātu nilavukiṟatu.
iṉpam vēṇṭi muyalum makkaḷiṉattiṟku maṟṟoru ciṟanta paṇpu mēmpaṭṭirukkiṟatu.  tammiṉum mikkār kāṭṭum vaḻiyip piṉpaṟṟic celvatu ap paṇpu.  atukaṇṭē cāṉṟōr naṉṉeṟiyāl viḷiyum iṉpattiyum tīneṟiyāl viḷiyum tuṉpattiyum tammuṭiya collālum eḻuttālum terivitta vaṇṇam irukkiṉṟaṉar.
itaṉi naṉku aṟintuṇarnta cāṉṟōruḷ, tolkāppiyar mutaliyōr kālattukku muṉpu vāḻnta cāṉṟōr ariyatōr aṉpuppaṇi purintuḷḷaṉar.  makkaṭ camutāyattiṉ aṭippaṭi, kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum āka iruvarum karuttorumittu naṭattum taṉik kuṭumpam; oruvaṉum oruttiyumāyp piṟantu kātalaṉum kātaliyumāy iyintu kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumāyp piṇippuṇṭu aṟaneṟiyil poruḷīṭṭi iṉpuṟṟu vāḻvatu ataṉ urimic ceyal.   ik kuṭumpattiṉiṉṟum kiḷittup perukum makkaḷiṉam aṟacceyalum poruṭpēṟum iṉpa nukarcciyum kuṟivaṟap peṟṟup pukaḻ niṟuvi maṇṇaka vāḻvi viṇṇulakap peruvāḻvāka māṟṟi māṇpuṟṟut tikaḻum eṉa eṇṇiṉar.
tamatu eṇṇatti uruvākkum vakiyil, kuṇamēyuruvāya ōr āṇmakaṉiyum avaṉōṭu oppap peṇmikkuriya kuṇamē vaṭivāṉa oru peṇmakaḷiyum paṭittu avarkaḷi muṟiyē talivaṉum taliviyumāka niṟutti iruvarum kātalāl ōruyirum īruṭampiṉarumāka iyintu maṉivāḻvu naṭatti ēṉik kuṇamum kuṟṟamum viraviya makkaḷiṉattukku vaḻikāṭṭiyāy amital vēṇṭumeṉat tuṇintu aintiṇiyoḻukkam eṉṉum aṉpu neṟiyi vakuttu eḻuttum collumpōla ituvum iḷamiyilēyē ovvoruvarum payilavēṇṭuvateṉa ilakkaṇattil iṇitturittaṉar.
tamiḻvāḻvil nērnta cīraḻivu kāraṇamākap poruḷilakkaṇam maṟikkappaṭṭatu.  piṉpu iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷum ataṟkup piṉ pala akapporuḷ nūlkaḷum taṉiyē tōṉṟalāyiṉa.  puṟapporuḷ vakiyil paṉṉirupaṭalam, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli mutaliyaṉa tōṉṟiṉa.  kālamāṟṟattāl ip poruḷilakkaṇam payilvatu arukiṟṟu.  tamiḻar uḷḷattil poruḷuṇarvu kuṉṟavum, poruḷaṟivum poruḷ ceyyum uṇarvum kuṟintaṉa.  taṟkāppukkum taṉakku iṉṟiyamiyāta uṇavu uṭikaṭkum piṟanāṭṭavari etirnōkkum iḻinili tōṉṟi nilavuvatāyiṟṟu.
ulaka vāḻviṉkaṇ makkaḷ ceyyum poruḷ, aṉpārnta maṉivāḻvum, poruḷum pukaḻum nalkum puṟavāḻvum eṉa iraṇṭallatu iṉmiyiṉ, iraṇṭiyum tamiḻc cāṉṟōr akam puṟam eṉak koṇṭaṉar pōlum, poruṭpēṟṟukkum viṉiceytaṟkum iṭam maṉikkup puṟamātalāl puṟam eṉṟum, ap poruḷik koṇarntu payaṉ nukarumiṭam akamātalāl, akavāḻviṉ aṉpu aṟaṅkaḷi akameṉavum vakuttu muṟi ceytaṉar.
mācaṟṟa kuṇavaṭivāya talimakkaḷ taṉittaṉiyē oruvar oruvarik kaṇṭu kātaluṟṟut karuttorumittu iyivatu kaḷavu eṉṟum, piṉpu iruvarum nāṭum ūrum aṟiya naṉmaṇam ceytu koṇṭu maṉiyaṟam mēṟkoṇṭu nallaṟa naṉviṉikaḷāl pēriṉpap peruvāḻviṉarātal kaṟpu eṉṟum muṟiceytaṉar.
kaḷavum kaṟpumākiya akapporuḷi aṉpiṉ aintiṇi yoḻukkam eṉṟaṉar.  aintiṇi eṉpavi kātalaṉpuṟṟa talimakkaḷ kūṭalum, piritalum, kūṭiyum pirintum iruttalum, kūṭumiṭattu ūṭalum, pirintaviṭattu iraṅkalum eṉa aintākum.  ovvoṉṟiṉ kaṇṇum talamakkaḷ uḷḷatteḻum karuttuk kaḷip pāṭaluṟṟa cāṉṟōr, ovvoṉṟiṟkum iṭamum kālamum vakuttu iyaṟkic cūḻniliyoṉṟi amittu urittaṉar.  pāṭutaṟkuriya karutti uripporuḷ eṉavum, iyaṟkic cūḻniliyik karupporuḷ eṉavum, iṭamum kālamumākiya iraṇṭiṉiyum mutaṟporuḷ eṉavum vakuttuk koṇṭu, kūṭalākiya uripporuḷik kuṟiñciyeṉavum, pirivip pāliyeṉavum, iruttali mulliyeṉavum, ūṭali marutameṉavum, iraṅkali neytaleṉavum pakirntaṉar.  kuṟiñciyiṉ cūḻnili malināṭu; mulli kāṭṭunāṭu; marutam , nīrvaḷam niṟinta naṉcey vaḷamikka nilappakuti; neytal kaṭaṟkari nāṭu.
innūliṉkaṇ, paṇṭita vittuvāṉ cāmpacivam avarkaḷ neytaṟ pāṭṭukkaḷit tokuttu avaṟṟiṉkaṇ aminta mutaṟporuḷ karupporuḷ uripporuḷ mūṉṟiyum vakuttu ovvoṉṟiyum taṉittaṉiyāka niṟutti viḷakkiyiruppatu itaṉip payilvōrkku akapporuḷ nalatti eḷitil uṇarntu iṉpuṟutaṟku vāyppāka irukkiṟatu.
karupporuṭ kūṟu, iyaṟkiyil kāṇappaṭum maram ceṭi koṭi mutaliyaṉavum, paṟavikaḷum vilaṅkukaḷum aṅku vāḻpavar mēṟkoṇṭa toḻilum piṟavum aṭaṅkiyatu.  ātalāl, neytaliṟ kāṇappaṭum uyiriṉaṅkaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum taṉittaṉiyākak kāṭṭiyiruppatu mikavum poruttamāṉa ceyalākum.  ciṟanta tamiḻāciriya rātalāl, māṇavarkaḷum piṟarum poruḷiyalpi naṉku uṇarattakka vakiyil eṭutturikkiṉṟār.
neytaṟṟiṇi ārāyccikkeṉa eṭṭuttoki, pattup pāṭṭu, patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku eṉṟa toki nūlkaḷil uḷḷa neytaṟ pāṭṭukkaḷit tokuttu avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷ yāvum iṉitu  viḷaṅka eḷiya naṭiyil urippatu avaratu iyalpāka irukkiṟatu.
iyaṟki uyirkaḷiṉ iyalpu kūṟaṟkaṇṇum uripporuḷiṉ uyarnta karuttukkaḷi viḷakkutaṟ kaṇṇum naṇpar tiru.cāmpacivam eḷiya muṟiyil yāvarum nayantu makiḻumāṟu iṉimi kamaḻa eḻutuvatu avaratu pulami nayatti naṉku veḷip paṭuttukiṟatu.  itaṉik kāṇum eṉ uḷḷam ivar ēṉit tiṇikaṭkum ivvāṟu eḻutavēṇṭumeṉa virumpukiṟatu.
tiru.cāmpacivattiṉ it tamiḻp paṇi kālattāṟ ceyyum arumpaṇi yātaliṉ tamiḻulakam itaṉi virumpi varavēṟku meṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
ivarkaṭku itupōlum naṟpaṇi puritaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta nalam palavum peruka aruḷumāṟu ñālam pukaḻum ālavāy aṇṇal tiruvaruḷip paravukiṉṟēṉ.



41. tamiḻicippāṭal 
irākam: iratipatippiriyā	       tāḷam: āti
ā: ca ri ka pa ni ca			a: ca ni pa ka ri ca
20-āvatu mēḷattiṟ piṟantatu
mutaṉili
iṉinalamē tamiḻmā makaḷē, kēḷ	iṉi,
iṭinili
niṉivelām niṉip parava vēṇṭi
nikaḻva tātalāl kuṟiva tilāmalē	iṉi
iṟutinili
tāṉ taṉi yām tamiḻt tāy niṉa tāḷpaṇi
tāṉpuri vōm tayaṅ kōm iṉi yām
tēṅkupukaḻ mikac collum vaḷamilliyō
ceñcol nalam pala ceytiṭu vōm yām	iṉi



42. kavitikaḷiṉ aṇiyamippu
orukāl, kampar kōtāviri yāṟṟiṉ ciṟappik kūṟa nērntapōtu ataṉuṭiya poruḷ nalamum, teḷivum, oḻukkum  avarkkuc caṅkac cāṉṟōr pāṭiya pāṭṭi avar niṉivil eḻuppiṉa.  avar,
"puviyiṉukku aṇiyāy āṉṟa poruḷ tantu pulattiṟṟāki
  aviyakat tuṟikaḷ tāṅki aintiṇi neṟiyaḷāvic 
  caviyuṟat teḷintu taṇṇeṉṟu oḻukkamum taḻuvic cāṉṟōr
  kaviyeṉak kiṭanta kōtāviriyiṉi vīrar kaṇṭār"
eṉṟār.  itaṉāl āṉṟa poruḷum, aintiṇi neṟiyum, caviyuṟu teḷivum, taṇṇeṉṟa oḻukkamum poruntiyiruppatu cāṉṟōr kavikaḷiṉ taṉmiyāvatu terikiṟatu.  aintiṇi neṟi poruḷoṭupaṭṭa tākiyāl, poruḷnalamum, caviyuṟu teḷivum, taṇṇeṉṟoḻukkamum kavikaṭkuc ciṟapput taruvaṉa eṉak koḷḷalām.  ivaṟṟuḷ cavi eṉpatu oḷiyeṉap poruḷ tarumāyiṉum, iṉiya ōci kāraṇamākap piṟakkum oṭpam eṉa nām ataṟkup poruḷ ceyyalām.  ivvāṟē taṇṇeṉṟa oḻukkam eṉak kampar kūṟiyatu, kavikaḷil cāṉṟōr toṭuttirukkum coṟkaḷ oṉṟaṉiyoṉṟu toṭarntu niṟkuñ colloḻuṅku eṉṟal poruntum.  eṉavē, poruḷvaḷamum, iṉṉōciyum, teḷivum, cevviya colloḻuṅkum kavikaṭkuriya māṇpukaḷ eṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.  avaṟṟi muṟiyākak kaṇṭaṟiyap piṟakkum aṟiviṉpattik kaviyiṉpam eṉṟu kūṟuvar.  āṉāl, kamparō avviṉpattip piṟitōriṭattil "teṉṉuṇ tēṉil tēkkiya ceñcoṟ kaviyiṉpam" eṉa aḷḷūṟittittikkap pēcukiṉṟār.
mēlum, oṉṟi yoṉṟu toṭarntu iciyāl iyintu niṟkum colloḻuṅkiṉi, taṇṇeṉṟa oḻukkam eṉak kampar kūṟiyataṉi nūlkaḷ yāppu eṉak kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.  yāḻiṉkaṇ narampuk kaṭṭu amiyātapōtu iṉiya ici eḻātu; atupōlavē, kaviyiṉkaṇ yāppu naṉku poruntātāyiṉ iṉṉōci piṟavātu.  "paṇṇeṉṉām pāṭaṟku iyipu iṉṟēl" eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷ iṅkē niṉikkattakkatu.  itupaṟṟiyē kaviyamitikku yāppu iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭukiṟatu.
kavinalattik kuṟikka vanta kampar vēṟōr aḻakiya karutti aṟivikkiṉṟār.  ciṟanta kaviyeṉpatutāṉ piṟanta nāṭṭukkē aṇiyāyt tikaḻavēṇṭum eṉpatu avar karuttu.  itē karuttu āṅkila nāṭṭup pulavarkaḷiṭiyēyum nilaviṟṟu.  namatu intiya nāṭu āṅkilap pēraraciṉ kippaṭṭiruntapōtu, "intiyap pēraracu vēṇṭumā? cekappiriyariṉ kaviti vēṇṭumā? eṉiṉ, intiya nāṭṭi iḻantālum iḻappēṉ, cekappiriyariṉ kavitiyi iḻakka oruppaṭēṉ" eṉṟu āṅkilap perum pulavarāṉa kārlil kūṟiṉār.  avaratu akkarutti avarkkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu vāḻnta kamparum, "puviyiṉukku aṇiyāy āṉṟa poruḷ tantu pulattiṟṟāki" eṉa eṭuttu moḻintiruppatu aṟiñar niṉivu koḷḷaṟkuriyatu.
poruḷ vaḷamum, iṉṉōciyum, taṇṇeṉṟa yāppum uṭiya kavikaḷip pāmāli eṉṟum urimāli eṉṟum, paṇṭiyōr kuṟippatu vaḻakkam.  pāṭṭum uriyum iṉiya coṟkaḷāl ākkappaṭuvatu paṟṟi avaṟṟic coṉmāli eṉṟu urippatumuṇṭu.  māli eṉṟa col mutaṉmutalil tōṉṟiya pōtu pūkkaḷē mālikkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.  ataṉāl, coṉ māli, pāmāli eṉṟa toṭarkaḷāl, collum pāṭṭum pūkkaḷākak karutappaṭṭami iṉitu viḷaṅkum.  itaṉi, 
"colleṉṉum pūmpōtu tōṉṟip poruḷeṉṉum
  nallirun tīntātu nāṟutalāl - mallikiyiṉ
  vaṇṭār kamaḻ tāmamaṉṟē maliyāta
  taṇṭārāṉ kūṭaṟ ṟamiḻ"
eṉṟa paḻam pāṭṭum vaṟpuṟuttukiṟatu.  ēṉippūkkaḷ ellā vaṟṟiṉum  mikuntatākiyāl, ippāṭṭu mallikiyic ciṟanteṭuttu moḻikiṟatu. pūviṉkaṇ itaḻum maṇamum tēṉum iruntu kāṇpārkku iṉpam ceyvatupōlac coṉmāli pāmālikaḷiṟ ceñcollum, iṉṉōciyum, naṉporuḷum kiṭantu iṉpam ceykiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu karuttu.
ivvāṟu coṟkaḷip pūveṉṟu cāṉṟōr palarum kuṟippatil vēṟu ōr āḻnta karuttum uḷatu.  pūvō, taṉ niṟattālum, maṇattālum vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷit taṉṉiṭattuṟṟa tēṉi yaḷittu iṉpuṟuttum ceyal nalamuṭiyatu; pāṭṭō eṉiṉ, taṟ coṟcemmiyālum ōciyiṉimiyālum paṭippavar maṉatti īrttut taṉṉiṭattē aminta poruḷ nalattāl makiḻvikkiṉṟatu.  āyiṉum, pūvum kavitiyum oruvakiyil vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  pūvāṉatu niṟattālum maṇattālum, kāṇpōruṭiya kaṇṇum mūkkumākiya poṟiyiraṇṭiṉiyum paṇi koḷḷukiṟatu; kaviti, kaṟpavariṉ kaṇṇum ceviyumākiya iru poṟikaḷiyum iyakkukiṟatu.  itaṉi,
"kaṇṇiṉum ceviyiṉum nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarum
  uṇarvuṭi māntark kallatu teriyiṉ
  naṉṉayap poruḷkōḷ eṇṇaruṅ kurittē".
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṉār cuṭṭiyurittuḷḷār.  itaṉāl colliyum ōciyiyum karuviyākak koṇṭē pāṭṭukkaḷ naṉporuḷ uṇartti makkaḷukku aṟiviṉpam nalkum arumpaṇi ceyvati aṟiyalām.  inta aṟiviṉpattik kampar mutalāyiṉōr ceñcoṟ kaviyiṉpam eṉṟaṉar; ikkālattavar kavitiyiṉpam eṉṟu icikkiṉṟaṉar.
vaṇṭiṉattit tampāl varuvittaṟkuk karuviyākak koṇṭa niṟamum maṇamum pūkkaḷukku iyaṟkiyākavē amintuḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟiṟkuc ceyaṟkiyaḻaku oṉṟum cērppatilli.  yārē aḻakukku aḻaku ceyvār? coṟkaḷiyum pūvākak karutiṉamiyiṉ paḻaṅkālat tamiḻc cāṉṟōr, ceyaṟkiyākap puṇarkkappaṭum aṇivakikaḷit taṉiyāka eṭuttu ārāyavilli; pūmāli kaḷit toṭuppatil ceyalvaki tōṉṟiyatu pōlac coṉmāli toṭuppatiṟ cila tōṉṟak kaṇṭu avaṟṟi māṭṭu eṉṟum vaṇṇam eṉṟum vakutturittaṉar.  kavitikkaṇ iyalpāka amiya vēṇṭiya aḻaku vakikaḷi māttiram vaṉappu eṉa vakuttum virittum urittārkaḷ.
uvamam eṉpatu makkaḷuṭiya pēccu, eḻuttu, pāṭṭu eṉṟa mūṉṟilum payiṉṟu poruḷ iṉitu viḷaṅkuvatu nōkkamāka aminta col vaḻakkākak karutappaṭṭatu.  oruvaṉ vāḻkkiyil nāḷum payilum iṭam poruḷ mutaliyaṉa vaḻaṅkum kāṭcikaḷum, avvappōtu nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷum, payilum nūṟporuḷum avaṉ uḷḷattiṉkaṇ kiṭantu avaṉuṭiya pēccilum eḻuttilum pāṭṭilum uvamaṅkaḷāka veḷippaṭum.  avaṟṟiyum, taṉ karuttuk kaḷi piṟar naṉku uṇaravēṇṭum eṉṟa kuṟippilēyē avaṉ kiyāḷukiṉṟāṉ.  ataṉāl oruvaṉ vaḻaṅkum uvamaṅkaḷ avaṉuṭiya ulakiyalaṟiviyum, vāḻkki vaḷattiyum, kaṟpaṉit tiṟaṉiyum kāṭṭutaṟkup peruntuṇikaḷākap piṟaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.  perumpulami paṭitta periyōriṭattum, ceytaṟkeḷiyavaṟṟiyum ceytu muṭikkum tiṟam peṟāta ciṟiyōriṭattum ivvuvamaṅkaḷ oppa niṉṟu avaravar maṉa virivukkēṟpa avar karuttukkaḷip piṟar aṟiyap pulappaṭuttu kiṉṟaṉa.  itupaṟṟiyē paḻantamiḻc cāṉṟōr uvamattic ceyyuḷ pōlat taṉiyē niṟutti ārāyntaṉar.  mēlum oruvaṉ tāṉ piṟa poruḷkaḷi yuṇartaṟkum piṟarkku uṇarttaṟkum ciṟanta colvaḻakkāka iruppatu paṟṟiyē paṇṭiyōr uvamattiyum yāppiyum poruḷiṉkaṇ aṭakki, eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟu pālāka niṟutti ilakkaṇam kaṇṭaṉar.  tolkāppiyarum anneṟi paṟṟiyē uvamattiyum yāppiyum poruḷatikārattil vittu urittār.  paṉampāraṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōrum "eḻuttum collum poruḷum nāṭi" urippatē tolkāppiyar karuttu eṉṟār.
tolkāppiyarkkup paṉṉūṟāṇṭu piṉṉartōṉṟiya cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kāṇappaṭum pāvakikaḷil iṉaṅkaḷ pala tōṉṟak kaṇṭu yāppu eṉṟa peyarāṟ pala nūlkaḷ eḻutu vārāyiṉar.  avaṟṟuṭ pala poruṭ ciṟappu illāmiyālō pēṇuvār kiviṭṭamiyālō iṟantu pōyiṉa; oṉṟiraṇṭē eñciṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟak kēṭukaḷāl, kavikaḷi malar eṉṟu karutiya karuttōṭu uṭampu eṉak karutum karuttoṉṟu uruvāyiṟṟu.  kākkipāṭiṉiyār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr tām pāṭiya yāppu nūluḷ,
"nātam mutalāka nalluṟup pēḻum iyintu
  ētamil taṉmi iyalācām - tātukkaḷ
  ēḻum puṇarntatu yākki eḻuttāti
  ēḻum puṇarntatu yāppu"
eṉṟu kūṟiṉar.  ikkaruttiyē ēṟakkuṟiya 600 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu viḷaṅkiya pavaṇantiyār,
"palvakit tātuviṉ uyirkkuṭal pōṟpala
  collāṟ poruṭku iṭaṉāka uṇarviṉiṉ
  vallōr aṇipeṟac ceyvaṉa ceyyuḷ"
eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttic ceṉṟār.  350 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar vāḻnta kumarakurupara aṭikaḷum,
"kalimakaḷ vāḻkki mukatta teṉiṉum
  malaravaṉ vaṇṭamiḻōrkku ovvāṉ - malaravaṉcey
  veṟṟuṭampu māyvaṉapōl māyāpukaḻ koṇṭu
  maṟṟivar ceyyum uṭampu"
eṉṟu pārāṭṭiṉar.
iṅṅaṉam kavikaḷi uṭampākak karutum koḷki nili peṟavum āṭi aṇikaḷāl oppaṉi ceyyum uṇarvu ciṟantu, ceyyuḷkaḷiṉ mēl paravuvatāyiṟṟu.  āṭiyaṇikaḷuḷ āṭi iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉpatu ulakaṟinta uṇmi.  "āṭiyillātavaṉ arimaṉitaṉ" eṉavum, "aṇiyellām āṭiyiṉ piṉ" eṉavum varum paḻamoḻikaḷē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟu.  aṇivaki kavitiyākiya uṭampukaṭku ēṟiyapōtu uvamatti āṭi yākavum meyppāṭu vaṉappu mutaliya nalaṅkaḷi aṇikaḷākavum aṟiñar cilar karutat talippaṭṭaṉar.  vaṭanūl āciriyar oruvar, kavitiyi ōr āṭaraṅkākavum, uvamiyaṇiyi āṭukaḷamākavum kaṟpaṉi ceytār.
tām ceyyum ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum aṇi aṇintu aḻakuṟuttik kāṇpatu makkaḷ iyalpu.  nāḷ cellac cella aḵtu iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyumākiya ellāp poruḷkaḷukkum aṇi peytu makiḻum taṉmiyāyiṟṟu.  mutalil atu uṭiyip paṟṟip piṉpu uṭampip paṟṟi muṭivil uṇarvukkum urikkum ceṉṟu paṟṟik koṇṭatu.  piṉṉar, makkaḷuṭiya pēccu eḻuttu pāṭṭu ākiya ellāt tuṟiyilum aṇivakikaḷ paraviṉamiyāl, cāṉṟōr avaṟṟukkeṉat taṉiyē ilakkaṇam kāṇpārāyiṉar.  tamiḻ āṅkilam mutaliya iyaṟki moḻikaḷip pōlākātu camakkirutam, naṉku ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉap poruḷpaṭum ceyki moḻiyākiyāl ataṉkaṇ aṇi nūlkaḷ mikappala uṇṭāyiṉa.  āṅkilattil aṇiyilakkaṇamē kiṭiyātu; collōviyam.1 eṉpatu aṇiyē yāyiṉum atu tamiḻp poruḷatikārattil uvamam niṟpatupōla āṅkilac colliṇakkattōṭē cērntu niṟkiṟatu.  tolkāppiyarkkup pallāyiram āṇṭukkup piṉ tōṉṟiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōr cilar, camakkirutam payiṉṟu, ataṉkaṭ kaṇṭa alaṅkāraṅkaḷiyum, tolkāppiyattil uḷḷa collatikāram poruḷatikāram eṉṟa iraṇṭilum kāṇappaṭum coṟporuḷ vaḻakkāṟukaḷiyum tokuttu, aṇiyilakkaṇam eṉṟa peyarāl orucila nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉpē eḻutiṉar.  poruḷatikārattuc ceyyuḷiyali vēṟu niṟuttip piṟkālac cāṉṟōr yāppilakkaṇam kaṇṭatu, inta aṇiyilakkaṇam vakutturippataṟku vaḻikāṭṭiyāyiṟṟu.  tamiḻil ippōtu, taṇṭiyalaṅkāram, vīracōḻiyam, māṟaṉalaṅkāram, ilakkaṇa viḷakkam eṉa nāṉku nūlkaḷē uḷḷaṉa.
nāḷum vaḷarntu varum makkaḷ uḷḷatti ūkkip putiya putiya pāṭṭuvakikaḷil avarkaḷit toḻiṟpaṭuttuvatil yāppu nūlkaḷ perumpaṇi ceytuḷḷaṉa.  cīvakacintāmaṇi kamparāmāyaṇam periyapurāṇam mutaliya peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyatu itaṉālēyām.  ivvāṟē aṟiñar aṟivip putu neṟiyiṟ pukutti mēlum mēlum makkaḷ vāḻvi māṇpuṟuttum aṇi niṟinta kāppiyaṅkaḷ ceyyumāṟu tūṇṭuvatil inta aṇiyilakkaṇaṅkaḷ tavaṟiṉa.  āyiṉum āṅkāṅku avvap pōtu tōṉṟiya tamiḻk kavitikaḷil mēlē kāṭṭiya aṇikaḷ amintu tamakkuriya iṉpatti nalkuvatil tavaṟuvatilli.
tamiḻ aṇiyilakkaṇaṅkaḷ, potuvaṇi, poruḷaṇi, collaṇi eṉa aṇikaḷi mūṉṟāka vakuttu avaṟṟi viritturik kiṉṟaṉa; collilum poruḷilum yāppilum kuṟṟam eṉa vilakkappaṭṭavaṟṟukku maṟutaliyāṉa kuṇaṅkaḷiyellām tokuttup potuvaṇi eṉṟa pakutiyilum, icit tamiḻp pakutiyil kiṭanta maṭakku vakikaḷiyum vaṭanūluṭ kāṇappaṭum cittirakkavi vakikaḷiyum iṟutiyil niṟkum, collaṇip pakutiyilum, ēṉi uvamam, uruvakam mutaliya aṇivakikaḷip poruḷaṇip pakutiyilum urikkiṉṟaṉa.
oru poruḷiṉ vaṭivam niṟam payaṉ toḻil eṉpavaṟṟi oruvaṉ piṟarkku urittuk kāṭṭumiṭattu uvamam tōṉṟuvatu iyalpu; uvamattāl viḷakkappaṭuvatu poruḷ eṉappaṭum., ulakukkellām muḻumutal kaṭavuḷākiya ātipakavaṉ eṉṟu colla vanta tiruvaḷḷuvar,
"akaramutala eḻut tellām; āti
  pakavaṉ mutaṟṟē yulaku"
eṉṟu kūṟiṉar; ippāṭṭil eḻuttellām akaramutala eṉṟatu uvamam; "ulaku ātipakavaṉ mutaṟṟu" eṉṟatu poruḷ.
uvamam ellām poruḷi viḷakkutaṟ kēṟṟa ciṟappum uyarvum uṭiyavātal vēṇṭum.  mēlum, avi, kēṭpōr maṉam icintu "takum" eṉat taliyacittu amiyat takuvaṉavāy iruttal vēṇṭum.  oru peṇṇiṉ kūntaṟ karumiyik kuṟikka vanta kaviñar ataṉi "mēkattiyaṉiya kūntal" eṉṟum, "iruḷiruṅ kūntal" eṉṟum kūṟuvatu takum.  oruvaṉ "karipōl irukkumkāṇ kaṉṉiyivaḷ kūntal" eṉpāṉāyiṉ, avaṉ kūṟṟi aṟivuṭiyōr koḷḷār.  mēkamum iruḷum pōlak kari uyarnta poruḷākātu; makkaḷum kariyi uvamiyākac coṉṉāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷātu ikaḻvārkaḷ.  itupaṟṟiyē, tolkāppiyam 'uyarntataṉ mēṟṟē uḷḷum kāli" eṉavum, "uvamum, poruḷum ottal vēṇṭum" eṉavum urittuḷḷatu.  ivviyalpu aṟiyāmiyāl innāḷiya pāvalaroruvar, "arikkaṉ viḷakkuppōl aminta viḻi iraṇṭuṭiyāḷ" eṉṟu pāṭiṉar; kēṭṭavar aṉivarum ikaḻntu kikoṭṭi nakiyāṭiṉar.  eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷiṉ ciṟappum ataṉic ceypavaratu cālpumē uvamattiṉ takuti kāṇpataṟku aminta urikal.  ivvāṟē aṇikaḷ yāvum, "vallāṉ vakuttatē vāykkāl eṉṉum peruvaḻakku"  vaḻi niṟpaṉavākum.
iṉi, kavikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum aṇivakikaḷ yāvum avaṟṟil iṭaṅkoṇṭirukkum poruḷiṉ uyarvu tāḻvukaḷiyuṇarttalum, nalantīṅkukaḷik kāṭṭutalum, viruppu veṟuppukkaḷip pulap paṭuttalum, vaṉmi meṉmikaḷic ciṟappittalum eṉṟa nāṉkiṉ aṭippaṭiyil eḻuvaṉavākum.  "ciṟappē nalaṉē kātal valiyoṭu, annāṟpaṇpum nilikkaḷam eṉpa" eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟukiṉṟār.  mēlum avar, "annāṟpaṇpum nilikkaḷamākum" eṉṉāmal, "nilikkaḷam eṉpa" eṉṟu urippatāl, tolkāppiyaṉārkku muṉpu vāḻnta cāṉṟōrkaḷē inta aṭippaṭiyik kaṇṭaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.
kāliyil, kiḻakkil tōṉṟi viḷaṅkum ñāyiṟṟik kaṇṭa cāṉṟōr oruvar ataṉ tōṟṟac ciṟappi ārvattōṭu taṉ tōḻarkkuc collukiṉṟār.  virinta vāṉattil kiḻakkil ñāyiṟu tōṉṟavum, ataṉkaṇ paraviyirunta iruḷ muṟṟum akaṉṟatu; pakal oḷi eṅkum virintatu eṉṟu collak karutiṉa avar,
"akaliru vicumpil pāyiruḷ paruki
 pakalkāṉ ṟeḻutaru palkatirp pariti"
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār.  kātalaṉip pirinta naṅki yorutti mālip poḻutu vara, matiyam vāṉattil eḻuntatum ñāyiṟu mēṟkil maṟintatum kaṇṭu kavali mikuntu maṉam varuntit taṉ tōḻikkuk kūṟuvāḷāy,
"putumatipuri mukattāy pōṉārnāṭṭu uḷatāṅkol
 matiyumiḻntu katirviḻuṅki vanta immaruṇmāli"
eṉṟu kūṟuvatāka iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ iyampukiṉṟār.  itu kātal aṭippaṭiyil avaḷ eytiya varuttatti iṉitu uṇarttuvatu kāṇalām.  itu potuvaṇivaki.
cēranāṭṭuk kaḻumalam eṉṉum ūriṉkaṇ nikaḻnta pōr oṉṟil cōḻaṉ ceṅkaṇāṉ veṟṟi peṟṟāṉ.  avaṉatu kaḷattip pāṭiya poykiyār eṉṉum cāṉṟōr, māṟṟārākiya cēraruṭiya paṭi ēnti niṉṟa veṇkuṭikaḷ cōḻaṉatu paṭik kaḷiṟu utittatāl kīḻmēlāy muṟintu vīḻntu citaṟiya kāṭciyip poruḷaṇivakiyuḷ oṉṟāṉa uvamavaṇiyāl urikkiṉṟār. maḻikkālattil kuppi mēṭukaḷil tōṉṟum kuṭik kāḷāṉkaḷ, mēyavarum āṉiṉaṅkaḷiṉ kālpaṭṭu uṭintu citaṟuvatu yāvarum aṟinta toṉṟu, ātalāl, ataṉi uvamiyākki yurippārāy,
"ō ō uvamaṉ uṟaḻviṉṟi ottatē
 kāviri nāṭaṉ kaḻumalam koṇṭanāḷ
 māvutippa māṟṟār kuṭiyellām kīḻmēlāy
 āvuti kāḷāmpi pōṉṟa ceṅkaṇāṉ
 mēvāri aṭṭa kaḷattu"
eṉṟu pāṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟār. pōrkkaḷiṟukaḷ utittataṉāl māṟṟār kuṭi yellām keṭṭaḻintaṉa eṉa vāḷā kūṟiya vaḻit tōṉṟāta iṉpam, "āvuti kāḷāmpi pōṉṟa" eṉṟa uvamiyāl ciṟantu tōṉṟukiṟatu.  itu māṟṟār tōlviyic ciṟappittu niṟpatu. 
caṅka kālattil toṇṭi nāṭṭukkuk kāñci talinakarum pavattiri kaṭaṟkari nakarumāka viḷaṅkiṉa.  annāḷil oru cāṉṟōr kāñci nakarik kaṇṭār; ataṉ perumiyum āṅku eḻum pēroliyum avarip peritum viyappaṭiyac ceytu kaṭali niṉikkac ceytaṉa.  uṭaṉē avar,
"malitērāṉ kacciyum mākaṭalum tammuḷ
 oliyum perumiyum okkum"
eṉṟār; appōtu kacci nakariṉ celva vaḷam avar cintaṉiyil mikku mikku eḻuntatu; ataṉāl avar                 - malitērāṉ
"kacci paṭuva  kaṭalpaṭā kacci
  kaṭalpaṭuva ellām paṭum"
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār.  ivvāṟu kacci nakarkkum kaṭalukkum toṭakkattil oṟṟumi kāṭṭip piṉpu vēṟṟumi kāṭṭiya vaḻiyum  kacci nakariṉ perumiyē piṟaṅkuvatāl namakku iṉpamuṇṭākiṟatu.  itaṉip poruḷaṇi vakiyuḷ vēṟṟumiyaṇi eṉpar.
pakivar eṟinta vēl mārpil tuḷittuc ceṉṟamiyāl atiyamāṉ neṭumāṉañci iṟantu paṭavē, atu kuṟittu oḷaviyār; 'pāṇar mutaliya irappōr kūṭṭamum puravalar cuṟṟamum pulavar perumakkaḷum ātaravu iḻantaṉar" eṉṟa karuttuppaṭa moḻiyat toṭaṅkiṉār.  ataṉi avvāṟu kūṟiṉāl kēṭpōr uḷḷam eḷitil īṭupaṭāteṉṟu,
"pāṇar akaṉ maṇṭit tuḷiyurīi
  irappōr kiyuḷum pōkip
  purappōr puṉkaṇ pāvi cōra
  añcol nuṇtērccip pulavar nāvil
  ceṉṟu vīḻntaṉṟu avaṉ
  aruniṟat tiyaṅkiya vēlē."
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār.  itaṉ kaṇ, atiyamāṉ mārpit tuḷitta vēl, pāṇar uṇavu peṟṟuṇṇum maṇṭiyeṉṉum uṇkalattit tuḷittu, irappavar ēntum kiyi vīḻttip purappōruṭiya aṉpu nāṭum kaṇṇik keṭuttup pulavar nāviyum tuṇṭittatu eṉṟu moḻintu nammi mikka irakkattil āḻttukiṉṟār.  ivvāṟu kūṟuvati aṇinūlār acaṅkati eṉṉum aṇi eṉpar.
mōci kīraṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr orukāl ōr aracaṉik kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvi yirukkiyil, "nāṭṭu makkaḷ tam vēntaṉiyē uyirākak karuti vāḻkiṉṟaṉar; ataṉāl, nāṭāḷum vēntaṉ nāṭṭit taṉakku uṭampākavum avvuṭampukkuḷ nilavum uyir tāṉākavum uṇarntu oḻukuvatu kaṭaṉ" eṉṟu urikka eṇṇiṉār.  maṉṉaṉē nāṭṭukku uyir eṉpatu nilināṭṭap peṟṟāltāṉ maṟṟiya karuttu valiyuṟum; ākavē, uyireṉak karuta niṟkum nellum nīrumākiya poruḷkaḷi vilakki vēntaṉē uyir eṉpārāy,
"nellum uyiraṉṟu nīrum uyiraṉṟu
  maṉṉaṉ uyirttē malartali yulakam"
eṉṟu muṟpaṭa moḻintu, piṉṉar,
"ataṉāl yāṉuyir eṉpatu aṟiki
  vēlmiku tāṉi vēntaṟkuk kaṭaṉē"
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār.  ippāṭṭil, nelliyum nīriyum vilakki vēntaṉi vaṟpuṟuttuvatāl itaṉi aṇinūlār avanuti eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
paṟampu nāṭṭilirunta nikaraṟṟa koṭi vaḷḷalākiya vēḷpāriyip pārāṭṭi, "vēḷ pārikku nikarāka maḻi mēkantāṉ uḷatu; āyiṉum atu vēṇṭuvōr vēṇṭum pōtu kuṟippaṟintu nalkum iyalpuṭiyataṉṟu; ataṉāl pulavar palarum pāri pāri eṉa avaṉ oruvaṉiyē pukaḻkiṉṟaṉar" eṉṟa karutti vittu orukāl kapilar pāṭa niṉittār.  akkarutti appaṭiyē urippatu mikiyātal kaṇṭu,
"pāri pāri eṉṟu palavētti
 oruvaṟ pukaḻvar cennāp pulavar;
 pāri yoruvaṉum allaṉ;
 māriyum uṇṭu īṇṭuulaku purappatuvē"
eṉṟu pāṭiṉar.  itaṉ kaṇ, māriyum uṇṭu īṇṭu ulaku purappatu; ataṉic cennāp pulavar ēttātu vilakkukiṉṟaṉarē eṉpatu tōṉṟa niṟkiṟatu.  iḵtu aṇivakiyuḷ vilakkuvami eṉappaṭukiṟatu.
naṅki yorutti taṉ kātalaṉ taṉ poruṭṭu naḷḷiravil koṭiya kāṭṭik kaṭantu varuvati nōkki, "iṉi, ivvāṟu varuvatu kūṭātu" eṉṟu kūṟa niṉittu, "kāṭṭil vēṅkimaram pūttup polivati oru matayāṉi kaṇṭu uṇmip puliyeṉa maruṇṭu taṉ piṭiyāṉiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu puliyoṭu māṟupaṭṭu niṉṟu piḷiṟuvatu nīvarum kāṭṭuvaḻi; avvaḻiyē variṉ uṉakkut tīṅkuṇṭām" eṉak kuṟippāl uṇartti,
"pāyāta vēṅki malarap paṭumatamā
  pūvāta puṇṭarikam eṉṟañci - mēvum
  piṭitaḻuvi māṟatirum kāṉil piḻiyām
  vaṭitavaḻum vēlōyniṉ varavu"
eṉṟu kūṟukiṟāḷ.  ivvāṟu taṉ karuttic cintittu uṇarac ceyyum itaṉi aṇinūlār vipāvaṉi aṇi eṉpar.  itaṉ kaṇ vēṅkimaram eṉa uṇarttaṟkup pāyāta vēṅki eṉṟum, puṇṭarikam eṉpatu tāmariyiyum puliyiyum kuṟippatāl, iṅkē atu puliyik kuṟikkumāṟu tōṉṟap pūvāta puṇṭarikam eṉṟum kūṟiyatu veḷippaṭi eṉṉum aṇiyākum.
camakkiruta alaṅkāra nūlkaḷip payiṉṟu tamiḻil aṇi nūl kaṇṭa taṇṭiyāciriyarum piṟarum uvamappakutiyil tolkāppiyar kāṭṭiya uḷḷuṟi uvamattik kāṇātu pōyiṉar.  piṉ vanta māṟaṉalaṅkāram eḻutiya āciriyartāṉ anta uḷḷuṟiyi ōr aṇiyākak kaṇṭu aṇinūluḷ cērttu urippārāyiṉar.  nikaḻcci yoṉṟik kūṟa niṉikkum pulavaṉ mutaṟkaṇ ataṟkōr iṭattiyum kālattiyum kuṟittuk koḷvaṉ; iṭam, āṅkuḷḷa maram, ceṭi-koṭi, vilaṅku, paṟavi, vayal, cōli, kāṭu, āṟu, mali, mutaliyavaṟṟik kāṭṭā niṟkum.  kālam iṭattiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷāl viḷaṅkum. inta iyaṟkic cūḻniliyil, yātāṉumōr nikaḻcciyik kūṟuvōṉ, an nikaḻccikkup piṉṉaṇiyāka accūḻnili kāṭṭum kāṭci yoṉṟi niṟuttuvaṉ; iyaṟki vaḻaṅkum kāṭcikaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉa vātalāl avaṉ yātāṉumoṉṟi varintu koḷvaṉ; atu tāṉ kūṟum nikaḻccikku evvāṟāyiṉum toṭarpuṟṟu niṟkum.  attoṭarpu mikka nerukkamuṭiyatāka amiyumiṭattu uḷḷuṟi eṉappaṭum.  anneruṅkiya toṭarpu eṭuttuk koṇṭa nikaḻccikku uvamamāka amiyumāyiṉ aḵtu uḷḷuṟi uvamam eṉappaṭum.
kātalaṉōṭu kūṭiyuṟiyum iṉpattik kūṟak karuti naṅki, avaṉuṭiya naṭpuk kūṭiyiruppārkkuc ciṟanta pātukāppāy iṉpam payakkum eṉṟu collukiṟāḷ.  kātalaṉ iyalpi uṇarttuvataṟku malināṭṭu iyaṟkik kāṭciyoṉṟi uḷḷuṟutti urikkiṉṟāḷ.
"peruṅki iruṅkaḷiṟu aivaṉam māntik
 karuṅkāl marāmpoḻil pācaṭit tuñcum
 curumpuimir cōli malināṭaṉ kēṇmi
 poruntiṉārkku ēmāp puṭittu"
eṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟāḷ.  itaṉ kaṇ, malināṭaṉ kēṇmi poruntiṉārkku ēmāppuṭittu eṉṟu avaḷ kūṟuvatu nikaḻ poruḷ; ataṟkup periya kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi aivaṉa nelvakiyuṇṭu marāmaram niṟinta cōliyil utirntu kiṭakkum iliyiṉ mēl paṭuttu uṟaṅkum eṉṟatu.  ataṟku aminta iyaṟkic cūḻnilik kāṭciyākiya uḷḷuṟiyākum.  peruṅkaḷiṟu taṉakku vēṇṭum aivaṉamākiya uṇavip peṟṟu marāmarap poḻilil iṉitu uṟaṅkuvatu pōla, malināṭaṉuṭiya naṇpar tamakku vēṇṭum nalaṅkaḷip peṟṟu iṉpuṟuvar eṉa eṭuttuk koṇṭa karuttukku uvamamāy niṉṟu viḷaṅkuvatu paṟṟi iḵtu uḷḷuṟi uvamam eṉappaṭukiṟatu.  tamiḻiluḷḷa aṇinūlār ellārkkum piṉvanta māṟaṉalaṅkāramuṭiyār, itaṉi uḷḷuṟi uvamavaṇi eṉpar.
iṟutiyāka niṟkum collaṇippakutiyil maṭakku, cittira kavi eṉṟa iraṇṭumē koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tiṅkaḷ māli veṇkuṭiyāṉ
	ceṉṉi ceṅkōl atuōccik
kaṅki taṉṉip puṇarntālum
	pulavāy vāḻi kāvēri
kaṅki taṉṉip puṇarntālum
	pulavā toḻital kayaṟkaṇṇāy
maṅki mātar aruṅkaṟpeṉṟu
	aṟintēṉ vāḻi kāvēri.
eṉṟa intap pāṭṭu iraṇṭāmaṭi maṭaṅki mūṉṟāmaṭiyilum varuvatāl maṭakku eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu.  piṟkālattē immaṭakkuc cilēṭiyaṇi peṟṟē peritum iyaluvatāyiṟṟu.  pukār nakarattu viṭiyaṟkālac ciṟappik kūṟa vanta maṇimēkali immaṭakkaṇiyic cilēṭiyaṇi kalantu,
paṇi nilip puravi pala eḻuntālap
paṇi nilip puḷḷum pala eḻuntālap
pūm poḻilār kippuḷ ḷoli ciṟappap
pūṅ koṭiyār kippuḷ ḷoli ciṟappa
eṉap pāṭukiṉṟatu.
cittirak kavikaḷ pāṭaluḷ aminta poruṭkuc ciṟapput tarātu, coṟkaḷukkē ciṟappaḷittu niṟkum.
tēyattiyaṅki yalakil talaṅkaḷil ceṉṟu aṭikaḷ
tēyattiyaṅki aliyal tiṭam katicērtal aṟin
tēyattiyaṅki nittilam tāṉ eṟicentil aṭin
tēyattiyaṅki tarittāṉ taṉayaṟ ṟericikkiṉē.
ippāṭṭu tiruccentil niroṭṭaka yamaka antātiyil uḷḷatu.  tiruccentilil uḷḷa murukaṉit tericittāl katicērtal tiṭam; tēyantōṟum ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkuḷḷa talaṅkaḷi nōkkip pōy aliya vēṇṭā eṉpatu karuttu.  niroṭṭakam eṉpatu vāyiṉ mēlitaḻum kīḻitaḻum toṭṭu mūṭātavāṟu pāṭuvatu; yamakam eṉpatu mutalaṭikkaṇ vanta cīrē ellā aṭiyilum vantu poruḷ vēṟupaṭa niṟpatu.
ivvaṇṇam coṟkaḷukkē ēṟṟam koṭuttup pāṭap paṭum collaṇip pakutikkuḷ poruḷaṇikkaṇ kuṟikkap paṭṭirukkum coṟpiṉ varunili, muraṇ, veḷippaṭi, tīpakam, cilēṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiyum aṭakkiyirukkalām.  avaṟṟi aṅkē vittataṟkuriya kāraṇam aṇinūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭa villi.
43. caṅkakālat tamiḻmakaṉ
paṇṭi nāḷil vāḻnta tamiḻmakaṉ eṉṉātu caṅkakālat tamiḻmakaṉ eṉṟē talippu amikiṟatu.  paṇṭikkālam eṉpatu oru kālavellikkuḷ aṭaṅkātu paranta paṇpiṉatāy irukkiṟatu.  caṅkakālam eṉṟa vaḻi, muṟkālattē nam centamiḻnāṭṭil caṅkam iruntu tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟit taṉipperuñ ciṟappeytiyirunta kālam eṉṟa uṇarvu nam uḷḷattil eḻak kāṇkiṉṟōm. āyiṉum, ic caṅkakālam, ittuṇi āṇṭukaṭku uṭpaṭṭa kālam, mēṟpaṭṭa kālam eṉa eṇṇiṭṭuk kāṇamuṭiyāta niliyil uḷḷatu.  caṅkattiṉ uṇmiyiyē aiyuṟuvōrum, aiyuṟṟuc caṅkameṉpatē iruntatilli yeṉpōrum, uṇṭeṉ pōrum, kiṟistu piṟappataṟku muṉṉeṉpōrum piṉ eṉpōrum eṉap pala tiṟattarāy ārāycciyāḷar uḷar.  maṟṟu, nam centamiḻ moḻikkaṇ paḻiya nūlkaḷ pala caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟa peyarāl vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. avvilakkiyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi nilaviya kālamē caṅka kālamām eṉṟa potu elliyi īṇṭuk kāla velliyākak koḷkiṉṟēṉ.  avvilakkiyaṅkaḷ pattuppāṭṭu, eṭṭuttoki, patiṉeṇkīḻkkaṇakku eṉa mūvakiyāy iyalukiṉṟaṉa.  ivi eḻuntu vaḷarnta ciṟanta kālam, caṅka kālam iṉṟēl, ivi caṅka ilakkiyam eṉappaṭā.
pattuppāṭṭu eṉpatu oru toki nūl. itaṉkaṇ tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi mutalākak kūttarāṟṟuppaṭi īṟākap pattup perumpāṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  ivaṟṟuḷ iyaṟkiyiṉ iṉiya kāṭci iṉitu kāṭṭap peṟukiṟatu.  akkāṭciyik kāṭṭum kaṭṭurikaḷ ilakkaṇavarampu kaṭavātu, uḷḷam koḷḷat takka aḷavil niṉṟu iṉpam curappikkum tiṟam eṇṇuntōṟum namakku iṟumpūtu tarukiṉṟatu.  itaṉāl kalviyiṉ iṉṟi yamiyāmiyi vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟārkaḷ. tamiḻmaṉṉaṉ oruvaṉ taṉ kōl niḻalil vāḻum makkaḷi nōkki,
"uṟṟuḻi yutaviyum uṟuporuḷ koṭuttum
  piṟṟinili muṉiyātu kaṟṟal naṉṟē;
  piṟappō raṉṉa uṭaṉvayiṟ ṟuḷḷum
  ciṟappiṉ pālāl tāyum maṉamtiriyum;
  oru kuṭip piṟanta pallō ruḷḷūm
  mūttōṉ varuka eṉṉātuavaruḷ
  aṟivuṭiyōṉ āṟu aracum cellum"
eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟāṉ.  oru periyavar, vāḻnāḷ mikutiyākiya vaḻiyum, tamakku nariyō tiriyō uṇṭākāmal iruntār.  atu kaṇṭa aṟiñar cilar avari nōkki, itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉi eṉa viṉaviṉar.  avarkaṭku avar, "eṉ maṉivi, makkaḷ mutal aṉivarum eṉkaruttiṟkēṟpa oḻukum iyalpuṭiyavar.  vēntaṉum allavi ceyvatilaṉ.  cāṉṟōr palar yāṉ vāḻum ūrē" eṉṟār.  eṉavē, cāṉṟōr iṉattaṉāy, kalviyaṟivu ciṟantu niṟpatu taṉakku iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉpatic caṅkakālat tamiḻmakaṉ iṉitaṟintu oḻukiya tiṟam nām kāṇkiṉṟōm.
caṅkakālat tamiḻmakaṉ, taṉ vāḻvil, uṭal vaḷarcci karutā uyir vaḷarcciyoṉṟē karuti, kalvi payiṉṟu aṟivupeṟu ārvam koṇṭāṉ eṉpavar avaṉatu iyalpi aṟiyātavarāvar.  uṭal urampeṟuvataṟkēṟṟa āḷviṉikkaṇ avaṉatu uḷḷam aḻuntiya payiṟci peṟṟiruntatu.  vāḷēntip pōruṭaṟṟic ceyvaṉa ceytal taṉ kaṭaṉ eṉpati avaṉ, tāyāl iḷamiyilēyē vaṟpuṟuttap peṟukiṉṟāṉ.  nīril nīntutal, yāṉi kutiri mutaliya ivarntu cellutal, vāṭpayiṟci, viṟpayiṟci mutaliyaṉa payiṉṟiruntāṉ.  itaṉi naṉkuṇarnta kampaṉum, irāmaṉ vāyil vittu, "matitaru taṉayarum valiyarkol' eṉṟu viṉavukiṉṟāṉ.  uṭal vaḷarccikkup paṭippayiṟciyum, aṟivu vaḷarccikkut kalviyum vēṇṭuvaṉavām eṉpatic caṅkakālat tamiḻmakaṉ naṉku aṟintiruntāṉ.  uṭal vaṉmiyāl maṉattiṭpamum viṉittiṭpamum piṟakkum eṉpatu avaṉatu uḷḷakkiṭi. vīramakaṉoruvaṉ, maṉakkiṉiya makaḷoruttiyi maṇam puṇaruṅ kālattē, aracaṉatu pōrppaṟi muḻaṅkak kēṭṭāṉ.  oruttiyi maṇantu nukarum iṉpattilum, taṉ nāṭṭiṟku uṟṟa iṭar nīkkutalāl uḷatākum iṉpam periteṉak karuti maṇattit taḷḷivittup pōrkkuc celkiṉṟāṉ.
"viḷaṅkiḻip polinta vēḷā melliyaṟ
  cuṇaṅkaṇi vaṉamuli yavaḷoṭu nāḷi
  maṇampuku vikal ākutal oṉṟō,
  ārama ruḻakkiya maṟaṅkiḷar muṉpiṉ
  nīḷili yeḵka maṟutta uṭampoṭu
  vārā ulakam pukutal oṉṟeṉap
  paṭitoṭ ṭaṉaṉē kuricil"
eṉṟu paraṇar kūṟukiṉṟār.
ivvaṇṇam kalviyaṟivum, uṭal vaṉmiyum oruṅku peṟṟut tikaḻum tamiḻ makaṉukkut tirumaṇam ceyvikkum eṇṇam  innāḷil, peṟṟōrkku amikiṉṟatu.  annāḷil aṅṅaṉam illi.  takkōṉāy maṇappatam peṟṟa makaṉ, taṉ maṉam virumpum māṇpuṭiya makaḷ oruttiyit tāṉē tērntu kāṇkiṉṟāṉ.  avaḷatu maṉavorumiyip pala neṟikaḷāl ārāykiṉṟāṉ.  kaṟpiṉ tiṇmiyik kaḷavil oḻukik kāṇkiṉṟāṉ.  piṉ taṉ kaḷavu neṟi ulakaṟiya veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟāṉ.  "ampalum alarum kaḷavu veḷippaṭuttaliṉ, aṅku ataṉ mutalvaṉ kiḻavaṉ ākum" eṉṟē āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār urikkiṉṟār.  piṉṉar, peṟṟōr maṇattiṟku iyintu maṇam puṇarvikkiṉṟaṉar.
maṉivāḻkkiyiṉ māṇpu kuṟittu avaṉ ceyyum tiṟam mikka viyapput taruvatākum.  tāṉ naṭattum vāḻkki taṉ nalamē kuṟitta vāḻvātal kūṭātu.  ītalum icipaṭa vāḻtalumē maṉivāḻkki.  taṉ vāḻvu ēṉi evvaki makkaṭkum ēmam payakkum vāḻvātal vēṇṭum.  tāṉ īṭṭiya poruḷit tāṉē nukartaliṉṟi, piṟarkkum payaṉpaṭa vāḻavēṇṭiya tamiḻmakaṉ poruḷ īṭṭal karutik kalaṅkaḷ ēṟik kaṭal kaṭakkiṉṟāṉ.  nilavariyilum nāṭiṭiyiṭṭum kāṭiṭiyiṭṭum celkiṉṟāṉ.  celpavaṉ taṉ maṉiviyiṉ pirivāṟṟāmi kaṇṭu teruṭṭu mukattāl vāḻviṉ kuṟikkōḷi naṉku vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟāṉ.  avaṉ pirintavaḻi, ataṉi avaṉ maṉiyuṟiyum makaḷir pēcikkoḷvatāl nām iṉitu aṟiyalām.
"aṟantalip piriyātu oḻukalum, ciṟanta 
 kēḷir kēṭupala vūṉṟalum, nāḷum
 varuntā uḷḷamoṭu iruntōrkku il"
eṉṟum,
"aṟaṉkaṭip paṭāa vāḻkkiyum, eṉṟum
 piṟaṉkaṭic celāa celvamum iraṇṭum
 poruḷiṉ ākum puṉiiḻi"
eṉṟum poruḷik karutit tamiḻmakaṉ vāḻntirukkiṉṟāṉ.  ivvaṇṇam tāṉum, taṉ nāṭṭu makkaḷum poruṭ kuṟipāṭiṉṟi iṉitu vāḻtal vēṇṭum; nāṭṭil vaṟumi tōṉṟiṉ,  (nāṭṭavar maṉattē nalla eṇṇaṅkaḷ vaṟṟiviṭum;) nallaṟivu māyum eṉṟu añci, avaṉ, "celvam eṉpatu cērntōr puṉkaṇ añcum meṉkaṭ celvam" eṉṟu piṟarkkeṉa vāḻtalip poruḷākap pēṇi vāḻntu vantāṉ.
tāṉ īṭṭiya poruḷit tāṉum ēṉit tamiḻmakkaḷum piṟarum peṟṟu iṉpavāḻvu naṭattavēṇṭum eṉa virumpum at tamiḻmakaṉukku vēṟoru kaṭamiyum uṇṭākiṉṟatu.  nāṭṭukkup pakivarāl uṇṭākum kēṭum accamum, vāḻkkiyiṉpattic citikkum eṉṟa eṇṇaṅ koḷkiṉṟāṉ.  ataṉāl, taṉ nāṭṭiṟ kāval kuṟittup pōr ceytaṟkum, piṟa viṉi ceytaṟkum avaṉ uḷḷam koṇṭu, atu kuṟittu viṉi ceyvatum kaṭami eṉpati uṇarkiṉṟāṉ.  tāṉ iḷamiyiṟ peṟṟa kalvi, nāṭṭiṉ nalam karutic ceyvaṉa ceytaṟkup pōtiya tuṇi ceyyāmi kaṇṭavaḻi, nirampiya kalvi peṟuvatum kaṭaṉ eṉṟu teḷikiṉṟāṉ.  oru kāl oru tamiḻmakaṉ kalvi kuṟittup pirintiruntapōtu, avaṉ pirivāṟṟātu varuntiya avaṉṟaṉ maṉimakaḷir, vāṭikkāṟṟi nōkkik kūṟuvārāy, "ē, vāṭikkāṟṟē,
tamiyōr matuki tūkkāy taṇṇeṉa,
muṉiya alitti, muraṇil kāḷi
kittoḻu marapiṉ kaṭavuṭ cāṉṟa
ceyviṉi maruṅkiṉ ceṉṟōr valvariṉ,
nī, karikālaṉōṭu vākiyeṉṉumiṭattē oṉpatu vēntar oṉṟukūṭi etirttapōtu, avaṉāl alippuṇṭu tam koṟṟakkuṭi iḻanta antap
'pīṭil maṉṉar pōla
 ōṭuvi maṉṉāl vāṭinī emakkē'
eṉkiṉṟaṉar.  iṅkē, tamiḻmakaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa kalviyi, "kitoḻu marapiṉ kaṭavuṭcāṉṟa ceyviṉi" eṉkiṉṟaṉar.  aracu muṟiyāyiṉum, vāḻkki muṟiyā yiṉum avvataṟku vēṇṭiya kalviyaṟivu ilvaḻic ciṟavātu eṉpataṟkākak,
"kāvaṟ cākāṭu ukippōṉ māṇiṉ,
  ūṟiṉ ṟāki āṟuiṉitu paṭumē;
  uyttal tēṟṟā ṉāyiṉ, vikalum
  pakikkūḻ aḷḷalpaṭṭu
  mikappal tīnōy talittalit tarumē"
eṉṟu iḷantiriyaṉ kūṟukiṉṟāṉ.
nāṭṭiṉ kāval kuṟittup pōruṭaṟṟi uyirkoṭuppatu nalviṉiyeṉṟum, avvāṟu uyirkoṭuttup pukaḻ koḷvārē ciṟantōr eṉṟum tamiḻmakaṉ karutiyiruntāṉ.  ak karutti avaṉēyaṉṟi, avaṉ makaḷirum iṉitaṟintu iruntaṉar.  pakivaruṭaṉ pōruṭaṟṟac ceṉṟa tamiḻmakaṉ oruvaṉṟaṉ maṉimakaḷir,
"nōṟṟōr maṉṟa tāmē kūṟṟam
 kōḷūṟa viḷiyār piṟarkoḷa viḷintōr eṉat
 tāḷvalam paṭuppac cēṭpulam paṭarntōr"
eṉṟu pēcik koḷkiṉṟaṉar.  inneṟiyil makaḷir vīrat tī eriya niṟkum uḷḷamuṭiyarātalic caṅkakālat tamiḻmakaḷ iyalaṟiyum neṟiyiṟ pulappaṭum.
maṉivāḻkkiyil poruḷīṭṭal, kalviyaṟivu peṟutal, viṉiceytu nāṭukāttal mutaliyaṉa  ceytoḻukum tamiḻmakaṉ makappēṟu kuṟittu makiḻkiṉṟāṉ.  makkaḷiyillāta vāḻkkiyi virumpuvatilaṉ, "makkaḷi illōrkkup payak kuṟiyilli tāmvāḻum nāḷē" eṉṟu kūṟuvaṉ.  peṟṟa makkaḷ tam kuṭiyiṉ uyarccikku uḻittal vēṇṭumeṉak karutukiṉṟāṉ.  aṉṉa makkaḷip peṟṟōri vāyāra vāḻtti makiḻvaṉ.
"eṇṇiyal muṟṟi īraṟivu purintu,
  cālpum cemmiyum uḷappaṭap piṟavum
  kāvaṟ kaminta aracutuṟi pōkiya
  vīṟucāl putalvaṟ payantaṉi"
eṉṟu pārāṭṭutalik kāṇka.
iṉi, ivaṉatu uḷḷattē kaṭavuḷ uṇarcci vīṟupeṟṟu niṟkiṉṟatu.  "pallōrum paramporuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭu; ataṉpāl parivukoṭu paraci vāḻtal ellōrum ceykaṭaṉām" eṉṟu karuti vāḻpavaṉ.  ivaṉ āṇṭavaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu vēṇṭuvaṉa mikka viyapput taruvaṉavām.  caṅka kālattē ēṉi nilattu makkaḷ vēṇṭiyaṉa vēṟu.  piṟar aḻivatiyum, piṟar ākkam keṭuvatiyum poruḷākak karuti āṇṭavaṉi vēṇṭiṉar piṟar; tamiḻaṉ,
"yām irappavi
 poruḷum poṉṉum pōkamu malla,
 aruḷum aṉpum aṟaṉum mūṉṟum"
eṉṟum
"yāmum em cuṟṟamum paravutum,
 ēma vikal peṟukayām eṉavē"
eṉṟum vēṇṭukiṉṟāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam, caṅkakālat tamiḻmakaṉ, taṉ vāḻkkiyit taṉakkēyaṉṟip piṟarkkum taṉ nāṭṭiṉ nalattukkum eṉak karutic celutti, āṇṭavaṉ vaḻipāṭṭi maṟavātu, mēṟkoṇṭu avvaḻipāṭṭilum taṉ vāḻviṉ kuṟikkōḷ ki kūṭutaṟku ākkamākum aruḷ, aṉpu, aṟam eṉṟa mūṉṟumē virumpiyirunta niliyi, iṟṟināḷil makkaḷ aṟintu nalam peṟuvārāka!

                                    “She is mine own, 
And as I rich in having such a jewel, 
As twenty seas, if all their sands were pearls,
The water nectar and the rocks pure gold”        eṉṟār
* Solution of a geometrical rider.
1.  “For nowwhere either with more quiet of more freedom from trouble does a man retire, than into his own soul particularly, when he has within him such thoughts that by looking into them he is immediately in perfect tranquility” - Marcus - Aurelius.
2.	Peace of mind must come in its own time as the waters settle themselves into clearness as well as quietness; you can no more filter your mind into purity than you can compress in into Calmness.” - Ruskin.
3.	“Self - reverecnce, Self - knowledge, Self - Control, These three alone lead life to sovereign power” Tennyson.
4.	“The greatest evils are from within us; and from our- selves also we must look for our greatest good” - Jermey Taylor.
5.	“The mind is its own place, and in itself, can make a Heaven of Hall, a Hell of Heaven” - Milton.
